# 食物与饮料

**food**
英/美 /fuːd/
n. 食物；食品
[义] Things that people and animals eat to stay alive and healthy. 维持生命与健康而摄入的东西（fast food 快餐，fresh food 新鲜食物，food waste 食物浪费）。不可数；表「一种/一份食物」用 a food 或 foods（foods 多指不同种类）。
[例] ① The restaurant is known for using local, seasonal food. 这家餐厅以使用当地时令食材闻名。 ② Store leftover food in the fridge within two hours. 剩饭菜应在两小时内放入冰箱。
[搭] fast food 快餐, fresh food 新鲜食物, food waste 食物浪费, food safety 食品安全, street food 街头小吃, comfort food 慰藉食物
[联] meal 一餐, dish 菜肴, ingredient 食材, cuisine 菜系, diet 饮食, nutrition 营养, hunger 饥饿, cook 烹饪, grocery 杂货, restaurant 餐厅
[记] 不可数；不说 × a food（表种类除外）。food 与 feed（喂养）同族但 feed 多作动词

**meal**
英/美 /miːl/
n. 一餐；一顿饭
[义] An occasion when food is eaten, or the food eaten at that time. 进食的场合或该场合所吃的食物（a big meal 丰盛的一餐，home-cooked meal 家常便饭，three meals a day 一日三餐）。可数。
[例] ① We had a long meal and talked until midnight. 我们吃了一顿很长的饭，聊到午夜。 ② Skipping meals is not a healthy way to lose weight. 跳过正餐不是健康的减肥方式。
[搭] a big meal 丰盛的一餐, home-cooked meal 家常便饭, three meals a day 一日三餐, light meal 便餐, ready meal 即食餐
[联] food 食物, breakfast 早餐, lunch 午餐, dinner 晚餐, snack 零食, dish 菜肴, restaurant 餐厅, cook 烹饪, appetite 食欲, menu 菜单
[记] 可数：a meal。make a meal of sth. 英式口语指「小题大做」。meal 与 mill（磨坊）同音不同义

**dish**
英/美 /dɪʃ/
n. 菜肴；盘碟
[义] Food prepared and cooked in a particular way; also a flat container for food. 按特定方式烹制的一道菜；亦指盛食物的盘碟（main dish 主菜，side dish 配菜，local dish 本地特色菜）。可数。
[例] ① The chef's signature dish is slow-roasted duck with plum sauce. 主厨的招牌菜是慢烤鸭配梅酱。 ② Could you pass me that dish of olives? 能把那盘橄榄递给我吗？
[搭] main dish 主菜, side dish 配菜, local dish 本地特色菜, signature dish 招牌菜, dish up 盛菜/端上
[联] meal 一餐, recipe 食谱, cuisine 菜系, plate 盘子, bowl 碗, serve 上菜, cook 烹饪, ingredient 食材, flavor 风味, menu 菜单
[记] 可数。动词 dish out 可指「分发」（dish out advice 大谈建议）。dish 与 wish 押韵

**recipe**
英/美 /ˈresəpi/
n. 食谱；配方
[义] A set of instructions for preparing a particular dish, including ingredients and steps. 制作某道菜的说明，含食材与步骤（follow a recipe 按食谱做，family recipe 家传菜谱，recipe for success 成功秘诀〔喻〕）。可数。
[例] ① She wrote down her grandmother's recipe for apple pie. 她写下了祖母的苹果派食谱。 ② There is no single recipe for raising happy children. 培养快乐孩子并没有唯一配方。
[源] 源自拉丁语 *recipere*（接收），经法语 → 原指「取用药剂的一纸说明」，后泛指烹饪配方。
[族] recipient 接收者, reception 接收
[搭] follow a recipe 按食谱做, family recipe 家传菜谱, recipe book 食谱书, secret recipe 秘方, recipe for ……的配方/秘诀
[联] ingredient 食材, cook 烹饪, dish 菜肴, bake 烘烤, measure 计量, tablespoon 汤匙, instruction 说明, cuisine 菜系, chef 厨师, formula 公式
[记] 可数：a recipe。recipe for success 等比喻用法很常见。与 receipt（收据）同源不同形

**ingredient**
英/美 /ɪnˈɡriːdiənt/
n. 食材；成分
[义] One of the foods or substances that are combined to make a particular dish. 组合成某道菜的食物或物质之一（key ingredient 关键食材，natural ingredients 天然成分，active ingredient 有效成分〔药品〕）。可数。
[例] ① Check the list of ingredients on the label before you buy. 购买前请看标签上的成分表。 ② Trust is an essential ingredient in any good relationship. 信任是任何良好关系的基本要素。
[源] 源自拉丁语 *ingredi*（进入、走入），*in-* + *gradi*（走）→ 进入（混合物）的成分。
[族] ingress 进入, grade 等级
[搭] key ingredient 关键食材, natural ingredients 天然成分, list of ingredients 成分表, active ingredient 有效成分, fresh ingredients 新鲜食材
[联] recipe 食谱, food 食物, spice 香料, flavor 风味, mix 混合, combine 组合, component 成分, element 要素, dish 菜肴, cook 烹饪
[记] 可数。比喻义「要素」很常用。ingredient 强调「构成整体的一部分」，component 更偏技术/抽象

**cuisine**
英/美 /kwɪˈziːn/
n. 烹饪；菜系
[义] A style or method of cooking associated with a particular country, region, or chef. 与特定国家、地区或厨师相关的烹饪风格或方法（French cuisine 法式料理，local cuisine 本地菜，fusion cuisine 融合菜）。不可数/可数。
[例] ① Thai cuisine is known for balancing sweet, sour, and spicy flavors. 泰国菜以甜、酸、辣平衡著称。 ② The hotel offers a range of international cuisines. 酒店提供多种国际菜系。
[源] 源自法语 *cuisine*（厨房），来自拉丁语 *coquina*（烹饪），与 cook 同族。
[族] cook 烹饪, culinary 烹饪的, kitchen 厨房
[搭] French cuisine 法式料理, local cuisine 本地菜, fusion cuisine 融合菜, traditional cuisine 传统菜肴, fine cuisine 精致料理
[联] dish 菜肴, recipe 食谱, chef 厨师, flavor 风味, restaurant 餐厅, cook 烹饪, food 食物, menu 菜单, spice 香料, gourmet 美食家
[记] 不可数/可数。比 food 更强调「烹饪体系与风格」。fine cuisine 略正式

**menu**
英/美 /ˈmenjuː/
n. 菜单
[义] A list of dishes available in a restaurant, or a plan of meals for a particular day. 餐厅可供选择的菜肴列表，或某日餐饮计划（set menu 套餐，à la carte menu 单点菜单，children's menu 儿童餐单）。可数。
[例] ① The waiter handed us the menu and took our drink orders. 服务员递上菜单，并记下我们的饮料点单。 ② The school publishes its lunch menu online each week. 学校每周在网上公布午餐菜单。
[源] 源自法语 *menu*（小的、详细的），原指「详细清单」，后专指餐单。
[搭] set menu 套餐, à la carte menu 单点菜单, children's menu 儿童餐单, menu item 菜单项目, daily menu 当日菜单
[联] dish 菜肴, order 点餐, restaurant 餐厅, meal 一餐, course 一道菜, appetizer 开胃菜, dessert 甜点, waiter 服务员, price 价格, choice 选择
[记] 可数：a menu。on the menu 亦可喻指「在选项之中」（A pay rise is not on the menu 加薪不在考虑之列）

**breakfast**
英/美 /ˈbrekfəst/
n. 早餐
v. 吃早餐
[义] The first meal of the day, usually eaten in the morning. 一天中的第一餐，通常在早晨（continental breakfast 欧陆式早餐，full English breakfast 全英式早餐，skip breakfast 不吃早餐）。可数/不可数。
[例] ① I usually have toast and coffee for breakfast. 我早餐通常吃吐司配咖啡。 ② The hotel serves breakfast from seven until ten. 酒店七点到十点供应早餐。
[源] 古英语 *break*（打破）+ *fast*（禁食）→ 打破夜间的禁食。
[搭] continental breakfast 欧陆式早餐, full English breakfast 全英式早餐, skip breakfast 不吃早餐, breakfast cereal 早餐麦片, bed and breakfast 民宿
[联] lunch 午餐, dinner 晚餐, meal 一餐, morning 早晨, coffee 咖啡, toast 吐司, egg 鸡蛋, cereal 麦片, brunch 早午餐, hungry 饥饿的
[记] 可数/不可数。have/eat breakfast 比 take breakfast 更常见。动词 breakfast 很书面，日常少用

**lunch**
英/美 /lʌntʃ/
n. 午餐
v. 吃午餐
[义] A meal eaten in the middle of the day. 中午吃的一餐（packed lunch 自带午餐，business lunch 商务午餐，out for lunch 出去吃午饭）。可数/不可数。
[例] ① Let's meet for lunch at the café on Main Street. 咱们在主街那家咖啡馆吃午饭吧。 ② She brought a packed lunch to work to save money. 她自带午餐上班以省钱。
[搭] packed lunch 自带午餐, business lunch 商务午餐, lunch break 午休, school lunch 学校午餐, out for lunch 出去吃午饭
[联] breakfast 早餐, dinner 晚餐, meal 一餐, sandwich 三明治, noon 中午, restaurant 餐厅, snack 零食, work 工作, colleague 同事, buffet 自助餐
[记] 可数/不可数。have lunch 最自然。luncheon 更正式（business luncheon 商务午宴）

**dinner**
英/美 /ˈdɪnə(r)/
n. 晚餐；正餐
[义] The main meal of the day, eaten in the evening in many countries; in some regions, the midday meal. 许多国家指傍晚的主餐；部分地区亦指中午正餐（family dinner 家庭晚餐，Christmas dinner 圣诞大餐，TV dinner 电视晚餐）。可数/不可数。
[例] ① We invited the neighbours over for dinner on Saturday. 我们周六请邻居来家吃晚饭。 ② What's for dinner tonight? 今晚晚饭吃什么？
[搭] family dinner 家庭晚餐, Christmas dinner 圣诞大餐, dinner party 晚宴, cook dinner 做晚饭, out to dinner 外出就餐
[联] lunch 午餐, breakfast 早餐, meal 一餐, supper 晚饭, dish 菜肴, cook 烹饪, restaurant 餐厅, guest 客人, evening 傍晚, feast 盛宴
[记] 英美用法有差异：英式 dinner 常指傍晚主餐；美式有时 dinner 指较正式的晚餐，supper 更家常。have dinner 最常用

**snack**
英/美 /snæk/
n. 零食；小吃
v. 吃零食
[义] A small amount of food eaten between meals. 两餐之间吃的一小份食物（healthy snack 健康零食，snack bar 小吃部，midnight snack 夜宵）。可数。
[例] ① She grabbed a quick snack before the meeting. 开会前她匆匆吃了点零食。 ② The kids snack on fruit and nuts after school. 孩子们放学后吃水果和坚果当点心。
[搭] healthy snack 健康零食, snack bar 小吃部, midnight snack 夜宵, snack on 吃……当零食, light snack 轻食
[联] meal 一餐, lunch 午餐, hunger 饥饿, candy 糖果, cookie 曲奇, chip 薯片, appetite 食欲, bite 一口, treat 款待/小食, munch 咀嚼
[记] 可数：a snack。动词 snack on sth. 很口语。snack 比 treat 更中性，treat 带「犒赏」意味

**dessert**
英/美 /dɪˈzɜːt/
n. 甜点；餐后甜品
[义] Sweet food eaten at the end of a meal. 一餐末尾吃的甜食（chocolate dessert 巧克力甜点，dessert menu 甜品单，for dessert 作为甜点）。可数/不可数。
[例] ① We ordered ice cream for dessert. 我们点了冰淇淋当甜点。 ② The dessert wine paired well with the cheese. 这款甜白葡萄酒与奶酪很搭。
[搭] for dessert 作为甜点, dessert menu 甜品单, chocolate dessert 巧克力甜点, dessert wine 甜酒, skip dessert 不吃甜点
[联] sweet 甜的, cake 蛋糕, ice cream 冰淇淋, pudding 布丁, fruit 水果, course 一道菜, dinner 晚餐, sugar 糖, treat 小食, pastry 糕点
[记] 不可数/可数。只有一个 s：dessert（甜点），不是 × desert（沙漠）。stressed on the second syllable

**appetite**
英/美 /ˈæpɪtaɪt/
n. 食欲；胃口
[义] A desire for food; also a strong desire for something non-food. 对食物的欲望；亦指对非食物事物的强烈渴望（good appetite 胃口好，lose one's appetite 没胃口，appetite for risk 冒险欲〔喻〕）。可数/不可数。
[例] ① The walk gave us an appetite for a big lunch. 散步让我们胃口大开，想吃顿丰盛的午餐。 ② Young readers showed an appetite for longer fantasy novels. 年轻读者对长篇奇幻小说表现出浓厚兴趣。
[源] 源自拉丁语 *appetitus*（渴望），*ad-* + *petere*（寻求）→ 趋向食物的欲望。
[族] appetizing 开胃的, compete 竞争（同 petere 族）
[搭] good appetite 胃口好, lose one's appetite 没胃口, healthy appetite 食欲旺盛, appetite for 对……的渴望, whet one's appetite 勾起食欲/兴趣
[联] hungry 饥饿的, hunger 饥饿, meal 一餐, food 食物, taste 品尝, crave 渴望, snack 零食, feast 盛宴, full 饱的, starve 挨饿
[记] 可数/不可数。work up an appetite 指「活动后饿了」。appetizer（开胃菜）同族

**hungry**
英/美 /ˈhʌŋɡri/
adj. 饥饿的；渴望的
[义] Feeling a need or desire to eat; also strongly wanting something. 感到需要或想要进食；亦指强烈渴望某事物（go hungry 挨饿，hungry for success 渴望成功）。比较级 hungrier, hungriest。
[例] ① The children were hungry after swimming all afternoon. 孩子们游了一下午泳，都饿了。 ② Investors remain hungry for new tech stocks. 投资者对新兴科技股依然渴求。
[搭] go hungry 挨饿, hungry for 渴望, feel hungry 感到饿, hungry look 饥饿的样子/渴求的眼神, starving hungry 饿极了（口语）
[联] hunger 饥饿, thirsty 口渴的, appetite 食欲, eat 吃, meal 一餐, snack 零食, food 食物, starve 挨饿, full 饱的, famine 饥荒
[记] 表语/定语均可。I'm starving 口语指「饿坏了」，比 hungry 夸张。hungry 与 angry 押韵，便于记忆

**thirsty**
英/美 /ˈθɜːsti/
adj. 口渴的；渴望的
[义] Feeling a need to drink; also having a strong desire for something. 感到需要喝水；亦指强烈渴望（thirsty work 让人口渴的活，thirsty for knowledge 渴求知识）。比较级 thirstier, thirstiest。
[例] ① Running in the heat made us very thirsty. 大太阳底下跑步让我们非常口渴。 ② The campaign was thirsty for fresh ideas. 这场运动迫切渴望新点子。
[搭] feel thirsty 感到口渴, thirsty work 让人口渴的活, thirsty for 渴望, die of thirst 渴死
[联] thirst 口渴, drink 喝, water 水, juice 果汁, dry 干的, hot 热的, hungry 饥饿的, beer 啤酒, dehydration 脱水, quench 解渴
[记] 表语/定语均可。thirsty 亦可形容「干旱的」（thirsty soil 干渴的土地）。th 发音 /θ/，勿读成 /s/

**bread**
英/美 /bred/
n. 面包
[义] A basic food made from flour, water, and yeast, baked in an oven. 用面粉、水和酵母烤制的基本食物（a loaf of bread 一条面包，white bread 白面包，bread and butter 涂黄油的面包/生计〔喻〕）。不可数/可数。
[例] ① She sliced the bread and made sandwiches for everyone. 她把面包切片，给每人做了三明治。 ② Fresh bread smells wonderful when it comes out of the oven. 刚出炉的面包闻起来特别香。
[搭] a loaf of bread 一条面包, white bread 白面包, wholemeal bread 全麦面包, bread and butter 黄油面包/生计, bread roll 小圆面包
[联] flour 面粉, bake 烘烤, toast 吐司, sandwich 三明治, butter 黄油, yeast 酵母, grain 谷物, slice 切片, bakery 面包店, crust 面包皮
[记] 不可数/可数。a slice of bread 一片。breadwinner（养家糊口的人）同族比喻义。与 bred（breed 过去式）同音

**rice**
英/美 /raɪs/
n. 大米；米饭
[义] Small white or brown grains from a plant, cooked and eaten as food. 植物的小粒白色或棕色谷粒，煮熟作食物（a bowl of rice 一碗米饭，fried rice 炒饭，brown rice 糙米）。不可数。
[例] ① We usually serve curry with rice. 我们通常用咖喱配米饭。 ② Brown rice takes longer to cook than white rice. 糙米比白米更费火。
[搭] a bowl of rice 一碗米饭, fried rice 炒饭, brown rice 糙米, rice cooker 电饭煲, sticky rice 糯米
[联] grain 谷物, noodle 面条, pasta 意面, bowl 碗, cook 烹饪, stir-fry 炒, curry 咖喱, staple 主食, harvest 收获, field 田地
[记] 不可数。一种 rice 不说 × a rice。rice 作作物/谷物不可数；a grain of rice 一粒米

**pasta**
英/美 /ˈpæstə/
n. 意大利面；面食
[义] An Italian food made from flour and water or eggs, formed into shapes such as spaghetti or penne. 用面粉加水或鸡蛋制成的意大利食物，呈 spaghetti、penne 等形状（fresh pasta 鲜面，pasta sauce 意面酱，cook pasta 煮意面）。不可数/可数。
[例] ① Boil the pasta until it is al dente. 把意面煮到弹牙程度。 ② They served three different pastas at the buffet. 自助餐上提供了三种不同的意面。
[源] 源自意大利语 *pasta*（面团），来自拉丁语 *paste*（糊状物），与 paste 同族。
[族] paste 糊状物, pastry 油酥点心
[搭] fresh pasta 鲜面, pasta sauce 意面酱, cook pasta 煮意面, pasta dish 意面料理, whole-wheat pasta 全麦意面
[联] noodle 面条, spaghetti 意大利细面, sauce 酱汁, Italian 意大利的, boil 煮, flour 面粉, recipe 食谱, dinner 晚餐, parmesan 帕玛森, olive oil 橄榄油
[记] 不可数/可数。单数 pasta 指这类食物；复数 pastas 指不同种类。与 paste（浆糊）同源

**noodle**
英/美 /ˈnuːdl/
n. 面条
[义] A long, thin strip of food made from flour, water, and sometimes egg, used especially in Asian cooking. 用面粉、水及有时加鸡蛋制成的细长条食物，尤用于亚洲烹饪（instant noodles 方便面，rice noodles 米粉，noodle soup 汤面）。可数，常用复数 noodles。
[例] ① She ordered a bowl of chicken noodle soup. 她点了一碗鸡肉汤面。 ② Add the noodles to the boiling water for three minutes. 把面条放进沸水里煮三分钟。
[搭] instant noodles 方便面, rice noodles 米粉, noodle soup 汤面, stir-fried noodles 炒面, egg noodles 鸡蛋面
[联] pasta 意面, rice 米饭, soup 汤, bowl 碗, chopstick 筷子, Asian 亚洲的, boil 煮, strand 条, slurp 吸溜, broth 肉汤
[记] 常用复数 noodles。instant noodles 固定说法。与 noodle「笨蛋」（美式俚语）同形不同义

**sandwich**
英/美 /ˈsænwɪtʃ/
n. 三明治
v. 夹入；挤在……之间
[义] Two slices of bread with meat, cheese, or other filling between them. 两片面包中间夹肉、奶酪或其他馅料（chicken sandwich 鸡肉三明治，make a sandwich 做三明治）。可数。动词指「夹在中间」。
[例] ① He ate a ham sandwich at his desk. 他在办公桌前吃了一个火腿三明治。 ② Our cottage was sandwiched between two tall apartment blocks. 我们的小屋夹在两座高层公寓之间。
[搭] chicken sandwich 鸡肉三明治, make a sandwich 做三明治, sandwich bar 三明治店, club sandwich 公司三明治, sandwich between 夹在……之间
[联] bread 面包, lunch 午餐, filling 馅料, toast 吐司, snack 零食, packed lunch 自带午餐, cheese 奶酪, ham 火腿, bite 一口, picnic 野餐
[记] 可数：a sandwich。来自 Earl of Sandwich 传说。动词 be sandwiched between 很常用

**wrap**
英/美 /ræp/
n. 卷饼；卷饼卷
v. 包裹
[义] A thin flat bread rolled around a filling of meat, salad, or cheese; to cover something by folding paper or cloth around it. 薄扁面饼卷入肉、沙拉或奶酪等馅料；亦指包裹。可数/动词。
[例] ① She ordered a chicken wrap with extra salad. 她点了一份加量沙拉的鸡肉卷饼。 ② Wrap the leftovers tightly before putting them in the fridge. 剩菜放进冰箱前包紧。
[搭] chicken wrap 鸡肉卷, veggie wrap 素食卷, wrap in foil 用箔纸包, lunch wrap 午餐卷饼, tortilla wrap 玉米卷饼
[联] sandwich 三明治, tortilla 玉米饼, lunch 午餐, filling 馅料, salad 沙拉, bread 面包, roll 卷, snack 零食, portable 便携的, cafe 咖啡馆
[记] 可数：a wrap。wrap 动词极常用（wrap a gift 包礼物）。与 rap（说唱）同音不同义

**meat**
英/美 /miːt/
n. 肉；肉类
[义] The flesh of animals used as food. 用作食物的动物肉（red meat 红肉，white meat 白肉，eat less meat 少吃肉）。不可数。
[例] ① The recipe calls for 500 grams of lean meat. 食谱要求 500 克瘦肉。 ② Many people are cutting down on meat for health reasons. 许多人出于健康原因减少吃肉。
[搭] red meat 红肉, white meat 白肉, lean meat 瘦肉, minced meat 肉末, meat eater 肉食者
[联] beef 牛肉, pork 猪肉, chicken 鸡肉, fish 鱼肉, protein 蛋白质, vegetarian 素食者, butcher 屠夫, grill 烧烤, cook 烹饪, steak 牛排
[记] 不可数。表「一种肉」用 a type of meat。meat 与 meet（遇见）同音异义

**beef**
英/美 /biːf/
n. 牛肉
v. 抱怨（口语）
[义] Meat from a cow. 牛的肉（ground beef 牛肉馅，roast beef 烤牛肉，beef stew 炖牛肉）。不可数。动词 beef 口语指「抱怨」。
[例] ① She bought ground beef to make burgers. 她买了牛肉馅做汉堡。 ② Some residents beef about noise from the new bar. 一些居民抱怨新酒吧太吵。
[搭] ground beef 牛肉馅, roast beef 烤牛肉, beef stew 炖牛肉, beef cattle 肉牛, beef up 加强/充实

[记] 不可数。beef about 抱怨；beef up 加强（beef up security 加强安保）。与 leaf 复数 leaves 不同，beef 无变化

**pork**
英/美 /pɔːk/
n. 猪肉
[义] Meat from a pig. 猪的肉（pork chop 猪排，pork belly 五花肉，pork sausage 猪肉香肠）。不可数。
[例] ① The menu features slow-cooked pork with apple sauce. 菜单上有慢炖猪肉配苹果酱。 ② Muslims and Jews traditionally avoid pork. 穆斯林和犹太传统上不吃猪肉。

**chicken**
英/美 /ˈtʃɪkɪn/
n. 鸡肉；鸡
[义] A bird kept for its meat and eggs; the meat of this bird. 为肉和蛋而饲养的禽类；其肉（fried chicken 炸鸡，roast chicken 烤鸡，chicken soup 鸡汤）。可数/不可数。
[例] ① We're having roast chicken for Sunday lunch. 我们周日午餐吃烤鸡。 ② The farmer keeps chickens in the back yard. 农夫在后院养鸡。

[记] 作「鸡」可数；作「鸡肉」不可数。play chicken 指「比胆量飙车/对峙」。chicken out 临阵退缩

**egg**
英/美 /eɡ/
n. 蛋；鸡蛋
[义] An oval object laid by a bird, especially a chicken, eaten as food. 鸟产下的卵形物，尤指鸡蛋，可食用（fried egg 煎蛋，boiled egg 煮蛋，egg yolk 蛋黄）。可数。
[例] ① She cracked an egg into the frying pan. 她往平底锅里打了一个鸡蛋。 ② The recipe needs three eggs and a cup of flour. 这食谱需要三个鸡蛋和一杯面粉。

**fish**
英/美 /fɪʃ/
n. 鱼肉；鱼
v. 钓鱼
[义] As food: the flesh of fish eaten as a meal. 作食物：作为餐食的鱼肉（grilled fish 烤鱼，fish and chips 炸鱼薯条）。作动物物种、生态时主归属第三章；本章取饮食义。可数/不可数。
[例] ① We had grilled fish with lemon and herbs. 我们吃了配柠檬和香草的烤鱼。 ② Fresh fish should smell like the sea, not strong or sour. 鲜鱼应带有海的味道，不应腥臭。

**fruit**
英/美 /fruːt/
n. 水果
[义] The sweet, fleshy product of a plant that contains seeds and can be eaten. 植物含种子的甜美多肉产物，可食用（fresh fruit 新鲜水果，fruit juice 果汁，dried fruit 果干）。可数/不可数。
[例] ① Eat plenty of fruit and vegetables every day. 每天多吃水果和蔬菜。 ② Tropical fruit is cheaper in summer at this market. 这个市场夏天热带水果更便宜。

**vegetable**
英/美 /ˈvedʒtəbl/
n. 蔬菜
[义] A plant or part of a plant used as food, such as carrots, broccoli, or spinach. 用作食物的植物或其部分，如胡萝卜、西兰花、菠菜（fresh vegetables 新鲜蔬菜，leafy vegetables 叶菜，frozen vegetables 冷冻蔬菜）。可数/不可数。
[例] ① Stir-fry the vegetables for five minutes until tender. 把蔬菜翻炒五分钟至变软。 ② Children often refuse green vegetables at first. 孩子起初常拒绝绿叶蔬菜。

[记] 可数/不可数。vegetable 俚语可侮辱指「植物人/呆板的人」，慎用。与 fruit 并列极常见

**salad**
英/美 /ˈsæləd/
n. 沙拉；色拉
[义] A mixture of raw or cooked vegetables, often with dressing; sometimes including meat, cheese, or fruit. 生或熟蔬菜的混合，常加调味酱；有时含肉、奶酪或水果（green salad 蔬菜沙拉，fruit salad 水果沙拉，salad dressing 沙拉酱）。可数/不可数。
[例] ① I'll have the grilled chicken with a side salad. 我要烤鸡配一份 side salad。 ② She tossed the salad with olive oil and vinegar. 她用橄榄油和醋拌沙拉。
[源] 源自拉丁语 *sal*（盐）→ 原指「加盐的凉拌菜」。

**potato**
英/美 /pəˈteɪtəʊ/
n. 土豆；马铃薯
（复数 potatoes /pəˈteɪtəʊz/）
[义] A round vegetable with brown or red skin and white flesh, eaten cooked in many ways. 一种圆形蔬菜，皮呈 brown 或 red，果肉白色，可多种烹饪（mashed potato 土豆泥，baked potato 烤土豆，sweet potato 红薯）。可数。
[例] ① Peel the potatoes and cut them into small cubes. 把土豆去皮，切成小丁。 ② Fish and chips is often served with mushy peas and a potato. 炸鱼薯条常配豌豆泥和土豆。
[搭] mashed potato 土豆泥, baked potato 烤土豆, sweet potato 红薯, potato chips 薯片（美式）, couch potato 电视迷（俚语）
[联] vegetable 蔬菜, starch 淀粉, fry 炸, roast 烤, peel 削皮, crop 作物, farm 农场, tomato 番茄, onion 洋葱, carbohydrate 碳水化合物
[记] 可数；复数 potatoes。hot potato 喻「烫手山芋（棘手问题）」。couch potato 指「懒人电视迷」

**tomato**

[例] ① Slice the tomatoes and add them to the salad. 把番茄切片放进沙拉。 ② The pasta sauce is made from fresh tomatoes and basil. 意面酱用新鲜番茄和罗勒做成。

[联] vegetable 蔬菜, fruit 水果, salad 沙拉, sauce 酱汁, pasta 意面, red 红色的, ripe 成熟的, ketchup 番茄酱, basil 罗勒, onion 洋葱
[记] 可数；复数 tomatoes。botanically 是 fruit，culinarily 当 vegetable。与 potato 一样来自美洲作物

**onion**

n. 洋葱

**apple**

n. 苹果

n. 牛奶
v. 挤奶；榨取
[义] The white liquid produced by cows, goats, or other mammals, drunk as a drink or used in cooking. 牛、羊等哺乳动物产出的白色液体，可饮或用于烹饪（skimmed milk 脱脂奶，milk tea 奶茶，plant-based milk 植物奶）。不可数。

[联] dairy 乳制品, cheese 奶酪, butter 黄油, cream 奶油, cow 牛, coffee 咖啡, cereal 麦片, calcium 钙, lactose 乳糖, yogurt 酸奶
[记] 不可数。milk 作动词指「挤奶」或喻「榨取」（milk the system 钻制度空子）。don't cry over spilt milk 常用谚语

[搭] Greek yogurt 希腊酸奶, plain yogurt 原味酸奶, fruit yogurt 果味酸奶, yogurt drink 酸奶饮料, frozen yogurt 冻酸奶
[联] milk 牛奶, dairy 乳制品, bacteria 细菌, probiotic 益生菌, breakfast 早餐, honey 蜂蜜, fruit 水果, snack 零食, creamy 乳脂状的, sour 酸的
[记] 亦拼 yoghurt（英式）。不可数/可数。Greek yogurt 更浓稠、蛋白质更高

**water**
英/美 /ˈwɔːtə(r)/
n. 水
v. 浇水；流口水
[义] The clear liquid that falls as rain and is used for drinking, cooking, and washing. 雨落下的清澈液体，用于饮用、烹饪和洗涤（drinking water 饮用水，sparkling water 气泡水，boil water 烧水）。不可数。
[例] ① Could I have a glass of water, please? 请给我一杯水好吗？ ② Boil the water before adding the pasta. 水开后再下意面。
[搭] drinking water 饮用水, sparkling water 气泡水, boil water 烧水, tap water 自来水, water bottle 水瓶
[联] drink 喝, thirsty 口渴的, juice 果汁, tea 茶, coffee 咖啡, liquid 液体, boil 煮沸, ice 冰, mineral 矿物质, hydrate 补水
[记] 不可数。waters 可指「水域」（international waters 公海）。water down 喻「稀释/削弱」

**coffee**
英/美 /ˈkɒfi/ 美 /ˈkɔːfi/
n. 咖啡
[义] A hot drink made from roasted coffee beans; also the beans themselves. 用 roasted 咖啡豆制成的热饮；亦指咖啡豆本身（a cup of coffee 一杯咖啡，black coffee 黑咖啡，coffee shop 咖啡馆）。不可数/可数。
[例] ① I need a coffee before I can focus in the morning. 我早上得喝杯咖啡才能集中精神。 ② They met at a coffee shop to discuss the project. 他们在咖啡馆见面讨论项目。
[源] 源自阿拉伯语 *qahwa*，经土耳其语、意大利语传入欧洲。
[搭] a cup of coffee 一杯咖啡, black coffee 黑咖啡, coffee shop 咖啡馆, instant coffee 速溶咖啡, coffee break 工间咖啡休息
[联] tea 茶, caffeine 咖啡因, espresso 浓缩咖啡, latte 拿铁, breakfast 早餐, mug 马克杯, brew 冲泡, bean 豆, milk 牛奶, sugar 糖
[记] 不可数/可数。coffee 作可数指「一杯咖啡」。decaf 指 decaffeinated 脱咖啡因

**tea**
英/美 /tiː/
n. 茶；茶叶
[义] A hot drink made by pouring boiling water on dried tea leaves; also the leaves themselves. 用沸水冲泡干茶叶制成的热饮；亦指茶叶（a cup of tea 一杯茶，green tea 绿茶， afternoon tea 下午茶）。不可数/可数。
[例] ① Would you like tea or coffee? 你要茶还是咖啡？ ② She brewed a pot of tea for her guests. 她为客人泡了一壶茶。
[搭] a cup of tea 一杯茶, green tea 绿茶, afternoon tea 下午茶, iced tea 冰茶, not my cup of tea 不是我喜欢的（习语）
[联] coffee 咖啡, water 水, cup 杯子, pot 壶, brew 冲泡, leaf 叶子, caffeine 咖啡因, biscuit 饼干（英式配茶）, herbal 花草的, kettle 水壶
[记] 不可数/可数。not my cup of tea 指「不对我的胃口/不是我那款」。tea 在英式亦指傍晚便餐（tea time）

**juice**
英/美 /dʒuːs/
n. 果汁；汁
[义] The liquid that comes from fruit or vegetables, or a drink made from it. 水果或蔬菜的液体，或以其制成的饮料（orange juice 橙汁，fresh juice 鲜榨果汁，lemon juice 柠檬汁）。不可数/可数。
[例] ① The kids drank apple juice with their lunch. 孩子们午餐喝苹果汁。 ② Squeeze a little lemon juice over the fish. 在鱼上挤一点柠檬汁。
[搭] orange juice 橙汁, fresh juice 鲜榨果汁, lemon juice 柠檬汁, fruit juice 果汁, vegetable juice 蔬菜汁
[联] fruit 水果, water 水, drink 喝, squeeze 挤压, sweet 甜的, glass 玻璃杯, breakfast 早餐, vitamin 维生素, thirst 口渴, soda 汽水
[记] 不可数/可数。juice 俚语可指「精力/电」（get some juice 充电）。stew in one's own juice 自作自受

**cook**
英/美 /kʊk/
v. 烹饪；做饭
n. 厨师
[义] To prepare food by heating it; a person whose job is to prepare food. 通过加热准备食物；以做饭为职业的人（cook dinner 做晚饭，home cooking 家常菜，cook from scratch 从零开始做饭）。规则动词。
[形] cooked / cooked / cooking；复数 cooks
[例] ① He cooks dinner for the family every evening. 他每晚给全家做晚饭。 ② The restaurant hired a new cook last month. 餐厅上个月雇了新厨师。
[源] 源自拉丁语 *coquere*（烹饪），经法语 → 与 cuisine, kitchen 同族。
[族] cuisine 菜系, culinary 烹饪的, kitchen 厨房, cookie 曲奇（小蛋糕）
[搭] cook dinner 做晚饭, home cooking 家常菜, cook from scratch 从零开始做饭, cook up 编造/策划, too many cooks 人多坏事（谚语）
[联] bake 烘烤, fry 油炸, boil 煮, chef 厨师, recipe 食谱, kitchen 厨房, meal 一餐, ingredient 食材, heat 加热, serve 上菜
[记] 规则变化。cook the books 指「做假账」，与做饭无关。cook 比 chef 更泛指，chef 更偏专业主厨

**bake**
英/美 /beɪk/
v. 烘烤；烘焙
[义] To cook food using dry heat in an oven, without direct exposure to a flame. 在烤箱中用干热烹饪，不直接接触明火（bake a cake 烤蛋糕，bake bread 烤面包，baking tray 烤盘）。规则动词。
[形] baked / baked / baking
[例] ① She baked a chocolate cake for his birthday. 她为他生日烤了巧克力蛋糕。 ② Bake the potatoes at 200°C for forty minutes. 把土豆以 200°C 烤四十分钟。
[源] 源自古英语 *bacan*（烘烤），日耳曼语源。
[搭] bake a cake 烤蛋糕, bake bread 烤面包, baking tray 烤盘, baked goods 烘焙食品, bake at 180°C 以180度烘烤
[联] cook 烹饪, oven 烤箱, bread 面包, cake 蛋糕, pastry 糕点, roast 烤, fry 炸, flour 面粉, dough 面团, golden 金黄的
[记] 规则变化。baking hot 形容「酷热」。half-baked 喻「考虑不周的」

**fry**
英/美 /fraɪ/
v. 油炸；油煎
n. 油炸食品
[义] To cook food in hot fat or oil. 在热油或脂肪中烹饪（fry an egg 煎蛋，deep-fry 油炸，stir-fry 翻炒）。规则动词。
[形] fried / fried / frying
[例] ① Fry the onions in a little oil until soft. 用少量油把洋葱煎软。 ② The stall sells fried chicken and chips. 这摊位卖炸鸡和薯条。
[搭] fry an egg 煎蛋, deep-fry 油炸, stir-fry 翻炒, frying pan 煎锅, small fry 小人物/杂鱼
[联] cook 烹饪, oil 油, pan 平底锅, crisp 脆的, golden 金黄的, boil 煮, roast 烤, grill 烧烤, fat 脂肪, sizzle 滋滋声
[记] 规则变化。small fry 指「小角色/不重要的」。out of the frying pan into the fire 愈陷愈糟

**boil**
英/美 /bɔɪl/
v. 煮沸；煮
n. 沸点；疖子
[义] To heat a liquid until bubbles rise to the surface; to cook food in boiling water. 加热液体至冒泡；在沸水中煮食物（boil water 烧水，boil an egg 煮蛋，bring to the boil 煮沸）。规则动词。
[形] boiled / boiled / boiling
[例] ① Boil the kettle and make us some tea. 烧壶水给我们泡茶。 ② Boil the pasta for eight minutes, then drain it. 意面煮八分钟，然后沥干。
[搭] boil water 烧水, boil an egg 煮蛋, bring to the boil 煮沸, boiling point 沸点, boil down to 归结为
[联] cook 烹饪, water 水, steam 蒸, simmer 小火炖, hot 热的, kettle 水壶, pot 锅, heat 加热, bubble 泡, soup 汤
[记] 规则变化。boil down to 喻「归根结底是」。boil 作名词可指皮肤「疖子」，与烹饪义同形

**roast**
英/美 /rəʊst/
v. 烤；烘
n. 烤肉； roast
adj. 烤的
[义] To cook food, especially meat, in an oven or over a fire. 在烤箱或火上烹饪食物，尤指肉类（roast chicken 烤鸡，Sunday roast 周日烤肉大餐，roast vegetables 烤蔬菜）。规则动词。
[形] roasted / roasted / roasting
[例] ① We're roasting a joint of beef for Sunday lunch. 我们周日午餐烤一块牛肉。 ② Roast the nuts in the oven for ten minutes. 把坚果在烤箱里烤十分钟。
[搭] roast chicken 烤鸡, Sunday roast 周日烤肉大餐, roast vegetables 烤蔬菜, roast beef 烤牛肉, roasting tin 烤盘
[联] bake 烘烤, grill 烧烤, cook 烹饪, meat 肉, chicken 鸡肉, oven 烤箱, crisp 脆的, golden 金黄的, dinner 晚餐, potato 土豆
[记] 规则变化。roast 亦可指「吐槽式幽默颂词」（roast someone at a comedy event）。giving someone a roasting 英式指「痛骂」

**grill**
英/美 /ɡrɪl/
v. 烧烤；盘问
n. 烤架；烤鱼
[义] To cook food on a metal frame over direct heat; also to question intensely. 在金属架子上用直火烤；亦指严厉盘问（grill fish 烤鱼，barbecue grill 烧烤架，grill sb. about 盘问某人）。规则动词。
[形] grilled / grilled / grilling
[例] ① Grill the salmon for five minutes on each side. 三文鱼每面烤五分钟。 ② The reporter grilled the minister about the scandal. 记者就丑闻严厉追问部长。
[搭] grill fish 烤鱼, barbecue grill 烧烤架, grill sb. about 盘问某人, grilled cheese 烤奶酪三明治, under the grill 在烤架下
[联] roast 烤, fry 炸, barbecue 烧烤, cook 烹饪, heat 加热, flame 火焰, fish 鱼肉, steak 牛排, char 焦痕, smoke 烟
[记] 规则变化。grill 作动词「盘问」很常用。grilled 指「烤的」；grilling 名词可指「严厉询问」

**sweet**
英/美 /swiːt/
adj. 甜的；可爱的
n. 糖果；甜点（英式）
[义] Having a taste like sugar or honey. 具有糖或蜂蜜般的味道（sweet taste 甜味，sweet tooth 爱吃甜食）。英式名词 sweets 指「糖果」。比较级 sweeter, sweetest。
[例] ① The mango was perfectly ripe and sweet. 这芒果熟度刚好，很甜。 ② She doesn't eat sweets because they hurt her teeth. 她不吃糖果，因为伤牙。
[搭] sweet taste 甜味, sweet tooth 爱吃甜食, sweet potato 红薯, sweet and sour 酸甜的, sweet talk 甜言蜜语
[联] sour 酸的, bitter 苦的, salty 咸的, sugar 糖, honey 蜂蜜, dessert 甜点, candy 糖果, flavor 风味, fruit 水果, delicious 美味的
[记] 比较级 sweeter。sweet 可形容人「可爱/贴心」，与 taste 无关。sweet tooth 指「嗜甜」

**sour**
英/美 /saʊə(r)/
adj. 酸的；变质的
v. 变酸；变坏
[义] Having a sharp taste like lemon or vinegar; also spoiled or unpleasant. 具有柠檬或醋般的 sharp 味道；亦指变质或令人不快的（sour cream 酸奶油，sour grapes 酸葡萄〔喻〕，turn sour 变酸/变坏）。比较级 sourer, sourest。
[例] ① The milk has gone sour—throw it away. 牛奶酸了，倒掉吧。 ② Lemons taste sour but work well in cooking. 柠檬很酸，但做菜很合适。
[搭] sour cream 酸奶油, sour grapes 酸葡萄（喻）, turn sour 变酸/变坏, sour taste 酸味, sweet and sour 酸甜的
[联] sweet 甜的, bitter 苦的, salty 咸的, lemon 柠檬, vinegar 醋, yogurt 酸奶, spoil 变质, taste 味道, milk 牛奶, ferment 发酵
[记] 比较级 sourer。sour grapes 出自伊索寓言，指「吃不到说葡萄酸」。relations turn sour 关系恶化

**bitter**
英/美 /ˈbɪtə(r)/
adj. 苦的；痛苦的
n. 苦啤酒（英式）
[义] Having a sharp, unpleasant taste that is not sweet or salty; also feeling angry and hurt. 具有不甜不咸的尖锐刺激味；亦指愤懑痛苦（bitter chocolate 黑巧，bitter taste 苦味，bitter argument 激烈争吵）。比较级 bitterer, bitterest。
[例] ① Dark coffee tastes bitter without sugar. 黑咖啡不加糖很苦。 ② They had a bitter dispute over money after the divorce. 离婚后他们为钱激烈争执。
[搭] bitter chocolate 黑巧, bitter taste 苦味, bitter argument 激烈争吵, bitter cold 严寒, bitter pill to swallow 难以接受的事实
[联] sweet 甜的, sour 酸的, salty 咸的, coffee 咖啡, dark 深色的, resent 怨恨, painful 痛苦的, flavour 风味, herb 药草, medicine 药
[记] 比较级 bitterer。bitter 可表情感「怨愤」，与 taste 义平行。to the bitter end 坚持到底

**salty**
英/美 /ˈsɔːlti/
adj. 咸的；粗俗的
[义] Tasting of or containing salt. 有盐味或含盐的（salty snack 咸零食，too salty 太咸，salty language 粗话〔喻〕）。比较级 saltier, saltiest。
[例] ① The soup is a bit salty—add more water. 汤有点咸，加点水。 ② Chips and nuts are typical salty snacks. 薯片和坚果是典型的咸零食。
[搭] salty snack 咸零食, too salty 太咸, salty taste 咸味, salty water 咸水, salty language 粗话
[联] salt 盐, sweet 甜的, sour 酸的, bitter 苦的, spice 香料, flavor 风味, sea 海, snack 零食, taste 味道, season 调味
[记] 比较级 saltier。salt 名词不可数。salty language 指「带脏话的」，与盐味比喻相关

**spicy**
英/美 /ˈspaɪsi/
adj. 辛辣的；刺激的
[义] Containing strong flavours from spices, especially chilli; also exciting or slightly shocking. 含香料的 strong 风味，尤指辣椒；亦指刺激或略出格的（spicy food 辣菜，spicy sauce 辣酱，spicy story 劲爆故事）。比较级 spicier, spiciest。
[例] ① Thai food can be very spicy if you ask for extra chilli. 泰餐若加额外辣椒会很辣。 ② The memoir includes a few spicy details about life in Hollywood. 回忆录里有些关于好莱坞生活的劲爆细节。
[搭] spicy food 辣菜, spicy sauce 辣酱, too spicy 太辣, spicy flavour 辛辣味, hot and spicy 又热又辣
[联] spice 香料, hot 热的, pepper 胡椒, chilli 辣椒, flavour 风味, sweet 甜的, mild 温和的, curry 咖喱, tongue 舌头, sweat 出汗
[记] 比较级 spicier。spicy 与 hot 有重叠；hot 更口语，spicy 强调香料。hot 亦可指 temperature

**delicious**
英/美 /dɪˈlɪʃəs/
adj. 美味的；可口的
[义] Having a very pleasant taste or smell. 味道或香气非常悦人（delicious meal 美味的一餐，smell delicious 闻起来很香）。比较级 more delicious, most delicious（少用 deliciouser）。
[例] ① The homemade soup was absolutely delicious. 这碗家常汤简直太好喝了。 ② Something delicious was baking in the oven. 烤箱里正烤着什么好吃的东西。
[源] 源自拉丁语 *deliciosus*（令人愉悦的），*de-* + *licere*（取悦）→ 令人愉悦的（味道）。
[族] delight 喜悦, delectable 美味的
[搭] delicious meal 美味的一餐, smell delicious 闻起来很香, look delicious 看起来好吃, absolutely delicious 绝对美味
[联] tasty 美味的, yummy 好吃的（口语）, flavour 风味, sweet 甜的, fresh 新鲜的, cook 烹饪, dish 菜肴, appetite 食欲, savoury 咸鲜的, mouth-watering 令人垂涎的
[记] 比较级常用 more delicious。delicious 语气比 tasty 强。yummy 更儿语/口语

**fresh**
英/美 /freʃ/
adj. 新鲜的；清新的
[义] Recently made, picked, or prepared; not preserved or stale. 新近制作、采摘或准备的；未经保藏或变质的（fresh fruit 新鲜水果，fresh bread 新鲜面包，fresh air 新鲜空气）。比较级 fresher, freshest。
[例] ① Buy fresh fish the day you plan to cook it. 打算做鱼那天再买鲜鱼。 ② Nothing beats fresh bread from the bakery. 什么也比不上面包店刚出炉的面包。
[搭] fresh fruit 新鲜水果, fresh bread 新鲜面包, fresh ingredients 新鲜食材, farm fresh 农场新鲜, fresh out of 刚用完（口语）
[联] stale 不新鲜的, rotten 腐烂的, ripe 成熟的, raw 生的, frozen 冷冻的, local 本地的, crisp 脆的, clean 干净的, produce 农产品, market 市场
[记] 比较级 fresher。fresh 与 stale / frozen 相对。get fresh with sb. 美式俚语指「对某人无礼/动手动脚」

**restaurant**
英/美 /ˈrestrɒnt/ 美 /ˈrestrɑːnt/
n. 餐厅；餐馆
[义] A place where meals are prepared and served to customers. 为顾客准备并供应餐食的场所（fast-food restaurant 快餐店，fine dining restaurant 高级餐厅，restaurant review 餐厅点评）。可数。
[例] ① We booked a table at a small Italian restaurant. 我们在一家小意大利餐厅订了位。 ② The restaurant gets busy on Friday nights. 这家餐厅周五晚上很忙。

[搭] fast-food restaurant 快餐店, restaurant review 餐厅点评, restaurant owner 餐厅老板, open a restaurant 开餐厅, restaurant chain 连锁餐厅
[联] menu 菜单, meal 一餐, dish 菜肴, waiter 服务员, chef 厨师, order 点餐, cook 烹饪, cuisine 菜系, dine 就餐, reservation 预订

**salt**
英/美 /sɔːlt/
n. 盐；食盐
v. 撒盐；腌制
[义] A white substance used to add flavour to food and preserve it. 用于增味和防腐的白色物质（table salt 食盐，a pinch of salt 一撮盐，low-salt diet 低盐饮食）。不可数。
[例] ① Add a pinch of salt to the boiling water before the pasta. 下意面前往沸水里加一撮盐。 ② Too much salt in your diet can raise blood pressure. 饮食中盐太多可能升高血压。
