[搭] table salt 食盐, a pinch of salt 一撮盐, salt and pepper 盐和胡椒, low-salt diet 低盐饮食, take sth. with a grain of salt 对某事半信半疑
[联] pepper 胡椒, sugar 糖, spice 香料, season 调味, flavour 风味, preserve 保藏, taste 味道, sodium 钠, shake 撒, cook 烹饪
[记] 不可数。take with a grain of salt 指「对……持保留态度」。salt 动词指撒盐或腌（salted fish 咸鱼）

**sugar**
英/美 /ˈʃʊɡə(r)/
n. 糖
v. 在……上撒糖
[义] A sweet substance obtained from plants, used in food and drinks. 从植物提取的甜味物质，用于食物和饮料（brown sugar 红糖，blood sugar 血糖，sugar-free 无糖的）。不可数/可数。
[例] ① Do you take sugar in your tea? 你喝茶加糖吗？ ② Cut down on sugar if you want healthier snacks. 想要更健康的零食就减少糖摄入。
[搭] brown sugar 红糖, blood sugar 血糖, sugar-free 无糖的, teaspoon of sugar 一茶匙糖, sugar rush 糖带来的兴奋（口语）
[联] sweet 甜的, salt 盐, honey 蜂蜜, dessert 甜点, candy 糖果, coffee 咖啡, tea 茶, diabetes 糖尿病, cube 方糖, flavour 风味
[记] 不可数/可数。sugar 作可数指「一块方糖」。sugar coat 喻「粉饰」（sugar-coat bad news 粉饰坏消息）

**vanilla**
英/美 /vəˈnɪlə/
n. 香草；香草味
adj. 香草味的；普通的
[义] A flavouring from vanilla pods, used in ice cream, baking, and coffee; plain or ordinary. 香草荚提取的调味，用于冰淇淋、烘焙与咖啡；形容词可指「普通的、无特色的」。不可数/可数。
[例] ① She added a drop of vanilla to the cake batter. 她在蛋糕糊里加了一滴香草精。 ② Plain vanilla ice cream pairs well with warm pie. 原味香草冰淇淋配热派很搭。
[搭] vanilla extract 香草精, vanilla ice cream 香草冰淇淋, vanilla pod 香草荚, plain vanilla 原味/普通款, vanilla latte 香草拿铁
[联] ice cream 冰淇淋, dessert 甜点, flavour 风味, sweet 甜的, baking 烘焙, chocolate 巧克力, cinnamon 肉桂, pod 荚, extract 提取物, bean 豆
[记] 不可数/可数。vanilla 俚语可指「普通无花样」（vanilla software 基础版软件）——与食物义不同场

**oil**
英/美 /ɔɪl/
n. 油；食用油
v. 给……上油
[义] A thick liquid from plants or animals, used in cooking. 来自植物或动物的粘稠液体，用于烹饪（olive oil 橄榄油，vegetable oil 植物油，frying oil 炸用油）。不可数/可数。
[例] ① Heat a little oil in the pan before adding the garlic. 下蒜前在锅里热一点油。 ② Olive oil is often used in Mediterranean cooking. 地中海烹饪常用橄榄油。
[搭] olive oil 橄榄油, vegetable oil 植物油, frying oil 炸用油, oil and vinegar 油醋汁, dash of oil 少许油
[联] fry 油炸, fat 脂肪, butter 黄油, cook 烹饪, pan 平底锅, salad 沙拉, grease 油脂, drizzle 淋, heat 加热, ingredient 食材
[记] 不可数/可数。oil 亦指石油（不同语境）。a dash of oil 很常见

**pepper**
英/美 /ˈpepə(r)/
n. 胡椒；辣椒；甜椒
v. 撒胡椒；密集射击（喻）
[义] A spice made from dried berries (black/white pepper); also chilli peppers or bell peppers. 干浆果制成的香料（黑/白胡椒）；亦指辣椒或甜椒（black pepper 黑胡椒，bell pepper 甜椒，pepper mill 胡椒研磨器）。不可数/可数。
[例] ① Season the steak with salt and pepper before grilling. 烤前给牛排撒盐和胡椒。 ② She sliced a red pepper for the stir-fry. 她切了一个甜椒用来炒。
[搭] black pepper 黑胡椒, bell pepper 甜椒, salt and pepper 盐和胡椒, pepper mill 胡椒研磨器, ground pepper 胡椒粉
[联] salt 盐, spice 香料, spicy 辛辣的, season 调味, chilli 辣椒, flavour 风味, grind 研磨, hot 辣的, vegetable 蔬菜, steak 牛排
[记] 不可数/可数。pepper 动词 pepper sb. with questions 指「连珠炮式提问」。bell pepper 美式；英式常说 pepper 指甜椒

**garlic**
英/美 /ˈɡɑːlɪk/
n. 大蒜
[义] A bulb vegetable with a strong smell and taste, used to flavour food. 一种球茎蔬菜，气味和味道浓烈，用于增味（a clove of garlic 一瓣蒜，garlic bread 蒜香面包，garlic powder 蒜粉）。不可数/可数。
[例] ① Fry two cloves of garlic in olive oil until fragrant. 用橄榄油煎两瓣蒜至出香。 ② The sauce tastes better with fresh garlic. 这酱汁加新鲜大蒜更好吃。
[搭] a clove of garlic 一瓣蒜, garlic bread 蒜香面包, garlic powder 蒜粉, crush garlic 拍碎大蒜, roasted garlic 烤蒜
[联] onion 洋葱, spice 香料, flavour 风味, herb 香草, vegetable 蔬菜, smell 气味, chop 切碎, fry 炒, breath 口气, strong 浓烈的
[记] 不可数/可数。clove 指「一瓣」。garlic breath 指「满嘴蒜味」，社交场合常被提及

**spice**
英/美 /spaɪs/
n. 香料；情趣
v. 加香料；使增添趣味
[义] A substance made from plants, used to add flavour to food; also excitement. 植物制成的增味物质；亦指刺激/情趣（mixed spices 混合香料，spice rack 调料架，variety is the spice of life 变化是生活乐趣之源〔谚〕）。可数/不可数。
[例] ① The curry uses a blend of spices from India. 这道咖喱用了多种印度香料。 ② Travel adds spice to their otherwise quiet routine. 旅行给他们原本平静的日常增添了情趣。
[搭] mixed spices 混合香料, spice rack 调料架, spice up 使更有趣味/更刺激, add spice to 给……增添情趣
[联] spicy 辛辣的, herb 香草, pepper 胡椒, flavour 风味, curry 咖喱, cinnamon 肉桂, ginger 姜, season 调味, exotic 异域的, aroma 香气
[记] 可数/可数。spice 与 herb 有别：spice 多指干燥种子/树皮（cinnamon, cumin），herb 多指叶类（basil, parsley）

**sauce**
英/美 /sɔːs/
n. 酱汁；调味酱
[义] A thick liquid served with food to add flavour. 与食物一起供应以增加风味的浓稠液体（tomato sauce 番茄酱，soy sauce 酱油，hot sauce 辣酱）。可数/不可数。
[例] ① Pass the soy sauce, please—I like my rice salty. 请递一下酱油，我喜欢咸一点的饭。 ② The pasta came with a creamy mushroom sauce. 意面配了奶油蘑菇酱。
[源] 源自拉丁语 *sal*（盐），与 salt, salad 同族 → 原指「加盐的调味液」。
[族] salt 盐, salad 沙拉, sausage 香肠, salty 咸的
[搭] tomato sauce 番茄酱, soy sauce 酱油, hot sauce 辣酱, dipping sauce 蘸酱, on the side with extra sauce 另附额外酱汁
[联] spice 香料, flavour 风味, pasta 意面, dip 蘸, pour 浇, gravy 肉汁, condiment 调味品, cook 烹饪, dish 菜肴, recipe 食谱
[记] 可数/不可数。saucy 俚语旧式指「无礼的/调情的」，与酱汁本义无关

**vinegar**
英/美 /ˈvɪnɪɡə(r)/
n. 醋
[义] A sour liquid used in cooking and on salads, made from fermented alcohol. 烹饪和沙拉用的酸味液体，由发酵酒精制成（balsamic vinegar 意大利香醋，apple cider vinegar 苹果醋，oil and vinegar 油醋）。不可数。
[例] ① Mix olive oil and vinegar for a simple salad dressing. 橄榄油加醋就是简单的沙拉酱。 ② A splash of vinegar brightens the soup. 滴一点醋能让汤更鲜亮。
[源] 源自法语 *vinaigre*（酸酒），*vin*（酒）+ *aigre*（酸）→ 变酸的酒。
[族] wine 葡萄酒, vintage 佳酿年份
[搭] balsamic vinegar 意大利香醋, apple cider vinegar 苹果醋, oil and vinegar 油醋, splash of vinegar 少许醋, vinegar and baking soda 醋与小苏打
[联] sour 酸的, salad 沙拉, oil 油, pickle 腌制, ferment 发酵, flavour 风味, sauce 酱汁, lemon 柠檬, acid 酸性, dressing 沙拉酱
[记] 不可数。vinegar 与 wine 同源。balsamic 来自意大利摩德纳，常配沙拉或奶酪

**honey**
英/美 /ˈhʌni/
n. 蜂蜜
[义] A sweet, sticky substance made by bees. 蜜蜂生产的甜而黏的物质（raw honey 生蜂蜜，honey and lemon 蜂蜜柠檬，local honey 本地蜂蜜）。不可数。
[例] ① She stirred honey into her yogurt instead of sugar. 她在酸奶里搅蜂蜜代替糖。 ② Local honey is sold at the farmers' market every Saturday. 本地蜂蜜每周六在农贸市集出售。
[搭] raw honey 生蜂蜜, honey and lemon 蜂蜜柠檬, local honey 本地蜂蜜, drizzle honey 淋蜂蜜, honey bee 蜜蜂
[联] sweet 甜的, sugar 糖, bee 蜜蜂, nectar 花蜜, syrup 糖浆, yogurt 酸奶, tea 茶, sticky 黏的, golden 金黄的, natural 天然的
[记] 不可数。honey 作称呼「亲爱的」很口语。honey trap 指「美人计/诱饵圈套」

**flavour**
英 /ˈfleɪvə(r)/ 美 /ˈfleɪvər/
n. 风味；口味
v. 给……调味
[义] The particular taste of a food or drink; a substance added to give taste. 食物或饮料的特定味道；增味物质（natural flavour 天然风味，full of flavour 风味浓郁，artificial flavour 人工香精）。可数/不可数。美式拼写 flavor。
[例] ① The soup lacks flavour—try adding more herbs. 汤缺味，试试多加香草。 ② This ice cream comes in twelve different flavours. 这款冰淇淋有十二种口味。
[搭] natural flavour 天然风味, full of flavour 风味浓郁, artificial flavour 人工香精, rich flavour 浓郁风味, flavour of the month 红极一时的人/物（喻）
[联] taste 品尝, spice 香料, sweet 甜的, savoury 咸鲜的, aroma 香气, delicious 美味的, bland 淡而无味的, ingredient 食材, enhance 增强, dish 菜肴
[记] 不可数/可数。flavour 偏整体风味；taste 偏舌头感知的动作或瞬间。flavour of the month 喻「昙花一现的红人」

**chop**
英/美 /tʃɒp/
v. 切碎；砍
n. 排骨；砍
[义] To cut food into small pieces with a knife; a piece of meat on the bone. 用刀把食物切成小块；带骨肉块（chop onions 切洋葱，finely chopped 切得很细，pork chop 猪排）。规则动词。
[形] chopped / chopped / chopping
[例] ① Chop the vegetables while the oil heats up. 油热的同时把蔬菜切碎。 ② We grilled lamb chops with rosemary. 我们烤了迷迭香羊排。
[搭] chop onions 切洋葱, finely chopped 切得很细, pork chop 猪排, chop up 剁碎, chopping board 砧板
[联] slice 切片, dice 切丁, cut 切, knife 刀, onion 洋葱, garlic 大蒜, cook 烹饪, mince 绞碎, peel 削皮, stir 搅拌
[记] 规则变化。chop and change 英式指「反复变卦」。get the chop 俚语指「被解雇/被砍」

**slice**
英/美 /slaɪs/
n. 薄片；一片
v. 切片
[义] A thin, flat piece cut from food; to cut food into thin pieces. 从食物切下的薄平片；把食物切成薄片（a slice of bread 一片面包，thinly sliced 切得很薄，slice of pizza 一块披萨）。可数/规则动词。
[形] sliced / sliced / slicing
[例] ① Could you slice the bread for sandwiches? 你能把面包切片做三明治吗？ ② She ate a slice of cake with her coffee. 她喝咖啡时吃了一块蛋糕。
[搭] a slice of bread 一片面包, thinly sliced 切得很薄, slice of pizza 一块披萨, slice into 切成, slice off 切下
[联] chop 切碎, cut 切, bread 面包, cake 蛋糕, knife 刀, piece 一块, serve 上菜, portion 一份, thick 厚的, thin 薄的
[记] 可数/动词。a slice of life 指「生活片段（写实作品）」。any way you slice it 美式指「不管怎样看」

**stir**
英/美 /stɜː(r)/
v. 搅拌；激起
n. 搅动；轰动
[义] To mix food or liquid by moving a spoon round and round; to cause excitement. 用勺画圈搅动混合食物或液体；亦指引起轰动（stir the soup 搅汤，stir-fry 翻炒，stir sugar into tea 把糖搅进茶里）。规则动词。
[形] stirred / stirred / stirring
[例] ① Stir the sauce constantly so it doesn't stick. 不断搅拌酱汁以免粘锅。 ② The scandal stirred anger across the country. 丑闻在全国激起愤怒。
[搭] stir the soup 搅汤, stir-fry 翻炒, stir into 搅入, stir up 激起/惹事, stir well 搅匀
[联] mix 混合, spoon 勺子, cook 烹饪, fry 炒, boil 煮, blend 混合, whisk 搅打, sauce 酱汁, soup 汤, combine 结合
[记] 规则变化。stir-fry 作名词/动词指「翻炒」。stir 作名词 a stir 指「骚动/轰动」

**steam**
英/美 /stiːm/
n. 蒸汽
v. 蒸；蒸汽熨
[义] The hot vapour from boiling water; to cook food using this vapour; also to remove wrinkles from fabric with hot steam. 沸水产生的热气；用蒸汽烹饪；亦指用热蒸汽去除衣物褶皱（steam vegetables 蒸蔬菜，steam rice 蒸饭，steam a dress 蒸汽熨衣）。不可数/规则动词。
[形] steamed / steamed / steaming
[例] ① Steam the broccoli for five minutes to keep it crisp. 西兰花蒸五分钟保持脆嫩。 ② She steamed the silk blouse instead of ironing it. 她蒸汽熨丝绸女衫，没用熨斗。
[搭] steam vegetables 蒸蔬菜, steam rice 蒸饭, steamed bun 包子, steam a dress 蒸汽熨衣, let off steam 发泄（喻）
[联] boil 煮, cook 烹饪, hot 热的, vapour 蒸汽, pot 锅, iron 熨, moist 湿润的, tender 软嫩的, healthy 健康的, fry 炸
[记] 规则变化。steaming hot 指「滚烫的」。let off steam 指「发泄情绪」。熨烫语境看宾语是否为衣物

**peel**
英/美 /piːl/
v. 削皮；剥落
n. 果皮
[义] To remove the skin from fruit or vegetables. 去掉水果或蔬菜的皮（peel an apple 削苹果皮，peel and chop 削皮并切，orange peel 橙皮）。规则动词。
[形] peeled / peeled / peeling
[例] ① Peel the potatoes before you boil them. 煮土豆前先削皮。 ② The paint began to peel off the old kitchen wall. 旧厨房墙上的漆开始剥落。
[搭] peel an apple 削苹果皮, peel and chop 削皮并切, orange peel 橙皮, peel off 剥落, potato peeler 削皮器
[联] skin 皮, chop 切, apple 苹果, potato 土豆, onion 洋葱, slice 切片, fruit 水果, vegetable 蔬菜, knife 刀, zest 果皮屑
[记] 规则变化。keep one's eyes peeled 指「留意观察」。banana peel 常作滑倒梗

**serve**
英/美 /sɜːv/
v. 上菜；服务
[义] To give food or drink to someone at a meal; to provide a service. 在用餐时给某人提供食物或饮料；提供服务（serve dinner 上晚餐，serve with rice 配米饭上桌，serves four 够四人份）。规则动词。
[形] served / served / serving
[例] ① Dinner is served at seven o'clock sharp. 晚餐七点整开席。 ② The curry is best served with plain rice. 咖喱配白饭最好。
[搭] serve dinner 上晚餐, serve with rice 配米饭, serves four 够四人份, serve hot 热食上桌, self-service 自助
[联] dish 菜肴, meal 一餐, waiter 服务员, restaurant 餐厅, cook 烹饪, plate 盘子, portion 一份, guest 客人, order 点餐, food 食物
[记] 规则变化。serve 法律义「服刑/送达传票」与餐饮无关。service charge 服务费

**eat**
英/美 /iːt/
v. 吃
[义] To put food in your mouth, chew, and swallow it. 把食物放入口中、咀嚼并咽下（eat breakfast 吃早餐，eat out 外出就餐，eat healthy 吃得健康）。不规则动词。
[形] ate / eaten / eating
[例] ① We usually eat together as a family on Sundays. 我们周日通常全家一起吃饭。 ② Try to eat more vegetables and less processed food. 尽量多吃蔬菜、少吃加工食品。
[搭] eat breakfast 吃早餐, eat out 外出就餐, eat healthy 吃得健康, eat up 吃光, ready to eat 即食
[联] drink 喝, food 食物, meal 一餐, hungry 饥饿的, chew 咀嚼, swallow 咽下, taste 品尝, cook 烹饪, appetite 食欲, digest 消化
[记] 不规则：eat / ate / eaten。eat one's words 指「收回说过的话」。dog eat dog 竞争激烈

**drink**
英/美 /drɪŋk/
v. 喝
n. 饮料；酒
[义] To take liquid into your mouth and swallow; a liquid for drinking. 把液体送入口中并咽下；供饮用的液体（drink water 喝水，soft drink 软饮料，have a drink 喝一杯）。不规则动词。
[形] drank / drunk / drinking
[例] ① Drink plenty of water during hot weather. 天热时要多喝水。 ② Would you like a hot drink or something cold? 你要热饮还是冷的？
[搭] drink water 喝水, soft drink 软饮料, have a drink 喝一杯, drink up 喝光, alcoholic drink 含酒精饮料
[联] eat 吃, water 水, juice 果汁, coffee 咖啡, tea 茶, thirsty 口渴的, glass 玻璃杯, sip 小口喝, swallow 咽下, bar 酒吧
[记] 不规则：drink / drank / drunk。drunk 亦是形容词「醉的」。drink 作可数指「一杯/一种饮料」

**taste**
英/美 /teɪst/
n. 味道；品味
v. 品尝；有……味道
[义] The flavour sensed in the mouth; to try food to judge its flavour. 口中感知到的风味；尝食物以判断味道（good taste 好品味/好味道，taste of cheese 奶酪味，taste the soup 尝汤）。可数/不可数/规则动词。
[形] tasted / tasted / tasting
[例] ① Taste the sauce and add salt if needed. 尝尝酱汁，需要就加盐。 ② The cake has a strong taste of vanilla. 这蛋糕香草味很浓。
[搭] good taste 好品味, taste of cheese 奶酪味, taste the soup 尝汤, acquired taste 后天养成的口味, in good/bad taste 品味高雅/低俗
[联] flavour 风味, sweet 甜的, bitter 苦的, delicious 美味的, tongue 舌头, smell 闻, savour 品味, palate 味觉, dish 菜肴, sample 品尝
[记] 可数/不可数。taste 亦指「审美品味」（in good taste）。leave a bad taste in one's mouth 喻「留下坏印象」

**beer**
英/美 /bɪə(r)/
n. 啤酒
[义] An alcoholic drink made from grain, usually barley. 用谷物（通常大麦）酿制的含酒精饮料（a pint of beer 一品脱啤酒，craft beer 精酿啤酒，non-alcoholic beer 无醇啤酒）。不可数/可数。
[例] ① He ordered a beer with his burger. 他吃汉堡时点了一杯啤酒。 ② Local craft beer has become popular in the city. 本地精酿啤酒在这座城市越来越受欢迎。
[搭] a pint of beer 一品脱啤酒, craft beer 精酿啤酒, beer garden 啤酒花园, beer belly 啤酒肚, non-alcoholic beer 无醇啤酒
[联] wine 葡萄酒, alcohol 酒精, bar 酒吧, pub 酒馆, drink 喝, brewery 啤酒厂, pint 品脱, cold 冰的, foam 泡沫, toast 干杯
[记] 不可数/可数。a beer 指「一杯/一种啤酒」。beer 比 wine 更 casual。root beer 美式指「根汁汽水」，通常无酒精

**wine**
英/美 /waɪn/
n. 葡萄酒；酒
v. 用酒款待；喝酒
[义] An alcoholic drink made from fermented grapes. 发酵葡萄制成的含酒精饮料（red wine 红葡萄酒，white wine 白葡萄酒，glass of wine 一杯葡萄酒）。不可数/可数。
[例] ① They shared a bottle of wine over dinner. 他们晚餐分享了一瓶葡萄酒。 ② This region is famous for its white wine. 这一产区以白葡萄酒闻名。
[源] 源自拉丁语 *vinum*（酒），与 vinegar 同族。
[族] vinegar 醋, vintage 佳酿年份
[搭] red wine 红葡萄酒, white wine 白葡萄酒, glass of wine 一杯葡萄酒, wine list 酒单, wine and cheese 葡萄酒配奶酪
[联] beer 啤酒, grape 葡萄, alcohol 酒精, bottle 瓶, cellar 酒窖, cheese 奶酪, dinner 晚餐, toast 干杯, vineyard 葡萄园, sommelier 侍酒师
[记] 不可数/可数。wine and dine 指「宴请款客」。wine 动词很少用

**espresso**
英 /eˈspresəʊ/ 美 /eˈspresəʊ/
n. 浓缩咖啡
[义] Strong black coffee made by forcing hot water through finely ground coffee beans. 让热水高压穿过细磨咖啡豆制成的浓黑咖啡（double espresso 双份浓缩, espresso machine 意式咖啡机, pull an espresso 萃取一杯浓缩）。可数/不可数。
[例] ① She ordered a double espresso after lunch. 她午饭后点了一杯双份浓缩。 ② The barista pulled a perfect espresso with thick crema. 咖啡师萃出一杯油脂丰厚的浓缩。
[搭] double espresso 双份浓缩, espresso machine 意式咖啡机, pull an espresso 萃取浓缩, espresso shot 一份浓缩, iced espresso 冰浓缩
[联] coffee 咖啡, cappuccino 卡布奇诺, latte 拿铁, cafe 咖啡馆, caffeine 咖啡因, barista 咖啡师, crema 咖啡油脂, bean 咖啡豆, strong 浓的, Italian 意大利的
[记] 可数/不可数。意大利语借词，重音在第二音节。espresso 比 drip coffee 更浓、量更小

**cappuccino**
英/美 /ˌkæpuˈtʃiːnəʊ/
n. 卡布奇诺
[义] Coffee made with espresso and hot frothy milk, often topped with cocoa powder. 浓缩咖啡加热奶泡制成，常撒可可粉（cappuccino foam 卡布奇诺奶泡, iced cappuccino 冰卡布, oat milk cappuccino 燕麦奶卡布）。可数。
[例] ① He grabbed a cappuccino on the way to the office. 他去办公室路上买了杯卡布奇诺。 ② A proper cappuccino has equal parts espresso, steamed milk, and foam. 正宗卡布奇诺浓缩、热奶、奶泡比例相当。
[搭] iced cappuccino 冰卡布, cappuccino foam 卡布奇诺奶泡, oat milk cappuccino 燕麦奶卡布, order a cappuccino 点卡布奇诺, dry cappuccino 少奶泡卡布
[联] espresso 浓缩咖啡, latte 拿铁, coffee 咖啡, foam 奶泡, milk 牛奶, cafe 咖啡馆, barista 咖啡师, cocoa 可可, breakfast 早餐, Italian 意大利的
[记] 可数：a cappuccino。名字来自 Capuchin 修士袍色。英式下午有时不当正餐咖啡

**cocktail**
英/美 /ˈkɒkteɪl/
n. 鸡尾酒
[义] An alcoholic drink made by mixing spirits with juice, soda, or other ingredients. 烈酒与果汁、汽水或其他配料混合的含酒精饮料（cocktail menu 鸡尾酒单, house cocktail 招牌鸡尾酒, mocktail 无酒精鸡尾酒）。可数。
[例] ① They shared a fruity cocktail by the pool. 他们在泳池边分享一杯果味鸡尾酒。 ② The bartender invented a new cocktail for the opening night. 调酒师为开幕夜发明了一款新鸡尾酒。
[搭] cocktail menu 鸡尾酒单, house cocktail 招牌鸡尾酒, cocktail party 鸡尾酒会, cocktail hour 鸡尾酒时段, fruit cocktail 水果鸡尾酒
[联] bar 酒吧, alcohol 酒精, whiskey 威士忌, vodka 伏特加, rum 朗姆酒, bartender 调酒师, drink 喝, party 派对, mocktail 无酒精鸡尾酒, champagne 香槟
[记] 可数：a cocktail。fruit cocktail 亦可指「水果罐头拼盘」——语境区分

**champagne**
英/美 /ʃæmˈpeɪn/
n. 香槟；香槟酒
[义] A sparkling white wine from the Champagne region of France, drunk on celebrations. 法国香槟区产的气泡白葡萄酒，庆典时饮用（pop champagne 开香槟, glass of champagne 一杯香槟, champagne toast 香槟祝酒）。不可数/可数。
[例] ① They popped champagne when the deal was signed. 签约时他们开了香槟。 ② Only wine from Champagne can legally be called champagne in the EU. 在欧盟，只有香槟产的酒才能合法叫 champagne。
[搭] pop champagne 开香槟, glass of champagne 一杯香槟, champagne toast 香槟祝酒, champagne region 香槟产区, sparkling wine 气泡酒
[联] wine 葡萄酒, celebration 庆祝, toast 干杯, bubble 气泡, bottle 瓶, party 派对, wedding 婚礼, New Year 新年, luxury 奢侈, French 法国的
[记] 不可数/可数。许多非香槟产气泡酒只能叫 sparkling wine。champagne 喻「最精华部分」（the champagne of ……中的极品）

**whiskey**
英 /ˈwɪski/ 美 /ˈwɪski/
n. 威士忌
[义] A strong alcoholic spirit made from grain, aged in wooden barrels. 谷物酿制、木桶陈年的烈酒（Scotch whiskey 苏格兰威士忌, glass of whiskey 一杯威士忌, whiskey on the rocks 加冰威士忌）。不可数/可数。英式拼写 whisky 亦常见。
[例] ① He poured a small glass of whiskey after dinner. 晚饭后他倒了小杯威士忌。 ② Single malt whiskey can vary greatly by region. 单一麦芽威士忌因产区差异很大。
[搭] Scotch whiskey 苏格兰威士忌, glass of whiskey 一杯威士忌, whiskey on the rocks 加冰威士忌, bourbon whiskey 波本威士忌, whiskey sour 威士忌酸
[联] alcohol 酒精, bar 酒吧, cocktail 鸡尾酒, vodka 伏特加, rum 朗姆酒, barrel 木桶, ice 冰, drink 喝, toast 干杯, Scottish 苏格兰的
[记] 不可数/可数。whiskey（爱尔兰/美式）vs. whisky（苏格兰/加拿大）。bourbon 是美国波本威士忌

**vodka**
英/美 /ˈvɒdkə/
n. 伏特加
[义] A clear, strong spirit originally from Eastern Europe, often mixed in cocktails. 源自东欧的无色烈酒，常作鸡尾酒基酒（vodka tonic 伏特加汤力, flavored vodka 风味伏特加, shot of vodka 一小杯伏特加）。不可数/可数。
[例] ① She ordered a vodka tonic with lime. 她点了杯加青柠的伏特加汤力。 ② The cocktail uses vodka as a neutral base. 这款鸡尾酒以伏特加作中性基酒。
[搭] vodka tonic 伏特加汤力, flavored vodka 风味伏特加, shot of vodka 一小杯伏特加, vodka martini 伏特加马天尼, premium vodka 高端伏特加
[联] cocktail 鸡尾酒, alcohol 酒精, bar 酒吧, tonic 汤力水, lime 青柠, rum 朗姆酒, whiskey 威士忌, Russian 俄国的, clear 无色的, mix 混合
[记] 不可数/可数。vodka 几乎无强烈风味，适合调配。俄罗斯/波兰传统烈酒

**rum**
英/美 /rʌm/
n. 朗姆酒
[义] A strong alcoholic drink made from sugar cane, associated with the Caribbean. 甘蔗酿制的烈酒，与加勒比海地区关联（dark rum 深色朗姆, rum and coke 朗姆可乐, spiced rum 香料朗姆）。不可数/可数。
[例] ① The bartender made a classic rum and coke. 调酒师做了一杯经典朗姆可乐。 ② Dark rum adds depth to winter cocktails. 深色朗姆给冬季鸡尾酒增添层次。
[搭] dark rum 深色朗姆, rum and coke 朗姆可乐, spiced rum 香料朗姆, white rum 白朗姆, rum punch 朗姆潘趣
[联] cocktail 鸡尾酒, alcohol 酒精, Caribbean 加勒比, sugar 糖, coke 可乐, bar 酒吧, pirate 海盗, tropical 热带的, whiskey 威士忌, vodka 伏特加
[记] 不可数/可数。rum 在航海与海盗文化中形象鲜明。rum cake 朗姆蛋糕是加勒比常见点心

**sake**
英/美 /ˈsɑːki/
n. 日本清酒
[义] A Japanese alcoholic drink made from fermented rice, served warm or cold. 日本米发酵酿成的酒，可温饮或冷饮（hot sake 热清酒, sake cup 清酒杯, sake brewery 清酒厂）。不可数/可数。
[例] ① We shared a bottle of cold sake with sushi. 我们吃寿司时分了一瓶冷清酒。 ② The server warmed the sake in a small ceramic flask. 服务员用小陶壶温了清酒。
[搭] hot sake 热清酒, cold sake 冷清酒, sake cup 清酒杯, sake brewery 清酒厂, premium sake 高级清酒
[联] sushi 寿司, Japanese 日本的, rice 米饭, alcohol 酒精, warm 温的, bottle 瓶, restaurant 餐厅, fish 鱼, toast 干杯, brewery 酒厂
[记] 不可数/可数。日语 *sake* 广义可指「酒」，英语 sake 专指日本清酒。发音 /ˈsɑːki/，勿读成 /seɪk/

**smoothie**
英/美 /ˈsmuːði/
n. 思慕雪；冰沙
[义] A thick, cold drink made from fruit, yogurt, or milk blended together. 水果、酸奶或牛奶打成的浓稠冷饮（green smoothie 绿色果蔬冰沙, protein smoothie 蛋白冰沙, berry smoothie 浆果冰沙）。可数。
[例] ① She blended a banana smoothie for breakfast. 她早餐打了香蕉冰沙。 ② The cafe sells smoothies with no added sugar. 咖啡馆卖无添糖冰沙。
[搭] green smoothie 绿色果蔬冰沙, protein smoothie 蛋白冰沙, berry smoothie 浆果冰沙, blend a smoothie 打冰沙, fruit smoothie 水果冰沙
[联] fruit 水果, yogurt 酸奶, milk 牛奶, blend 搅拌, banana 香蕉, healthy 健康的, breakfast 早餐, juice 果汁, ice 冰, cafe 咖啡馆
[记] 可数：a smoothie。smooth 形容词 + -ie 指「顺滑饮品」。与 milkshake 相比通常更轻、更偏健康

**soda**
英/美 /ˈsəʊdə/
n. 汽水；苏打
[义] A sweet fizzy drink; also sodium bicarbonate used in baking. 含糖碳酸饮料；亦指烘焙用小苏打（diet soda 健怡汽水，baking soda 小苏打，soda water 苏打水）。不可数/可数。
[例] ① The kids asked for soda with their pizza. 孩子们吃披萨时要了汽水。 ② Mix baking soda with vinegar to clean the drain. 把小苏打和醋混合来疏通下水道。
[搭] diet soda 健怡汽水, baking soda 小苏打, soda water 苏打水, can of soda 一罐汽水, soda fountain 汽水机
[联] drink 饮料, fizzy 有气泡的, sugar 糖, juice 果汁, water 水, pop 汽水（美式口语）, cola 可乐, sweet 甜的, ice 冰, straw 吸管
[记] 美式 soda 常指甜汽水；英式更常说 fizzy drink / soft drink。baking soda 即碳酸氢钠

**soup**
英/美 /suːp/
n. 汤；羹
[义] A liquid food made by boiling meat, vegetables, or fish in water. 把肉、蔬菜或鱼在水中煮成的流质食物（chicken soup 鸡汤，vegetable soup 蔬菜汤，from scratch soup 从头做的汤）。不可数/可数。
[例] ① A bowl of hot soup warmed us up after the walk. 散步后一碗热汤让我们暖和起来。 ② She simmered the soup for an hour to deepen the flavour. 她把汤小火炖了一小时以增浓风味。
[搭] chicken soup 鸡汤, vegetable soup 蔬菜汤, bowl of soup 一碗汤, soup kitchen 施粥厨房, in the soup 陷入困境（俚语）
[联] broth 肉汤, stew 炖菜, boil 煮, ladle 汤勺, bowl 碗, noodle 面条, vegetable 蔬菜, warm 暖和的, simmer 小火炖, starter 前菜
[记] 不可数/可数。from soup to nuts 美式指「从头到尾一切」。in the soup 指「出麻烦」

**pizza**
英/美 /ˈpiːtsə/
n. 披萨；比萨
[义] A flat round bread base topped with tomato, cheese, and other ingredients, baked in an oven. 扁平圆形面底上铺番茄、奶酪及其他配料，在烤箱中烘烤（a slice of pizza 一块披萨，pepperoni pizza 意大利辣香肠披萨，order pizza 点披萨）。可数/不可数。
[例] ① We ordered pizza for the movie night at home. 家庭电影之夜我们点了披萨。 ② Homemade pizza tastes better with fresh mozzarella. 自制披萨用新鲜马苏里拉更好吃。
[搭] a slice of pizza 一块披萨, pepperoni pizza 意大利辣香肠披萨, order pizza 点披萨, pizza delivery 披萨外送, deep-dish pizza 深盘披萨
[联] cheese 奶酪, tomato 番茄, slice 一片, oven 烤箱, bake 烘烤, Italian 意大利的, delivery 外送, topping 配料, crust 饼底, fast food 快餐
[记] 可数/不可数。pizza 通常不可数表食物总称；a pizza 指整张。takeaway pizza 英式；takeout pizza 美式

**cake**
英/美 /keɪk/
n. 蛋糕；块
v. 结块
[义] A sweet baked food made from flour, eggs, butter, and sugar. 用面粉、鸡蛋、黄油和糖烤制的甜食（birthday cake 生日蛋糕，a piece of cake 一块蛋糕/轻而易举〔习〕，rice cake 米糕）。可数/不可数。
[例] ① She baked a chocolate cake for the party. 她为聚会烤了巧克力蛋糕。 ② Cutting the wedding cake is a traditional moment. 切婚礼蛋糕是传统环节。
[搭] birthday cake 生日蛋糕, a piece of cake 一块蛋糕, chocolate cake 巧克力蛋糕, rice cake 米糕, slice of cake 一块蛋糕
[联] bake 烘烤, dessert 甜点, sweet 甜的, icing 糖霜, flour 面粉, sugar 糖, birthday 生日, pastry 糕点, oven 烤箱, celebrate 庆祝
[记] 可数/不可数。a piece of cake 习语指「轻而易举」。have your cake and eat it 指「鱼与熊掌兼得」

**chocolate**
英/美 /ˈtʃɒklət/
n. 巧克力
adj. 巧克力的
[义] A sweet brown food made from roasted cacao seeds. 烘焙可可豆制成的甜棕色食物（dark chocolate 黑巧克力，chocolate bar 巧克力棒，hot chocolate 热巧克力）。不可数/可数。
[例] ① He bought a bar of dark chocolate at the checkout. 他在收银台买了一块黑巧克力。 ② Hot chocolate is perfect on a cold winter evening. 冬夜来杯热巧克力再合适不过。
[搭] dark chocolate 黑巧克力, chocolate bar 巧克力棒, hot chocolate 热巧克力, chocolate cake 巧克力蛋糕, white chocolate 白巧克力
[联] sweet 甜的, dessert 甜点, cocoa 可可, candy 糖果, sugar 糖, melt 融化, bitter 苦的, treat 小食, craving 嘴馋, bake 烘烤
[记] 不可数/可数。chocolate 作形容词（chocolate sauce 巧克力酱）。白巧克力几乎不含可可固体

**cream**
英/美 /kriːm/
n. 奶油；乳霜
v. 搅成奶油状；击败
[义] The thick, fatty part of milk; also used in cooking and desserts. 牛奶中浓稠的脂肪部分；亦用于烹饪和甜点（whipping cream 淡奶油，cream cheese 奶油奶酪，sour cream 酸奶油）。不可数/可数。
[例] ① Whip the cream until it forms soft peaks. 把奶油打发至软性发泡。 ② The soup is finished with a swirl of cream. 汤上淋一圈奶油收尾。
[搭] whipping cream 淡奶油, cream cheese 奶油奶酪, sour cream 酸奶油, ice cream 冰淇淋, cream of the crop 精英（喻）
[联] milk 牛奶, butter 黄油, dairy 乳制品, cheese 奶酪, dessert 甜点, coffee 咖啡, rich 浓郁的, whip 打发, fat 脂肪, sauce 酱汁
[记] 不可数/可数。cream 动词俚语 cream sb. 指「彻底击败」。cream tea 英式指「司康配果酱奶油茶点」

**flour**
英/美 /flaʊə(r)/
n. 面粉
[义] A powder made by grinding grain, used to make bread, cakes, and pasta. 谷物磨成的粉末，用于制作 bread、cakes、pasta（plain flour 普通面粉，wholemeal flour 全麦面粉，flour a surface 在台面上撒面粉）。不可数。
[例] ① Sift the flour before mixing it with the eggs. 筛面粉后再与鸡蛋混合。 ② Dust the counter with flour so the dough won't stick. 在台面上撒面粉以免面团粘住。
[搭] plain flour 普通面粉, wholemeal flour 全麦面粉, flour a surface 撒面粉, cup of flour 一杯面粉, self-raising flour 自发粉
[联] bread 面包, bake 烘烤, dough 面团, wheat 小麦, grain 谷物, sift 筛, cake 蛋糕, pasta 意面, ingredient 食材, mill 磨坊
[记] 不可数。flour 与 flower 同音，拼写不同。bleached flour 漂白面粉；wholemeal 英式，whole wheat 美式

**steak**
英/美 /steɪk/
n. 牛排；肉排
[义] A thick slice of good quality beef, or similar cuts from other animals. 优质牛肉的厚片，或其他动物的类似切块（sirloin steak 西冷牛排，steak and chips 牛排配薯条，well-done steak 全熟牛排）。可数/不可数。
[例] ① He ordered a medium-rare steak with pepper sauce. 他点了一份三分熟牛排配胡椒酱。 ② Grilled steak is a classic pub meal in Britain. 烤牛排是英国酒吧经典餐食。
[搭] sirloin steak 西冷牛排, steak and chips 牛排配薯条, medium-rare 三分熟, well-done 全熟, steak knife 牛排刀
[联] beef 牛肉, meat 肉, grill 烧烤, fry 煎, pepper 胡椒, sauce 酱汁, restaurant 餐厅, juicy 多汁的, rare 一分熟, chop 排骨
[记] 可数/不可数。熟度：rare, medium-rare, medium, well-done。steak 亦可指 fish steak（鱼排）

**bacon**
英/美 /ˈbeɪkən/
n. 培根；咸猪肉
[义] Meat from the back or sides of a pig, preserved with salt, served in thin slices. 猪背或侧腹的肉，盐渍保存，切成薄片供应（crispy bacon 脆培根，bacon and eggs 培根煎蛋，streaky bacon 五花培根）。不可数。
[例] ① The smell of bacon frying filled the kitchen. 煎培根的香味弥漫厨房。 ② Americans often have bacon with pancakes at brunch. 美国人早午餐常把培根配薄煎饼。
[搭] crispy bacon 脆培根, bacon and eggs 培根煎蛋, streaky bacon 五花培根, back bacon 背脊培根, bacon sandwich 培根三明治
[联] pork 猪肉, egg 鸡蛋, breakfast 早餐, fry 煎, salty 咸的, sandwich 三明治, rashers 几片培根（英式）, ham 火腿, meat 肉, smoke 烟熏
[记] 不可数。英式 rashers of bacon 指「几片」。bring home the bacon 指「挣钱养家」

**sausage**
英/美 /ˈsɒsɪdʒ/
n. 香肠
[义] A mixture of minced meat and spices packed in a tube of skin. 绞肉与香料混合后灌入肠衣（pork sausage 猪肉香肠，breakfast sausage 早餐香肠，vegetarian sausage 素香肠）。可数。
[例] ① Fry the sausages slowly so they cook through. 慢煎香肠使其熟透。 ② Full English breakfast usually includes sausages and beans. 全英式早餐通常含香肠和豆。
[源] 源自拉丁语 *sal*（盐），与 salt, sauce 同族 → 用盐腌渍的肉。
[族] salt 盐, sauce 酱汁, salty 咸的
[搭] pork sausage 猪肉香肠, breakfast sausage 早餐香肠, vegetarian sausage 素香肠, sausage roll 香肠卷, grill sausages 烤香肠
[联] bacon 培根, pork 猪肉, meat 肉, grill 烧烤, fry 煎, spice 香料, sandwich 三明治, breakfast 早餐, hot dog 热狗, mince 绞肉
[记] 可数：a sausage / sausages。sausage 与 sauce 同 sal 词源

**seafood**
英/美 /ˈsiːfuːd/
n. 海鲜
[义] Fish and shellfish eaten as food. 作为食物食用的鱼和贝类（fresh seafood 新鲜海鲜，seafood restaurant 海鲜餐厅，allergic to seafood 对海鲜过敏）。不可数。
[例] ① The coastal town is famous for its seafood. 这座滨海小镇以海鲜闻名。 ② If you're allergic to seafood, tell the waiter before ordering. 若对海鲜过敏，点餐前告诉服务员。
[搭] fresh seafood 新鲜海鲜, seafood restaurant 海鲜餐厅, seafood platter 海鲜拼盘, allergic to seafood 对海鲜过敏, local seafood 本地海鲜
[联] fish 鱼肉, shrimp 虾, crab 蟹, lobster 龙虾, oyster 牡蛎, shellfish 贝类, ocean 海洋, grill 烧烤, restaurant 餐厅, fresh 新鲜的
[记] 不可数。fish 与 shellfish 并提。seafood 本章取饮食义；具体物种名多归第三章

**toast**
英/美 /təʊst/
n. 吐司；烤面包片
v. 烤（面包）；举杯祝酒
[义] Sliced bread browned by heat; to drink to someone's health. 加热烤至金黄的面包片；为某人健康举杯（a slice of toast 一片吐司，French toast 法式吐司，propose a toast 祝酒）。不可数/规则动词。
[形] toasted / toasted / toasting
[例] ① I had toast and jam for breakfast. 我早餐吃了吐司配果酱。 ② They raised their glasses to toast the newlyweds. 他们举杯为新人们祝酒。
[搭] a slice of toast 一片吐司, French toast 法式吐司, propose a toast 祝酒, toast with butter 黄油吐司, make a toast 致祝酒词
[联] bread 面包, butter 黄油, jam 果酱, breakfast 早餐, bake 烘烤, crisp 脆的, slice 切片, celebrate 庆祝, cheers 干杯, warm 温热的
[记] 不可数/动词。toast 祝酒义与烤面包同形。dutch treat 各付各的，与 toast 无关

**cereal**
英/美 /ˈsɪəriəl/
n. 麦片；谷物
[义] A breakfast food made from grain, eaten with milk; also grain plants such as wheat and rice. 用谷物制成的早餐食品，配 milk 吃；亦指小麦、水稻等谷物（breakfast cereal 早餐麦片，bowl of cereal 一碗麦片，whole-grain cereal 全谷物麦片）。可数/不可数。
[例] ① The kids pour cereal into a bowl every morning. 孩子们每天早上往碗里倒麦片。 ② Choose a cereal that is low in added sugar. 选一款低添加糖的麦片。
[搭] breakfast cereal 早餐麦片, bowl of cereal 一碗麦片, whole-grain cereal 全谷物麦片, cereal box 麦片盒, dry cereal 干麦片
[联] breakfast 早餐, milk 牛奶, grain 谷物, wheat 小麦, oat 燕麦, sugar 糖, bowl 碗, fibre 纤维, snack 零食, healthy 健康的
[记] 可数/不可数。cereal 亦指「谷类作物」不可数。cap'n crunch 等为品牌名，不必单独立条

**diet**
英/美 /ˈdaɪət/
n. 饮食；节食
v. 节食
[义] The food that a person usually eats; a restricted way of eating for health or weight loss. 一个人通常吃的食物；为健康或减重而限制的饮食（balanced diet 均衡饮食，go on a diet 节食，plant-based diet 植物性饮食）。可数/不可数。
[例] ① A balanced diet includes plenty of vegetables and whole grains. 均衡饮食包括大量蔬菜和全谷物。 ② She went on a diet before the wedding but gave up after a week. 她婚礼前节食，一周后就放弃了。
[源] 源自希腊语 *diaita*（生活方式），经拉丁语 → 原指「生活规律」，后专指饮食。
[族] dietary 饮食的, dietitian 营养师
[搭] balanced diet 均衡饮食, go on a diet 节食, plant-based diet 植物性饮食, low-fat diet 低脂饮食, crash diet 极端节食
[联] nutrition 营养, food 食物, eat 吃, healthy 健康的, weight 体重, calorie 卡路里, vegetarian 素食者, meal 一餐, fibre 纤维, protein 蛋白质
[记] 可数/不可数。on a diet 指「在节食」。diet 动词较少用

**nutrition**
英/美 /njuˈtrɪʃn/
n. 营养
[义] The process of getting food needed for health and growth; the study of this. 获取健康与生长所需食物的过程；对该过程的研究（good nutrition 良好营养，nutrition label 营养标签，poor nutrition 营养不足）。不可数。
[例] ① Good nutrition is essential for children's development. 良好营养对儿童发育至关重要。 ② Check the nutrition label for sugar and salt content. 查看营养标签上的糖盐含量。
[源] 源自拉丁语 *nutrire*（滋养），与 nourish, nutrient 同族。
[族] nourish 滋养, nutrient 营养素, nutritious 有营养的, malnutrition 营养不良
[搭] good nutrition 良好营养, nutrition label 营养标签, poor nutrition 营养不足, nutrition facts 营养成分表, public nutrition 公共营养
[联] diet 饮食, food 食物, health 健康, vitamin 维生素, protein 蛋白质, calorie 卡路里, balanced 均衡的, eat 吃, growth 生长, label 标签
[记] 不可数。nutritionist 营养师；dietitian 更偏临床饮食指导，二者常混用

**vegetarian**
英/美 /ˌvedʒəˈteəriən/
n. 素食者
adj. 素食的
[义] A person who does not eat meat; food without meat. 不吃肉的人；不含肉的食物（vegetarian diet 素食饮食，vegetarian restaurant 素食餐厅，strict vegetarian 严格素食者）。可数/形容词。
[例] ① She has been a vegetarian since she was a teenager. 她从青少年起就是素食者。 ② The menu has several vegetarian options. 菜单上有好几道素食选项。
[源] 源自 vegetable + -arian（表「信奉/实践……者」），19 世纪造词。
[族] vegetation 植被, vegetable 蔬菜
[搭] vegetarian diet 素食饮食, vegetarian restaurant 素食餐厅, vegetarian option 素食选项, strict vegetarian 严格素食者, go vegetarian 改吃素
[联] vegan 纯素者, meat 肉, vegetable 蔬菜, diet 饮食, plant-based 植物性的, protein 蛋白质, ethical 伦理的, health 健康, tofu 豆腐, salad 沙拉
[记] 可数：a vegetarian。vegetarian 可不吃肉但吃蛋奶；vegan 连蛋奶也避。vegetable 是蔬菜，不是 vegetarian

**organic**
英/美 /ɔːˈɡænɪk/
adj. 有机的；天然的
[义] Produced without artificial chemicals, especially pesticides and fertilizers. 不用人工化学品（尤指农药和化肥）生产（organic vegetables 有机蔬菜，organic farming 有机农业，certified organic 认证有机）。无比较级常用。
[例] ① They pay more for organic fruit at the farmers' market. 他们在农贸市集买有机水果，价格更高。 ② The café uses organic milk in all its drinks. 这家咖啡馆所有饮品都用有机奶。
[搭] organic vegetables 有机蔬菜, organic farming 有机农业, certified organic 认证有机, organic food 有机食品, go organic 改买有机
[联] natural 天然的, fresh 新鲜的, pesticide 农药, farm 农场, healthy 健康的, vegetable 蔬菜, label 标签, sustainable 可持续的, local 本地的, chemical 化学的
[记] 无比较级。organic chemistry 指「有机化学」，与食品义不同。organic 标签各国标准不一

**raw**
英/美 /rɔː/
adj. 生的；未加工的
[义] Not cooked; in a natural state without processing. 未烹饪；处于未加工的自然状态（raw fish 生鱼，raw vegetables 生蔬菜，raw honey 生蜂蜜）。无比较级常用。
[例] ① Some people enjoy raw fish in sushi. 有些人在寿司里吃生鱼。 ② Wash raw vegetables carefully before eating. 生吃蔬菜前要仔细清洗。
[搭] raw fish 生鱼, raw vegetables 生蔬菜, raw honey 生蜂蜜, raw meat 生肉, eat raw 生吃
[联] cooked 做熟的, fresh 新鲜的, ripe 成熟的, sushi 寿司, salad 沙拉, peel 削皮, wash 清洗, safe 安全的, heat 加热, organic 有机的
[记] 无比较级。raw 亦可喻「未加工的/生涩的」（raw talent 未经雕琢的天赋）。raw deal 指「不公平待遇」

**chef**
英/美 /ʃef/
n. 主厨；厨师
[义] A professional cook, especially the head cook in a restaurant. 职业厨师，尤指餐厅主厨（head chef 主厨，celebrity chef 名厨，sous chef 副厨）。可数。
[例] ① The chef came out to thank regular customers after service. 营业结束后主厨出来感谢老主顾。 ② She trained as a chef in Lyon before opening her own bistro. 她在里昂受训后开了自己的小馆。
[源] 源自法语 *chef*（头、首领），来自拉丁语 *caput*（头）→ 厨房里的「头」。
[族] chief 首领, captain 队长, achievement 成就
[搭] head chef 主厨, celebrity chef 名厨, sous chef 副厨, chef's special 主厨推荐, executive chef 行政总厨
[联] cook 烹饪, kitchen 厨房, restaurant 餐厅, cuisine 菜系, recipe 食谱, menu 菜单, dish 菜肴, train 培训, culinary 烹饪的, waiter 服务员
[记] 可数：a chef。chef 比 cook 更专业；cook 可指普通做饭的人。Chief 首字母大写时指「首领」

**order**
英/美 /ˈɔːdə(r)/
n. 点单；顺序
v. 点餐；订购
[义] In a restaurant: a request for food or drink; to ask for food to be served. 在餐厅：对食物或饮料的要求；点单（place an order 下单，take one's order 记单，order takeaway 点外卖）。可数/规则动词。
[形] ordered / ordered / ordering
[例] ① Are you ready to order, or do you need more time? 可以点餐了吗，还是需要再看看？ ② We ordered two pizzas and a salad to share. 我们点了两张披萨和一份沙拉分着吃。
[搭] place an order 下单, take one's order 记单, order takeaway 点外卖, order online 在线点餐, side order 另点配菜
[联] menu 菜单, waiter 服务员, restaurant 餐厅, dish 菜肴, serve 上菜, delivery 外送, bill 账单, tip 小费, customer 顾客, meal 一餐
[记] 规则变化。order 多义极多（顺序、命令、秩序），餐饮语境很高频。pre-order 提前订购

**ice cream**
英/美 /ˌaɪs ˈkriːm/
n. 冰淇淋
[义] A sweet frozen food made from cream, milk, and sugar, often flavoured. 用奶油、牛奶和糖制成的冷冻甜食，常加风味（a scoop of ice cream 一球冰淇淋，vanilla ice cream 香草冰淇淋，ice cream cone 甜筒）。不可数/可数。
[例] ① The children chose ice cream for dessert. 孩子们甜点选了冰淇淋。 ② One scoop is enough—I'm too full for two. 一球就够了，两球我吃不下。
[搭] a scoop of ice cream 一球冰淇淋, vanilla ice cream 香草冰淇淋, ice cream cone 甜筒, ice cream shop 冰淇淋店, melt 融化
[联] cream 奶油, dessert 甜点, chocolate 巧克力, sweet 甜的, cold 冷的, cone 甜筒, flavour 风味, frozen 冷冻的, summer 夏天, treat 小食
[记] 不可数/可数。ice cream 两词；亦写 ice-cream 作形容词（ice-cream sundae）。brain freeze 吃太快头痛

**ham**
英/美 /hæm/
n. 火腿
[义] Meat from the leg or shoulder of a pig, preserved and usually sliced. 猪腿或肩部的肉，经腌渍后通常切片（ham sandwich 火腿三明治，sliced ham 火腿片，Christmas ham 圣诞火腿）。不可数/可数。
[例] ① She made a ham and cheese sandwich for lunch. 她午餐做了火腿奶酪三明治。 ② The holiday table included a glazed ham. 节日餐桌上有一道浇汁火腿。
[搭] ham sandwich 火腿三明治, sliced ham 火腿片, Christmas ham 圣诞火腿, ham and eggs 火腿煎蛋, cured ham 熟成火腿
[联] pork 猪肉, bacon 培根, sandwich 三明治, meat 肉, slice 切片, Easter 复活节, protein 蛋白质, salty 咸的, deli 熟食店, picnic 野餐
[记] 不可数/可数。ham 俚语可指「过火表演/业余无线电爱好者」，与食物义不同

**mushroom**
英/美 /ˈmʌʃrʊm/
n. 蘑菇
v. 迅速增长（喻）
[义] A fungus with a stem and cap, eaten as food. 有柄和伞盖的真菌，可食用（button mushrooms 白蘑菇，mushroom soup 蘑菇汤，wild mushrooms 野蘑菇）。可数。
[例] ① Sauté the mushrooms in butter until golden. 用黄油把蘑菇煎到金黄。 ② Never pick wild mushrooms unless you know they are safe. 不懂鉴别就不要采野蘑菇。
[搭] button mushrooms 白蘑菇, mushroom soup 蘑菇汤, wild mushrooms 野蘑菇, stuffed mushrooms 酿蘑菇, mushroom sauce 蘑菇酱
[联] vegetable 蔬菜, fungus 真菌, soup 汤, fry 炒, forest 森林, pizza 披萨, pasta 意面, slice 切片, earthy 土腥味的, poisonous 有毒的
[记] 可数：a mushroom。动词 mushroom 指「如雨后春笋般猛增」。区分 edible 与 poisonous 品种很重要

**carrot**
英/美 /ˈkærət/
n. 胡萝卜
[义] A long orange root vegetable, eaten raw or cooked. 长形橙色根茎蔬菜，可生吃或熟吃（carrot stick 胡萝卜条，grated carrot 胡萝卜丝，carrot cake 胡萝卜蛋糕）。可数/不可数。
[例] ① Add chopped carrots to the stew for sweetness. 炖菜里加胡萝卜丁增甜。 ② Rabbits are often shown eating carrots in cartoons. 卡通里兔子常啃胡萝卜。
[搭] carrot stick 胡萝卜条, grated carrot 胡萝卜丝, carrot cake 胡萝卜蛋糕, carrot and stick 胡萝卜加大棒（喻）, baby carrots 小胡萝卜
[联] vegetable 蔬菜, orange 橙色, root 根茎, rabbit 兔子, salad 沙拉, stew 炖菜, peel 削皮, chop 切, vitamin 维生素, crunchy 脆的
[记] 可数/不可数。carrot and stick 指「软硬兼施」。the carrot 喻「诱饵/好处」

**lemon**
英/美 /ˈlemən/
n. 柠檬
adj. 柠檬色的
[义] A yellow citrus fruit with sour juice, used in cooking and drinks. 黄色柑橘类水果，汁酸，用于烹饪和饮料（lemon juice 柠檬汁，lemon zest 柠檬皮屑，a slice of lemon 一片柠檬）。可数/不可数。
[例] ① Squeeze fresh lemon juice over the grilled fish. 在烤鱼上挤新鲜柠檬汁。 ② Hot water with lemon and honey soothes a sore throat. 柠檬蜂蜜热水能缓解喉咙痛。
[搭] lemon juice 柠檬汁, lemon zest 柠檬皮屑, a slice of lemon 一片柠檬, lemon tea 柠檬茶, when life gives you lemons 遇挫自强（谚）
[联] sour 酸的, citrus 柑橘, orange 橙子, vinegar 醋, flavour 风味, slice 切片, squeeze 挤, tea 茶, fresh 新鲜的, acidic 酸性的
[记] 可数/可数。when life gives you lemons 常见励志谚语。lemon 俚语可指「次品/坑货」（buy a lemon 买到烂车）

**ginger**
英/美 /ˈdʒɪndʒə(r)/
n. 姜；生姜
adj. 姜黄色的
[义] The root of a tropical plant, used fresh or dried to add spicy heat to food. 热带植物的根，鲜用或干用以给食物添辛辣（fresh ginger 鲜姜，ginger ale 姜味汽水，ground ginger 姜粉）。不可数。
[例] ① Grate a little ginger into the stir-fry. 炒菜里磨一点姜。 ② Ginger tea is a common home remedy for nausea. 姜茶是常用的止恶心偏方。
[搭] fresh ginger 鲜姜, ginger ale 姜味汽水, ground ginger 姜粉, gingerbread 姜饼, pickled ginger 腌姜
[联] spice 香料, spicy 辛辣的, garlic 大蒜, root 根茎, tea 茶, Asian 亚洲的, flavour 风味, hot 辣的, peel 削皮, grate 磨碎
[记] 不可数。ginger ale 通常无酒精。ginger 头发指「姜黄色/红发」

**jam**
英/美 /dʒæm/
n. 果酱
v. 挤满；卡住
[义] A sweet spread made by boiling fruit with sugar. 水果加糖煮成的甜抹酱（strawberry jam 草莓酱，bread and jam 面包配果酱，traffic jam 交通堵塞〔不同义〕）。不可数/可数。
[例] ① She spread jam on her toast at breakfast. 她早餐在吐司上抹果酱。 ② Homemade jam tastes better than many shop brands. 自制果酱比很多商店品牌更好吃。
[搭] strawberry jam 草莓酱, bread and jam 面包配果酱, jam jar 果酱罐, jam on toast 吐司果酱, homemade jam 自制果酱
[联] toast 吐司, bread 面包, fruit 水果, sweet 甜的, spread 涂抹, sugar 糖, preserve 保藏, breakfast 早餐, jar 罐子, marmalade 柑橘酱
[记] 不可数/可数。jam 动词指「堵塞/挤满」，与果酱无关。marmalade 通常指柑橘类果酱（英式）

**nut**
英/美 /nʌt/
n. 坚果
[义] A hard-shelled seed or fruit eaten as food, such as almonds or walnuts. 硬壳种子或果实，作食物，如杏仁、核桃（mixed nuts 混合坚果，nut allergy 坚果过敏，peanut 花生虽为豆科，口语常并称 nuts）。可数。
[例] ① The recipe uses chopped nuts for crunch. 食谱用碎坚果增加脆感。 ② Schools often ban nuts because of allergy risks. 学校常因过敏风险禁带坚果。
[搭] mixed nuts 混合坚果, nut allergy 坚果过敏, nut butter 坚果酱, crack a nut 夹开坚果, tough nut to crack 难对付的人（喻）
[联] peanut 花生, almond 杏仁, walnut 核桃, seed 种子, crunchy 脆的, snack 零食, protein 蛋白质, allergy 过敏, roast 烤, shell 壳
[记] 可数：a nut / nuts。hard nut to crack 指「难题/难缠的人」。nut 俚语可指「疯子/狂热者」

**peanut**
英/美 /ˈpiːnʌt/
n. 花生
[义] A small oval nut that grows underground; also peanuts as a snack. 地下生长的小椭圆形「坚果」（实为豆科）；亦指花生零食（peanut butter 花生酱，peanut allergy 花生过敏，roasted peanuts 烤花生）。可数/不可数。
[例] ① Peanut butter is a staple in many American lunchboxes. 花生酱是许多美国午餐盒里的常备品。 ② The airline stopped serving peanuts due to allergy concerns. 航空公司因过敏顾虑停止供应花生。
[搭] peanut butter 花生酱, peanut allergy 花生过敏, roasted peanuts 烤花生, peanut oil 花生油, peanut sauce 花生酱
[联] nut 坚果, butter 黄油, snack 零食, allergy 过敏, oil 油, protein 蛋白质, sandwich 三明治, salty 咸的, roast 烤, Thai 泰国的
[记] 可数/不可数。peanut 喻「极少金额」（work for peanuts 挣 peanuts）。植物学上属 legume（豆科），不是 tree nut

**almond**
英/美 /ˈɑːmənd/ 美 /ˈɑːmənd/
n. 杏仁
[义] An edible nut with a hard shell and mild sweet taste, eaten raw, roasted, or as almond milk. 硬壳、味淡甜的坚果，可生吃、烘烤或作杏仁奶（almond milk 杏仁奶, sliced almonds 杏仁片, almond butter 杏仁酱）。可数。
[例] ① She sprinkled sliced almonds over the salad. 她在沙拉上撒了杏仁片。 ② Almond milk is a common dairy-free alternative. 杏仁奶是常见的无乳制品替代。
[搭] almond milk 杏仁奶, sliced almonds 杏仁片, almond butter 杏仁酱, roasted almonds 烤杏仁, almond flour 杏仁粉
[联] nut 坚果, walnut 核桃, cashew 腰果, milk 奶, butter 酱, snack 零食, protein 蛋白质, allergy 过敏, roast 烤, flour 粉
[记] 可数：an almond / almonds。almond 亦可指「杏仁形」。tree nut 过敏者须注意

**walnut**
英/美 /ˈwɔːlnʌt/
n. 核桃；胡桃
[义] A nut with a hard wrinkled shell and rich flavour, used in baking and salads. 硬壳皱皮、味浓的坚果，用于烘焙与沙拉（walnut halves 半颗核桃, walnut oil 核桃油, chopped walnuts 碎核桃）。可数。
[例] ① The brownie recipe calls for chopped walnuts. 布朗尼食谱要求加碎核桃。 ② Walnut oil adds a nutty finish to the dressing. 核桃油给沙拉酱增添坚果尾韵。
[搭] walnut halves 半颗核桃, walnut oil 核桃油, chopped walnuts 碎核桃, black walnut 黑胡桃, walnut tree 核桃树
[联] nut 坚果, almond 杏仁, cashew 腰果, oil 油, bake 烘烤, salad 沙拉, crunchy 脆的, tree 树, protein 蛋白质, snack 零食
[记] 可数：a walnut / walnuts。walnut 亦指「核桃木（家具）」。English walnut 与 black walnut 风味不同

**cashew**
英/美 /ˈkæʃuː/
n. 腰果
[义] A curved, mild nut often eaten roasted or salted as a snack. 弯月形、味淡的坚果，常烤或盐渍当零食（roasted cashews 烤腰果, cashew butter 腰果酱, cashew chicken 腰果鸡）。可数。
[例] ① He grabbed a handful of roasted cashews from the bowl. 他从碗里抓了一把烤腰果。 ② Cashew chicken is a popular takeout dish. 腰果鸡是常见外卖菜。
[搭] roasted cashews 烤腰果, cashew butter 腰果酱, cashew chicken 腰果鸡, salted cashews 盐焗腰果, cashew nut 腰果
[联] nut 坚果, almond 杏仁, walnut 核桃, snack 零食, roast 烤, salty 咸的, chicken 鸡, Thai 泰国的, curry 咖喱, protein 蛋白质
[记] 可数：a cashew / cashews。cashew 来自葡萄牙语对热带树的称呼。生腰果壳含刺激性油脂，市售多为加工后

**bean**
英/美 /biːn/
n. 豆；豆荚
[义] An edible seed that grows in a pod, such as kidney beans or green beans. 长在豆荚里可食用的种子，如芸豆、四季豆（baked beans 茄汁焗豆，green beans 四季豆，coffee beans 咖啡豆〔不同用途〕）。可数/不可数。
[例] ① Add a can of beans to make the chili more filling. 加一罐豆子让辣炖肉更管饱。 ② Green beans steam quickly and stay crisp. 四季豆蒸很快，保持脆嫩。
[搭] baked beans 茄汁焗豆, green beans 四季豆, coffee beans 咖啡豆, bean soup 豆汤, full of beans 精力充沛（俚语）
[联] vegetable 蔬菜, protein 蛋白质, legume 豆科, soup 汤, salad 沙拉, stew 炖菜, fibre 纤维, vegetarian 素食者, pod 豆荚, kidney 芸豆
[记] 可数/不可数。full of beans 指「精力旺盛」。spill the beans 指「泄露秘密」，与豆无关

**tofu**
英/美 /ˈtəʊfuː/
n. 豆腐
[义] A soft white food made from soya beans, used in East Asian cooking and popular in vegetarian diets. 大豆制成的软白食品，用于东亚烹饪，素食者常用（firm tofu 老豆腐, silken tofu 嫩豆腐, fried tofu 炸豆腐）。不可数/可数。
[例] ① Mapo tofu is a classic Sichuan dish with a spicy sauce. 麻婆豆腐是经典川菜。 ② She marinated firm tofu before grilling it. 她把老豆腐腌过后再烤。
[搭] firm tofu 老豆腐, silken tofu 嫩豆腐, fried tofu 炸豆腐, tofu scramble 豆腐炒碎, marinated tofu 腌豆腐
[联] soy 大豆, vegetarian 素食, protein 蛋白质, Asian 亚洲的, stir-fry 炒, vegan 纯素, bean 豆, soft 软的, dish 菜肴, healthy 健康的
[记] 不可数/可数。来自日语 *tōfu*，源自中文「豆腐」。firm vs. silken 口感差异大

**corn**
英/美 /kɔːn/
n. 玉米；谷物（英式）
[义] In American English: maize, a yellow grain eaten as a vegetable. 美式指玉米；黄色谷粒作蔬菜食用（corn on the cob 玉米棒，sweet corn 甜玉米，popcorn 爆米花）。英式 corn 可泛指谷物。
[例] ① We grilled corn on the cob with butter and salt. 我们烤了玉米棒，抹黄油撒盐。 ② Popcorn is a classic cinema snack. 爆米花是经典影院零食。
[搭] corn on the cob 玉米棒, sweet corn 甜玉米, popcorn 爆米花, cornflakes 玉米片, corn oil 玉米油
[联] grain 谷物, vegetable 蔬菜, cob 玉米棒, butter 黄油, grill 烧烤, cereal 麦片, field 田地, harvest 收获, kernel 玉米粒, snack 零食
[记] 英美差异：美式 corn = 玉米；英式 corn 常指「谷物/小麦」。cornflakes 早餐麦片品牌泛称

**shrimp**
英/美 /ʃrɪmp/
n. 虾（美式）；小虾
[义] A small shellfish with a curved body, eaten as seafood. 身体弯曲的小贝类，作海鲜食用（fried shrimp 炸虾，shrimp cocktail 鸡尾虾，grilled shrimp 烤虾）。英式较大虾常说 prawn。可数/不可数。
[例] ① She ordered garlic shrimp with rice. 她点了蒜香虾配饭。 ② Shrimp cook very quickly—don't overcook them. 虾熟很快，别煮过头。
[搭] fried shrimp 炸虾, shrimp cocktail 鸡尾虾, grilled shrimp 烤虾, shrimp paste 虾酱, jumbo shrimp 大虾
[联] seafood 海鲜, prawn 大虾, fish 鱼肉, grill 烧烤, fry 炸, garlic 大蒜, cocktail 鸡尾酒, shellfish 贝类, peel 剥, restaurant 餐厅
[记] 可数/不可数。美式 shrimp 大小皆可；英式大虾常用 prawn。shrimp 俚语可指「矮个子」

**lamb**
英/美 /læm/
n. 羊肉；羔羊
[义] Meat from a young sheep; also the animal. 小羊的肉；亦指羔羊本身（roast lamb 烤羊肉，lamb chop 羊排，Spring lamb 春羔）。不可数/可数。
[例] ① Roast lamb with mint sauce is a classic British dish. 烤羊肉配薄荷酱是英国经典菜。 ② The market sells local lamb from nearby farms. 市场出售附近农场产的本地羊肉。
[搭] roast lamb 烤羊肉, lamb chop 羊排, leg of lamb 羊腿, lamb stew 羊肉炖菜, Spring lamb 春羔
[联] meat 肉, sheep 羊, chop 排骨, roast 烤, mint 薄荷, stew 炖菜, beef 牛肉, grill 烧烤, Easter 复活节, pasture 牧场
[记] 不可数/可数。like a lamb 指「温顺」。mutton 指成年羊肉，比 lamb 更老更膻

**stew**
英/美 /stjuː/
n. 炖菜
v. 炖；煨
[义] A dish of meat and vegetables cooked slowly in liquid; to cook this way. 肉与蔬菜在液体中慢炖的菜肴；以此法烹饪（beef stew 炖牛肉，vegetable stew 蔬菜炖菜，let sb. stew 让某人干着急〔喻〕）。可数/不可数/规则动词。
[形] stewed / stewed / stewing
[例] ① The beef stew simmered on the stove for three hours. 炖牛肉在炉上小火煨了三小时。 ② Leave the mushrooms to stew in their own juices. 让蘑菇在自身汁水里慢煨。
[搭] beef stew 炖牛肉, vegetable stew 蔬菜炖菜, slow stew 慢炖, stew in one's own juice 自作自受, hearty stew 暖心炖菜
[联] soup 汤, simmer 小火炖, boil 煮, meat 肉, vegetable 蔬菜, pot 锅, broth 肉汤, hearty 暖心, winter 冬天, comfort food 慰藉食物
[记] 可数/动词。let sb. stew 指「让某人焦虑等着」。比 soup 更浓、固体更多

**curry**
英/美 /ˈkʌri/
n. 咖喱；咖喱菜
[义] A spicy dish, especially from South Asia, with sauce and meat or vegetables; also the spice mix used. 辛辣菜肴，尤指南亚，含酱汁与肉或蔬菜；亦指咖喱粉（chicken curry 咖喱鸡，curry powder 咖喱粉，curry house 咖喱餐馆）。可数/不可数。
[例] ① We ordered chicken curry with rice and naan. 我们点了咖喱鸡配饭和馕。 ② Add curry powder a little at a time—it can get very hot. 咖喱粉一点一点加，会很辣。
[搭] chicken curry 咖喱鸡, curry powder 咖喱粉, curry house 咖喱餐馆, curry sauce 咖喱酱, vegetable curry 素咖喱
[联] spice 香料, spicy 辛辣的, rice 米饭, Indian 印度的, Thai 泰国的, sauce 酱汁, flavour 风味, coconut 椰奶, pepper 胡椒, takeaway 外卖
[记] 可数/不可数。curry favour 指「拍马屁」，与咖喱无关。经英式殖民饮食传播全球

**pie**
英/美 /paɪ/
n. 派；馅饼
[义] A baked dish of fruit or meat covered or wrapped in pastry. 水果或肉类包在或盖在酥皮里烘烤（apple pie 苹果派，meat pie 肉派，pie chart 饼图〔喻〕）。可数。
[例] ① She baked an apple pie for Thanksgiving. 她为感恩节烤了苹果派。 ② The pub serves steak and ale pie. 酒吧供应牛排艾尔派。
[搭] apple pie 苹果派, meat pie 肉派, pie crust 派皮, as easy as pie 轻而易举, humble pie 忍气吞声（习语）
[联] pastry 油酥, cake 蛋糕, bake 烘烤, apple 苹果, crust 酥皮, dessert 甜点, slice 一片, oven 烤箱, filling 馅料, sweet 甜的
[记] 可数：a pie。as easy as pie 指「极易」。as American as apple pie 指「典型美式」

**pastry**
英/美 /ˈpeɪstri/
n. 油酥点心；酥皮
[义] A mixture of flour, fat, and water used for pies and tarts; sweet baked goods made from it. 用于派和塔的面粉、油脂与水的混合物；以此做的甜点心（puff pastry 千层酥，pastry chef 点心师，Danish pastry 丹麦酥）。不可数/可数。
[例] ① The bakery sells fresh pastry every morning. 面包店每天早上卖新鲜点心。 ② Fold the butter into the pastry carefully for flaky layers. 把黄油叠进酥皮，小心做出分层。
[源] 源自 paste（面团）+ -ry → 面团制品。
[族] paste 糊状物, pasta 意面
[搭] puff pastry 千层酥, pastry chef 点心师, Danish pastry 丹麦酥, pastry shop 点心店, shortcrust pastry 短酥皮
[联] pie 派, cake 蛋糕, bake 烘烤, flour 面粉, butter 黄油, croissant 可颂, sweet 甜的, dough 面团, bakery 面包店, dessert 甜点
[记] 不可数/可数。pastry 指酥皮/点心类总称。pastry chef 专做甜点的主厨

**cookie**
英/美 /ˈkʊki/
n. 曲奇；饼干（美式）
[义] A small sweet baked treat, usually flat and round. 通常扁圆的小甜烘焙点心（chocolate chip cookie 巧克力曲奇，fortune cookie 幸运曲奇）。可数。
[例] ① She packed cookies in the kids' lunch boxes. 她在孩子午餐盒里装了曲奇。 ② Fresh cookies smell amazing when they come out of the oven. 刚出炉的曲奇闻起来特别香。
[搭] chocolate chip cookie 巧克力曲奇, fortune cookie 幸运曲奇, cookie jar 曲奇罐, bake cookies 烤曲奇, oatmeal cookie 燕麦曲奇
[联] biscuit 饼干（英式）, cake 蛋糕, bake 烘烤, sweet 甜的, snack 零食, dessert 甜点, jar 罐子, chocolate 巧克力, treat 小食, milk 牛奶
[记] 可数：a cookie。英式 biscuit 对应美式 cookie。fortune cookie 多见于美式中餐

**candy**
英/美 /ˈkændi/
n. 糖果（美式）
[义] Sweet food made with sugar or chocolate, especially in American English. 用糖或巧克力做的甜食，尤美式（candy bar 巧克力棒, candy store 糖果店，eye candy 花瓶〔喻〕）。不可数/可数。
[例] ① The children were told not to eat too much candy before dinner. 告诉孩子晚饭前别吃太多糖。 ② She kept a bowl of candy on her desk for visitors. 她桌上放了一碗糖招待访客。
[搭] candy bar 巧克力棒, candy store 糖果店, hard candy 硬糖, cotton candy 棉花糖, trick or treat 不给糖就捣蛋
[联] sweet 甜的, sugar 糖, chocolate 巧克力, snack 零食, treat 小食, dessert 甜点, cookie 曲奇, shop 商店, Halloween 万圣节, tooth 牙
[记] 不可数/可数。英式更常说 sweets。eye candy 指「养眼的人/物」，与吃无关

**simmer**
英/美 /ˈsɪmə(r)/
v. 小火炖；微沸
n. 微沸状态
[义] To cook liquid or food just below boiling, with small bubbles. 在沸点以下加热，小泡轻冒（simmer the sauce 小火煨酱，bring to a simmer 煮至微沸，simmer down 冷静〔喻〕）。规则动词。
[形] simmered / simmered / simmering
[例] ① Let the soup simmer for twenty minutes to deepen the flavour. 让汤小火炖二十分钟以增浓风味。 ② Turn the heat down so the milk simmers, not boils. 把火调小，让牛奶微沸而非大滚。
[搭] simmer the sauce 小火煨酱, bring to a simmer 煮至微沸, simmer down 冷静, leave to simmer 继续小火炖, gentle simmer 微微沸腾
[联] boil 煮沸, stew 炖, soup 汤, heat 加热, pot 锅, sauce 酱汁, slow 慢, bubble 泡, cook 烹饪, broth 肉汤
[记] 规则变化。simmer down 口语指「消消气」。比 boil 温和，比 warm 更热

**blend**
英/美 /blend/
v. 混合
n. 混合物
[义] To mix different foods or liquids together smoothly. 把不同食物或液体均匀混合（blend ingredients 混合食材，blender 搅拌机，blend of spices 混合香料）。规则动词。
[形] blended / blended / blending
[例] ① Blend the fruit with yogurt to make a smoothie. 把水果和酸奶打成冰沙。 ② The tea is a blend of several regional leaves. 这种茶是几个产区茶叶的拼配。
[搭] blend ingredients 混合食材, blend together 混合在一起, blend of spices 混合香料, smooth blend 顺滑混合, blender 搅拌机
[联] mix 混合, stir 搅拌, combine 结合, smoothie 冰沙, juice 果汁, spice 香料, whisk 搅打, puree 果泥, liquid 液体, texture 质地
[记] 规则变化。blender 指「搅拌机」。blend in 指「融入」

**whisk**
英/美 /wɪsk/
v. 搅打
n. 打蛋器
[义] To beat eggs, cream, etc. quickly with a fork or whisk to add air. 用叉或打蛋器快速搅打鸡蛋、奶油等以充气（whisk eggs 打鸡蛋，whisk cream 打发奶油）。规则动词。
[形] whisked / whisked / whisking
[例] ① Whisk the egg whites until stiff peaks form. 蛋白打到硬性发泡。 ② She whisked the sauce to remove lumps. 她搅打酱汁去结块。
[搭] whisk eggs 打鸡蛋, whisk cream 打发奶油, whisk together 搅打混合, wire whisk 打蛋器, whisk away 迅速拿走
[联] beat 打, stir 搅拌, blend 混合, cream 奶油, egg 鸡蛋, bowl 碗, bake 烘烤, fluffy 蓬松的, mixer 搅拌器, fork 叉
[记] 规则变化。whisk away/off 指「迅速带走」。名词指「打蛋器」

**drain**
英/美 /dreɪn/
v. 沥干
n. 排水管
[义] To remove liquid from food by pouring it away or letting it run off. 倒掉或让液体流掉以去除水分（drain the pasta 沥干意面，drain excess oil 沥掉多余油）。规则动词。
[形] drained / drained / draining
[例] ① Drain the rice and fluff it with a fork. 米饭沥干，用叉拨松。 ② Leave the fried food on paper to drain off oil. 油炸食品放纸上沥油。
[搭] drain the pasta 沥干意面, drain excess oil 沥掉多余油, drain well 充分沥干, drain off 沥去, colander 漏勺
[联] boil 煮, pasta 意面, rice 米饭, oil 油, water 水, sink 水槽, cook 烹饪, fry 炸, rinse 冲洗, wet 湿的
[记] 规则变化。down the drain 指「白费/浪费」。亦指「排水管/耗尽精力」

**season**
英/美 /ˈsiːzn/
v. 给……调味
n. 季节（不同义）
[义] To add salt, pepper, or spices to food to improve its taste. 给食物加盐、胡椒或香料以改善味道（season to taste 按口味调味，well-seasoned 调味到位，season the chicken 给鸡调味）。规则动词。
[形] seasoned / seasoned / seasoning
[例] ① Season the vegetables with salt and a little olive oil. 蔬菜撒盐、淋少许橄榄油。 ② Taste the soup and season it before serving. 汤上桌前尝味并调味。
[搭] season to taste 按口味调味, well-seasoned 调味到位, season the chicken 给鸡调味, seasoning 调味料, lightly seasoned 调味偏轻
[联] salt 盐, pepper 胡椒, spice 香料, flavour 风味, taste 品尝, cook 烹饪, add 添加, sauce 酱汁, marinate 腌制, bland 淡而无味的
[记] 规则变化。season 名词指「季节」，同形异义。seasoned 亦可指「经验丰富的」

**marinate**
英/美 /ˈmærɪneɪt/
v. 腌制
[义] To soak food in a seasoned liquid before cooking to add flavour. 烹饪前把食物浸泡在调味液里以增味（marinate overnight 腌一夜，marinade 腌料，marinate the meat 腌肉）。规则动词。
[形] marinated / marinated / marinating
[例] ① Marinate the chicken for at least two hours in the fridge. 鸡肉在冰箱至少腌两小时。 ② The tofu absorbs flavour better if you marinate it first. 豆腐先腌更入味。
[源] 源自拉丁语 *marinus*（海的）→ 原指「用盐水腌」，与 marine 同族。
[族] marine 海的, marina 码头
[搭] marinate overnight 腌一夜, marinate the meat 腌肉, marinade sauce 腌料, marinate in 浸泡于, well-marinated 腌透的
[联] season 调味, sauce 酱汁, flavour 风味, soak 浸泡, grill 烧烤, meat 肉, fridge 冰箱, overnight 过夜, tender 嫩的, vinegar 醋
[记] 规则变化。marinade 名词指「腌料」。比 season 强调浸泡时间

**savoury**
英 /ˈseɪvəri/ 美 /ˈseɪvəri/
adj. 咸鲜的
n. 咸点（英式）
[义] Tasting of salt or spice rather than sugar; not sweet. 尝起来是咸或辣而非甜；不甜（savoury snacks 咸点，savoury pie 咸派，sweet and savoury 甜咸搭配）。美式拼写 savory。
[例] ① The menu lists both sweet and savoury breakfast options. 菜单上有甜咸两种早餐。 ② Savoury biscuits go well with cheese. 咸饼干配奶酪很搭。
[搭] savoury snacks 咸点, savoury pie 咸派, sweet and savoury 甜咸搭配, savoury flavour 咸鲜风味, savoury dish 咸口菜
[联] sweet 甜的, salty 咸的, spicy 辛辣的, flavour 风味, snack 零食, umami 鲜味, bland 淡而无味的, dish 菜肴, cheese 奶酪, herb 香草
[记] 英式 savoury；美式 savory。savour 动词指「品味/欣赏」

**bland**
英/美 /blænd/
adj. 淡而无味的
[义] Having little flavour; not strong or interesting in taste. 味道很弱、不鲜明（bland diet 清淡饮食，bland food 寡淡食物，bland sauce 无味的酱）。比较级 blander, blandest。
[例] ① The hospital serves bland food for patients on restricted diets. 医院给饮食受限患者供清淡餐。 ② Add herbs if you find the soup too bland. 觉得汤太淡就加香草。
[搭] bland diet 清淡饮食, bland food 寡淡食物, bland taste 寡淡口味, rather bland 相当寡淡, bland sauce 无味的酱
[联] flavour 风味, taste 味道, spicy 辛辣的, salty 咸的, savoury 咸鲜的, boring 乏味的, mild 温和的, season 调味, spice 香料, delicious 美味的
[记] 比较级 blander。亦可形容人/风格「温和无趣」

**tasty**
英/美 /ˈteɪsti/
adj. 美味的
[义] Having a good flavour; pleasant to eat. 味道好、好吃（tasty meal 美味的一餐，tasty snack 好吃的小食）。比较级 tastier, tastiest。
[例] ① The street food was cheap and surprisingly tasty. 街头小吃便宜却意外好吃。 ② She shared a tasty recipe from her hometown. 她分享了家乡一道好吃的食谱。
[搭] tasty meal 美味的一餐, tasty snack 好吃的小食, very tasty 非常好吃, tasty treat 美味小食, look tasty 看起来好吃
[联] delicious 美味的, flavour 风味, yummy 好吃的, savoury 咸鲜的, sweet 甜的, cook 烹饪, dish 菜肴, appetizing 开胃的, mouth-watering 令人垂涎的, bland 淡而无味的
[记] 比较级 tastier。比 delicious 弱，比 nice 更强调味道

**mild**
英/美 /maɪld/
adj. 温和的（口味）
[义] Not strong or hot in flavour; gentle in taste. 味道不浓、不辣（mild cheese 温和奶酪，mild curry 微辣咖喱，mild sauce 清淡酱汁）。比较级 milder, mildest。
[例] ① Ask for a mild curry if you don't like spicy food. 不吃辣就点微辣咖喱。 ② This cheddar is mild and good for sandwiches. 这款切达奶酪口味温和，做三明治很合适。
[搭] mild cheese 温和奶酪, mild curry 微辣咖喱, mild flavour 温和风味, mild sauce 清淡酱汁, mild taste 温和口味
[联] spicy 辛辣的, hot 辣的, strong 浓烈的, gentle 温和的, flavour 风味, cheese 奶酪, curry 咖喱, pepper 胡椒, bland 淡而无味的, medium 中等的
[记] 比较级 milder。亦可指天气/性格「温和」

**greasy**
英/美 /ˈɡriːsi/
adj. 油腻的
[义] Covered with or full of grease or oil; unpleasantly oily in food. 满是油脂，食物口感过油（greasy food 油腻食物，greasy fingers 满手油，greasy spoon 廉价小餐馆）。比较级 greasier, greasiest。
[例] ① The fries were tasty but a bit greasy. 薯条好吃但有点油。 ② Avoid greasy food late at night if it upsets your stomach. 夜里胃不适就少吃油腻食物。
[搭] greasy food 油腻食物, greasy fingers 满手油, greasy spoon 廉价小餐馆, feel greasy 感觉油腻, greasy skin 出油皮肤
[联] oily 多油的, fat 脂肪, fry 炸, oil 油, unhealthy 不健康的, heavy 厚重的, stomach 胃, crisp 脆的, drain 沥干, fast food 快餐
[记] 比较级 greasier。greasy spoon 指「廉价小餐馆」

**ripe**
英/美 /raɪp/
adj. 成熟的
[义] Fully grown and ready to eat, especially fruit. 完全成熟可吃，尤指水果（ripe banana 熟香蕉，ripe tomato 熟番茄，ripe for 时机成熟〔喻〕）。比较级 riper, ripest。
[例] ① Wait until the avocados are ripe before you cut them. 牛油果熟了再切。 ② The peaches smell sweet—they must be ripe. 桃子闻着甜，肯定熟了。
[搭] ripe banana 熟香蕉, ripe tomato 熟番茄, ripe fruit 熟水果, ripe for picking 可采摘, perfectly ripe 恰到好处
[联] fresh 新鲜的, raw 生的, rotten 腐烂的, fruit 水果, sweet 甜的, harvest 收获, mature 成熟的, green 未熟的, soft 软的, juicy 多汁的
[记] 比较级 riper。ripe for change 喻「时机成熟」。overripe 过熟

**rotten**
英/美 /ˈrɒtn/
adj. 腐烂的
[义] Decayed and no longer safe or pleasant to eat. 已腐败，不宜食用（rotten fruit 烂水果，smell rotten 闻着腐烂，rotten egg 臭鸡蛋）。比较级 more rotten, most rotten。
[例] ① Throw away any fruit that looks rotten. 烂掉的水果一律扔掉。 ② The milk smelled rotten, so we poured it out. 牛奶闻着坏了，我们倒掉了。
[搭] rotten fruit 烂水果, smell rotten 闻着腐烂, rotten egg 臭鸡蛋, go rotten 变烂, rotten apple 害群之马（喻）
[联] fresh 新鲜的, stale 不新鲜的, spoil 变质, decay 腐烂, waste 浪费, bin 垃圾桶, smell 气味, food 食物, mould 发霉, safe 安全的
[记] 比较级 more rotten。feel rotten 指「身体难受/糟透了」

**frozen**
英/美 /ˈfrəʊzn/
adj. 冷冻的
[义] Kept at a very low temperature to preserve food. 低温保藏的食物（frozen vegetables 冷冻蔬菜，frozen pizza 冷冻披萨，frozen food 冷冻食品）。
[例] ① Frozen peas are convenient and keep their colour well. 冷冻豌豆方便且保色。 ② Defrost frozen meat in the fridge, not on the counter. 冷冻肉在冰箱解冻，别放台面。
[搭] frozen vegetables 冷冻蔬菜, frozen pizza 冷冻披萨, frozen food 冷冻食品, frozen meal 冷冻餐, deep-frozen 深冻的
[联] fresh 新鲜的, freeze 冷冻, fridge 冰箱, thaw 解冻, cold 冷的, preserve 保藏, convenient 方便的, microwave 微波炉, ice 冰, store 储存
[记] freeze 的过去分词作形容词。frozen with fear 喻「吓僵」

**cooked**
英/美 /kʊkt/
adj. 做熟的
[义] Heated and prepared for eating; not raw. 已加热可食；非生的（well-cooked 煮透的，fully cooked 全熟，cooked meat 熟肉）。
[例] ① Make sure the chicken is fully cooked before serving. 鸡肉全熟再上桌。 ② Cooked rice keeps for a few days in the fridge. 熟米饭在冰箱可放几天。
[搭] well-cooked 煮透的, fully cooked 全熟, cooked meat 熟肉, cooked food 熟食, slow-cooked 慢炖的
[联] raw 生的, fresh 新鲜的, heat 加热, cook 烹饪, safe 安全的, serve 上菜, reheat 复热, meal 一餐, done 熟了, temperature 温度
[记] cook 的过去分词作形容词。you're cooked 俚语指「完蛋了」

**waiter**
英/美 /ˈweɪtə(r)/
n. 服务员（男）
[义] A man who serves food and drinks in a restaurant. 餐厅里为顾客上菜、上酒的男性服务员（head waiter 领班，waiter service 桌边服务）。可数。女性 waitress；中性说法 server 渐增。
[例] ① The waiter brought the menu and took our drink order. 服务员递上菜单并记下饮料点单。 ② Ask the waiter if the dish contains nuts. 问服务员这道菜是否含坚果。
[搭] head waiter 领班, waiter service 桌边服务, call the waiter 叫服务员, tip the waiter 给服务员小费, friendly waiter 热情的服务员
[联] waitress 女服务员, server 服务员, restaurant 餐厅, menu 菜单, order 点餐, serve 上菜, tip 小费, customer 顾客, bill 账单, table 桌子
[记] 可数：a waiter。waiter/waitress 渐被 server 取代

**tip**
英/美 /tɪp/
n. 小费
v. 给小费
[义] Extra money given to a waiter or service worker for good service. 因服务好而额外给服务员等的钱（leave a tip 留小费，tip generously 大方给小费，service charge 服务费）。可数/规则动词。
[形] tipped / tipped / tipping
[例] ① We left a 15% tip for the excellent service. 服务很好，我们留了 15% 小费。 ② In some countries, tipping is not expected. 有些国家不期待给小费。
[搭] leave a tip 留小费, tip generously 大方给小费, service charge 服务费, tip jar 小费罐, tipping culture 小费文化
[联] waiter 服务员, bill 账单, restaurant 餐厅, service 服务, pay 支付, cash 现金, customer 顾客, gratuity 小费（正式）, server 服务员, order 点餐
[记] 可数/动词。英美差异大：美国普遍，日本常不需要。tip 多义：尖端/建议

**bill**
英/美 /bɪl/
n. 账单
v. 开账单
[义] A statement of how much you owe for food in a restaurant; also a banknote. 餐厅应付餐费清单；亦指钞票（pay the bill 结账，split the bill 分摊账单，foot the bill 付账/承担费用）。可数。
[例] ① Could we have the bill, please? 请结账。 ② They split the bill equally among four friends. 四位朋友平摊账单。
[搭] pay the bill 结账, split the bill 分摊账单, ask for the bill 要求结账, foot the bill 付账, cover the bill 买单
[联] tip 小费, waiter 服务员, restaurant 餐厅, order 点餐, meal 一餐, pay 支付, cheque 支票, cash 现金, card 银行卡, expensive 昂贵的
[记] 可数：the bill。英式 bill；美式餐厅常说 check。亦指法案/鸟嘴

**picnic**
英/美 /ˈpɪknɪk/
n. 野餐
v. 野餐
[义] A meal eaten outdoors, often on a blanket in a park. 在户外吃的餐，常在公园毯子上（family picnic 家庭野餐，picnic basket 野餐篮，go for a picnic 去野餐）。可数/规则动词。
[形] picnicked / picnicked / picnicking
[例] ① We packed sandwiches and fruit for a picnic by the lake. 我们打包三明治和水果去湖边野餐。 ② The weather was perfect for a picnic in the park. 天气正好，适合在公园野餐。
[搭] family picnic 家庭野餐, picnic basket 野餐篮, go for a picnic 去野餐, picnic blanket 野餐毯, picnic area 野餐区
[联] sandwich 三明治, outdoor 户外的, park 公园, basket 篮子, snack 零食, blanket 毯子, summer 夏天, barbecue 烧烤, family 家庭, spread 摆满食物
[记] 可数：a picnic。no picnic 指「不容易/够呛」

**buffet**
英/美 /ˈbʊfeɪ/ 美 /bəˈfeɪ/
n. 自助餐
[义] A meal where guests serve themselves from a table of many dishes. 客人从长桌自取多种菜品的餐（breakfast buffet 早餐自助，all-you-can-eat buffet 自助畅吃，buffet restaurant 自助餐厅）。可数。
[例] ① The hotel offers a breakfast buffet with fresh fruit and pastries. 酒店早餐自助有新鲜水果和点心。 ② We chose the buffet so everyone could pick what they liked. 我们选自助，各人挑喜欢的。
[搭] breakfast buffet 早餐自助, all-you-can-eat buffet 自助畅吃, buffet restaurant 自助餐厅, buffet table 自助餐桌, salad buffet 沙拉自助
[联] restaurant 餐厅, meal 一餐, serve 上菜, plate 盘子, variety 多样, all-you-can-eat 畅吃, hotel 酒店, lunch 午餐, choice 选择, queue 排队
[记] 可数：a buffet。发音英式 /ˈbʊfeɪ/，美式常 /bəˈfeɪ/。动词义「冲击」不同

**takeaway**
英 /ˈteɪkəweɪ/ 美 /ˈteɪkəweɪ/
n. 外卖（英式）
adj. 外卖的
[义] Food bought from a restaurant to eat elsewhere; also the shop itself. 从餐厅买来在别处吃的餐；亦指外卖店（order a takeaway 点外卖，Chinese takeaway 中餐外卖，takeaway container 外卖盒）。可数/不可数。美式 takeout。
[例] ① Let's get a takeaway instead of cooking tonight. 今晚别做了，叫外卖吧。 ② The takeaway arrives in about thirty minutes. 外卖大约三十分钟到。
[搭] order a takeaway 点外卖, Chinese takeaway 中餐外卖, takeaway food 外卖食物, takeaway container 外卖盒, local takeaway 本地外卖店
[联] order 点餐, delivery 外送, restaurant 餐厅, pizza 披萨, curry 咖喱, container 容器, home 家, meal 一餐, fast food 快餐, tip 小费
[记] 英式 takeaway；美式 takeout。take away 动词短语指「带走」

**lettuce**
英/美 /ˈletɪs/
n. 生菜；莴苣
[义] A plant with large green leaves eaten raw in salads and sandwiches. 大叶绿色蔬菜，常生吃做沙拉和三明治（iceberg lettuce 卷心生菜，lettuce leaf 生菜叶，shredded lettuce 生菜丝）。不可数/可数。
[例] ① Wash the lettuce and tear it into bite-sized pieces. 生菜洗净撕成一口大小。 ② The burger comes with tomato and lettuce. 汉堡配番茄和生菜。
[搭] iceberg lettuce 卷心生菜, lettuce leaf 生菜叶, shredded lettuce 生菜丝, lettuce salad 生菜沙拉, romaine lettuce 罗马生菜
[联] salad 沙拉, vegetable 蔬菜, sandwich 三明治, fresh 新鲜的, crisp 脆的, leaf 叶子, tomato 番茄, burger 汉堡, green 绿色的, limp 蔫的
[记] 不可数/可数。lettuce 与 let us 同音，可作记忆钩子。limp lettuce 指「蔫生菜/没精神」

**broccoli**
英/美 /ˈbrɒkəli/
n. 西兰花
[义] A green vegetable with a thick stem and tight clusters of flower buds, eaten cooked or raw. 粗茎、花蕾密集成簇的绿色蔬菜，可熟吃或生吃（steamed broccoli 蒸西兰花，broccoli florets 西兰花小朵）。不可数。
[例] ① Steam the broccoli for five minutes until bright green. 西兰花蒸五分钟至翠绿。 ② The kids will eat broccoli if you serve it with cheese sauce. 配奶酪酱孩子愿意吃西兰花。
[搭] steamed broccoli 蒸西兰花, broccoli florets 西兰花小朵, broccoli soup 西兰花汤, fresh broccoli 新鲜西兰花, roast broccoli 烤西兰花
[联] vegetable 蔬菜, cauliflower 花椰菜, steam 蒸, green 绿色的, healthy 健康的, stem 茎, vitamin 维生素, side dish 配菜, crisp 脆的, cheese 奶酪
[记] 不可数。单复同形。与 cauliflower 常并称，花椰菜是「白花版」

**spinach**
英/美 /ˈspɪnɪtʃ/
n. 菠菜
[义] A dark green leafy vegetable eaten cooked or raw. 深绿色叶菜，可熟吃或生吃（fresh spinach 新鲜菠菜，spinach salad 菠菜沙拉，wilted spinach 炒软菠菜）。不可数。
[例] ① Add a handful of spinach to the soup at the end. 汤起锅前抓一把菠菜进去。 ② Popeye eats spinach to grow strong in the cartoon. 卡通里大力水手吃菠菜变强壮。
[搭] fresh spinach 新鲜菠菜, spinach salad 菠菜沙拉, wilted spinach 炒软菠菜, baby spinach 嫩菠菜, creamed spinach 奶油菠菜
[联] vegetable 蔬菜, leaf 叶子, green 绿色的, iron 铁, salad 沙拉, soup 汤, healthy 健康的, Popeye 大力水手, wilt 蔫, nutrient 营养
[记] 不可数。Popeye 让 spinach 在英语文化里几乎等于「力量食物」

**celery**
英/美 /ˈseləri/
n. 芹菜
[义] A long crunchy vegetable with pale green stalks, eaten raw or cooked. 茎部淡绿、口感爽脆的长条蔬菜，可生吃或熟吃（celery stick 芹菜条，celery salt 芹菜盐，chopped celery 芹菜丁）。不可数。
[例] ① Chop celery, onion, and carrot for the soup base. 把芹菜、洋葱、胡萝卜切丁做汤底。 ② She munched on celery sticks with peanut butter. 她啃芹菜条蘸花生酱。
[搭] celery stick 芹菜条, celery salt 芹菜盐, chopped celery 芹菜丁, celery juice 芹菜汁, stalk of celery 一根芹菜
[联] vegetable 蔬菜, crunchy 脆的, salad 沙拉, soup 汤, onion 洋葱, carrot 胡萝卜, stalk 茎, dip 蘸酱, peanut butter 花生酱, crisp 脆的
[记] 不可数。celery stick 常作健康零食

**mustard**
英/美 /ˈmʌstəd/
n. 芥末酱
[义] A yellow or brown spicy sauce made from mustard seeds, eaten with meat and sandwiches. 芥末籽制成的黄或棕色辛辣酱，配肉和三明治（English mustard 英式芥末，mustard seed 芥末籽，Dijon mustard 第戎芥末）。不可数/可数。
[例] ① Pass the mustard—I like a bit of heat on my hot dog. 递一下芥末，热狗我想加点辣。 ② Mix mustard and honey for a simple salad dressing. 芥末加蜂蜜可做简易沙拉酱。

[记] 不可数/可数。cut the mustard 俚语指「达到标准/胜任」

**mayonnaise**
英/美 /ˌmeɪəˈneɪz/
n. 蛋黄酱
[义] A thick cold sauce made from egg, oil, and vinegar, used in sandwiches and salads. 鸡蛋、油、醋调成的浓稠冷酱，用于三明治和沙拉（light mayonnaise 低卡蛋黄酱，mayo 蛋黄酱〔口语〕，homemade mayonnaise 自制蛋黄酱）。不可数。
[例] ① She spread mayonnaise on the bread before adding the tuna. 面包先抹蛋黄酱再放金枪鱼。 ② Store opened mayonnaise in the fridge and use it within a month. 开封蛋黄酱放冰箱，一个月内用完。

**ketchup**
英/美 /ˈketʃəp/
n. 番茄酱
[义] A thick sweet and tangy red sauce made from tomatoes, eaten with fries and burgers. 番茄制成的浓稠甜酸红酱，配薯条和汉堡（tomato ketchup 番茄酱，ketchup bottle 番茄酱瓶，squeeze ketchup 挤番茄酱）。不可数。

[联] sauce 酱汁, tomato 番茄, fries 薯条, burger 汉堡, sweet 甜的, tangy 酸爽的, bottle 瓶子, condiment 调味品, red 红色的, dip 蘸
[记] 不可数。亦拼 catsup，ketchup 更常见

**gravy**
英/美 /ˈɡreɪvi/
n. 肉汁
[义] A sauce made from the juices of cooked meat, often served with roast dinners. 用烤肉汁液制成的酱汁，常配周日烤肉大餐（gravy boat 肉汁壶，pour gravy 浇肉汁，onion gravy 洋葱肉汁）。不可数/可数。
[例] ① Pour hot gravy over the mashed potatoes and turkey. 热肉汁浇在土豆泥和火鸡上。 ② The Sunday roast is not complete without gravy. 周日烤肉没有肉汁就不完整。

[联] sauce 酱汁, meat 肉, roast 烤, turkey 火鸡, potato 土豆, dinner 晚餐, pour 浇, thick 浓稠的, savoury 咸鲜的, biscuit 美式软饼（配 gravy 的经典组合）
[记] 不可数/可数。gravy train 俚语指「轻松捞钱的美差」

**olive**
英/美 /ˈɒlɪv/
n. 橄榄

[例] ① Top the pizza with sliced black olives. 披萨上铺黑橄榄片。 ② Drizzle olive oil over the salad before serving. 沙拉上桌前淋橄榄油。

[联] oil 油, fruit 水果, Mediterranean 地中海的, salad 沙拉, pizza 披萨, tree 树, ripe 成熟的, brine 盐水, antipasto 意式开胃小食, healthy 健康的
[记] 可数/不可数。extend an olive branch 指「示好/求和」

**peanut butter**
英/美 /ˈpiːnʌt ˌbʌtə(r)/
n. 花生酱

**stale**
英/美 /steɪl/
adj. 不新鲜的
[义] No longer fresh and pleasant to eat, especially bread and baked goods. 不再新鲜可口，尤指面包和烘焙品（stale bread 不新鲜面包，go stale 变不新鲜，stale air 闷空气〔喻〕）。比较级 staler, stalest。
[例] ① Don't use stale bread for French toast—it won't soak well. 法式吐司别用不新鲜面包，吸不住蛋奶。 ② The crackers had gone stale after the box was left open. 盒子没盖，饼干受潮不脆了。
[搭] stale bread 不新鲜面包, go stale 变不新鲜, stale food 不新鲜食物, stale taste 走味, slightly stale 略不新鲜
[联] fresh 新鲜的, rotten 腐烂的, bread 面包, biscuit 饼干, crisp 脆的, old 旧的, dry 干的, mould 发霉, spoil 变质, bin 垃圾桶
[记] 比较级 staler。stale joke 指「老掉牙的笑话」

**spoil**
英/美 /spɔɪl/
v. 变质
[义] Of food, to become bad and unsafe to eat; to cause food to go bad by poor storage. 食物变坏不宜食用；因储存不当使食物变质（food spoils 食物会坏，spoil quickly 很快变质，spoiled milk 变质牛奶）。规则动词。
[形] spoiled / spoiled / spoiling
[例] ① Fish spoils quickly if you leave it out of the fridge. 鱼不放冰箱很快就坏。 ② Check the date—the yogurt might have spoiled. 看日期，酸奶可能变质了。
[搭] food spoils 食物会坏, spoil quickly 很快变质, spoiled milk 变质牛奶, go bad 变坏, prevent spoilage 防止腐败
[联] fresh 新鲜的, rotten 腐烂的, stale 不新鲜的, fridge 冰箱, milk 牛奶, meat 肉, bacteria 细菌, waste 浪费, smell 气味, storage 储存
[记] 规则变化。spoiled child 指「宠坏的孩子」，与食物义不同

**reheat**
英/美 /ˌriːˈhiːt/
v. 重新加热

[形] reheated / reheated / reheating
[例] ① Reheat the soup on the stove, stirring occasionally. 汤放炉上复热，偶尔搅一下。 ② It's safe to reheat rice only if you cooled it quickly first. 米饭要先快速冷却再复热才安全。
[搭] reheat leftovers 复热剩菜, reheat in the microwave 用微波炉复热, reheat thoroughly 充分复热, reheat pizza 复热披萨, reheat gently 小火复热
[联] heat 加热, microwave 微波炉, leftover 剩菜, cook 烹饪, warm 加热, stove 炉灶, meal 一餐, safe 安全的, fridge 冰箱, serve 上菜
[记] 规则变化。部分食物不宜反复加热

**microwave**

n. 微波炉

[形] microwaved / microwaved / microwaving
[例] ① He microwaved last night's pasta for lunch. 他把昨晚的意面用微波炉热了当午餐。 ② The microwave beeped when the soup was hot. 汤热好了，微波炉叮一声。
[搭] microwave meal 微波餐, microwave popcorn 微波爆米花, put in the microwave 放进微波炉, microwave safe 可微波, microwave oven 微波炉
[联] oven 烤箱, heat 加热, reheat 复热, kitchen 厨房, quick 快的, leftover 剩菜, frozen 冷冻的, cook 烹饪, convenient 方便的, radiation 辐射
[记] 可数/动词。microwave safe 指容器「可微波」

[例] ① I need to pick up groceries on the way home. 回家路上得买点菜。 ② Milk and eggs are at the top of my grocery list. 牛奶和鸡蛋在我购物清单最上面。

**brunch**
英/美 /brʌntʃ/

[义] A meal eaten in the late morning, combining breakfast and lunch. 上午晚些时候吃的一餐，兼早餐与午餐（Sunday brunch 周日早午餐，brunch menu 早午餐菜单，go for brunch 去吃早午餐）。可数/不可数。
[例] ① We booked brunch at a café by the river. 我们在河边一家咖啡馆订了早午餐。 ② Brunch usually includes eggs, pastries, and coffee. 早午餐常有鸡蛋、点心和咖啡。

**protein**

n. 蛋白质
[义] A nutrient in food from meat, fish, eggs, and beans that the body needs to build muscle. 来自肉、鱼、蛋、豆等食物、身体用以构建肌肉的营养素（protein shake 蛋白奶昔，high-protein 高蛋白，source of protein 蛋白质来源）。不可数/可数。

**chew**

[义] To bite food repeatedly with the teeth before swallowing. 吞咽前用牙齿反复咬碎食物（chew slowly 慢慢嚼，chew gum 嚼口香糖，chew thoroughly 充分咀嚼）。规则动词。
[形] chewed / chewed / chewing

**bite**

n. 一口
v. 咬
[义] To cut into food with the teeth; a small amount taken in one mouthful. 用牙咬入食物；一口之量（take a bite 咬一口，bite-sized 一口大小，bite into 咬入）。不规则动词 bit / bitten / biting。
[例] ① She took a bite of the apple and smiled. 她咬了一口苹果，笑了。 ② Cut the meat into bite-sized pieces for the kids. 把肉切成孩子一口大小。

**swallow**
英/美 /ˈswɒləʊ/
v. 吞咽
n. 吞； swallow
[义] To make food or drink go down the throat into the stomach. 使食物或饮料经咽喉进入胃（swallow whole 整吞，hard to swallow 难以下咽，swallow quickly 快速吞咽）。规则动词。
[形] swallowed / swallowed / swallowing

**dine**

v. 进餐；用餐
[义] To eat dinner, especially in a formal or restaurant setting. 吃正餐，尤指正式或餐厅场合（dine out 外出就餐，dine in 在家用餐，wine and dine 宴请款客）。规则动词。
[形] dined / dined / dining
[例] ① We dined at a small Italian restaurant by the harbour. 我们在港口一家小意大利馆用了晚餐。 ② They prefer to dine in on weeknights to save money. 工作日晚间他们更在家吃以省钱。

n. 烧烤；烧烤架
v. 烧烤
[义] A meal or party where food is cooked outdoors over fire or coals; the grill used; to cook this way. 户外明火或炭火烤肉的一餐或聚会；烤架；以此法烹饪（barbecue sauce 烧烤酱，have a barbecue 办烧烤，barbecue ribs 烤肋排）。可数/规则动词。
[形] barbecued / barbecued / barbecuing
[例] ① We're having a barbecue in the garden on Saturday. 周六我们在花园烧烤。 ② Barbecue the chicken slowly so it stays juicy. 鸡肉慢慢烤，保持多汁。
[搭] barbecue sauce 烧烤酱, have a barbecue 办烧烤, barbecue ribs 烤肋排, barbecue grill 烧烤架, backyard barbecue 后院烧烤
[联] grill 烧烤, meat 肉, outdoor 户外的, summer 夏天, smoke 烟, sauce 酱汁, party 聚会, charcoal 木炭, picnic 野餐, ribs 肋排
[记] 可数/动词。英式常拼 barbeque；barbecue 更标准。BBQ 为缩写

**delivery**
英/美 /dɪˈlɪvəri/
n. 外送
[义] The bringing of food from a restaurant or shop to your home. 餐厅或商店把食物送到你家（food delivery 外卖配送，delivery driver 配送员，free delivery 免费配送）。可数/不可数。
[例] ① We ordered pizza delivery because it was raining. 下雨了我们叫披萨外送。 ② Delivery usually takes forty-five minutes at peak times. 高峰时段外送通常要四十五分钟。

[记] 可数/不可数。与 takeaway 近义：delivery 强调「送到家」。delivery 多义：分娩/投递

**starve**
英/美 /stɑːv/
v. 挨饿
[义] To suffer or die from lack of food; to feel very hungry (informal). 因缺食物而受苦或死亡；口语指「饿极了」（starving hungry 饿极了，starve to death 饿死，be starving 饿坏了）。规则动词。
[形] starved / starved / starving
[例] ① Millions starved during the famine. 饥荒中数百万人挨饿。 ② I'm starving—let's grab something to eat. 我饿坏了，去吃点东西吧。
[搭] starving hungry 饿极了, starve to death 饿死, be starving 饿坏了, starve for 渴望, half-starved 半饿半饱
[联] hungry 饥饿的, hunger 饥饿, food 食物, eat 吃, famine 饥荒, malnutrition 营养不良, appetite 食欲, meal 一餐, feed 喂养, thirst 口渴
[记] 规则变化。I'm starving 口语极常见，并非真「快饿死」。starving artist 指「穷困艺术家」

**pantry**
英/美 /ˈpæntri/
n. 食品储藏室
[义] A small room or cupboard where food, dishes, and cooking supplies are kept. 存放食物、餐具和烹饪用品的小房间或柜（kitchen pantry 厨房储藏室，pantry staples 常备食品，stock the pantry 储藏室备货）。可数。
[例] ① Keep dry goods in the pantry away from heat and moisture. 干货放储藏室，远离热和潮。 ② She checked the pantry before writing the shopping list. 写购物清单前她先看了储藏室存货。

[联] kitchen 厨房, cupboard 橱柜, storage 储存, shelf 架子, ingredient 食材, grocery 食品杂货, flour 面粉, canned 罐装的, organize 整理, stock 备货
[记] 可数：a pantry。pantry staples 指「常备粮」。美式家庭常见 walk-in pantry

**burger**
英/美 /ˈbɜːɡə(r)/
n. 汉堡（包）
[义] A flat round patty of minced meat, or a plant-based alternative, served in a bread roll with salad and sauce. 绞肉或植物肉压成的圆饼，夹入面包卷，配生菜与酱（cheeseburger 芝士汉堡，veggie burger 素汉堡，burger and fries 汉堡配薯条）。可数。
[例] ① We grabbed burgers from a food truck at the festival. 我们在音乐节从餐车买了汉堡。 ② The restaurant makes its own veggie burgers with black beans. 这家餐厅用黑豆自制素汉堡。
[搭] cheeseburger 芝士汉堡, veggie burger 素汉堡, burger and fries 汉堡配薯条, beef burger 牛肉汉堡, burger bun 汉堡胚
[联] sandwich 三明治, fries 薯条, meat 肉, grill 烧烤, fast food 快餐, bun 面包胚, cheese 奶酪, takeaway 外卖, restaurant 餐厅, patty 肉饼

**fries**
英/美 /fraɪz/
n. 薯条（美式）
[义] Strips of potato deep-fried until crisp, often served with burgers or fish. 土豆切条油炸至酥脆，常配汉堡或鱼（French fries 法式薯条，curly fries 卷曲薯条，side of fries 一份薯条）。常用复数。
[例] ① Can I get a burger with a large fries? 能来个大份薯条配汉堡吗？ ② British chips are thicker than American fries. 英式 chips 比美式 fries 更粗。
[搭] French fries 法式薯条, curly fries 卷曲薯条, side of fries 一份薯条, crispy fries 脆薯条, salt the fries 给薯条撒盐

**pancake**
英/美 /ˈpænkeɪk/
n. 薄煎饼
[义] A flat round cake made from batter and fried in a pan, eaten for breakfast or dessert. 面糊摊成圆饼在平底锅里煎，作早餐或甜点（stack of pancakes 一叠薄饼，pancake batter 薄饼面糊，pancake syrup 枫糖浆）。可数。
[例] ① She flipped the pancake when bubbles appeared on top. 表面冒泡时她把薄饼翻面。 ② We had pancakes with maple syrup on Sunday morning. 周日早上我们吃薄饼配枫糖浆。

n. 寿司

[例] ① We ordered a mixed platter of sushi to share. 我们点了一盘什锦寿司分着吃。 ② Good sushi depends on fresh fish and well-seasoned rice. 好寿司靠鲜鱼和调味到位的米饭。

**dumpling**

n. 饺子；团子
[义] A small ball of dough wrapped around a filling and boiled, steamed, or fried; also plain dough balls in stew. 面皮包馅后煮、蒸或炸；亦指炖菜里的面团团子（steamed dumplings 蒸饺，soup dumplings 小笼包，chicken and dumplings 鸡肉炖团子）。可数。
[例] ① The dumplings were steamed in bamboo baskets. 饺子用竹笼蒸的。 ② She learned to fold dumplings from her grandmother. 她跟祖母学包饺子。

n. 黄瓜

[例] ① The family carved the roast turkey at the holiday table. 全家在节日餐桌上切烤火鸡。 ② Ground turkey is a leaner alternative to beef mince. 火鸡绞肉比牛肉末更瘦。

**herb**

**cinnamon**

[例] ① Add chilli flakes a little at a time—you can always add more. 辣椒片一点一点加，不够再加。 ② The soup was too mild, so he stirred in extra chilli. 汤太淡，他又加了辣椒。

[联] spicy 辛辣的, pepper 胡椒, hot 辣的, sauce 酱汁, spice 香料, flavour 风味, mild 温和的, curry 咖喱, Mexican 墨西哥的, heat 辣度

**soy sauce**

**pickle**
