n. 腌渍菜；酸黄瓜（美式）
v. 腌渍
[义] A vegetable preserved in vinegar or salt water; in American English, often a pickled cucumber. 用醋或盐水保藏的蔬菜；美式常指酸黄瓜（dill pickle 莳萝腌黄瓜，pickle jar 腌菜罐，in a pickle 陷入困境〔喻〕）。可数/规则动词。
[形] pickled / pickled / pickling
[例] ① She put extra pickles on her burger. 她汉堡上加了更多酸黄瓜。 ② You can pickle onions and cucumbers at home. 在家可以腌洋葱和黄瓜。
[搭] dill pickle 莳萝腌黄瓜, pickle jar 腌菜罐, pickled vegetables 腌蔬菜, sweet pickle 甜口腌菜, pickle juice 腌菜汁
[联] vinegar 醋, cucumber 黄瓜, sour 酸的, jar 罐子, preserve 保藏, sandwich 三明治, burger 汉堡, crisp 脆的, salt 盐, ferment 发酵
[记] 可数/动词。英式 pickle 可指多种腌渍菜；美式常专指酸黄瓜。in a pickle 指「陷入困境」

**pudding**
英/美 /ˈpʊdɪŋ/
n. 布丁；甜点
[义] A sweet soft dessert, especially in British English; in American English, often a thick creamy dessert or a sausage-like dish (black pudding). 英式指软甜甜点；美式常指浓稠奶油甜点，或血肠等（rice pudding 米布丁，chocolate pudding 巧克力布丁，sticky toffee pudding 粘太妃布丁）。可数/不可数。
[例] ① For pudding we had apple crumble with custard. 甜点我们吃了苹果酥配卡士达酱。 ② The kids love chocolate pudding after dinner. 孩子晚饭后爱吃巧克力布丁。
[搭] rice pudding 米布丁, chocolate pudding 巧克力布丁, sticky toffee pudding 粘太妃布丁, bread pudding 面包布丁, for pudding 作为甜点
[联] dessert 甜点, sweet 甜的, cream 奶油, cake 蛋糕, custard 卡士达酱, British 英国的, dinner 晚餐, milk 牛奶, rice 米饭, bake 烘烤
[记] 可数/不可数。英式 pudding 泛指餐后甜食；美式 pudding 多指类似慕斯的甜点

**supper**
英/美 /ˈsʌpə(r)/
n. 晚饭；夜宵
[义] An evening meal, especially a light one or one eaten at home; in some regions, the main meal of the day. 晚间的一餐，尤指便餐或在家吃的；部分地区亦指一天主餐（light supper 便餐，supper time 晚饭时间，have supper 吃晚饭）。可数/不可数。
[例] ① We had a light supper of soup and bread. 我们晚饭简单吃了汤和面包。 ② Supper is ready—wash your hands and come down. 晚饭好了，洗手下来吧。
[搭] light supper 便餐, supper time 晚饭时间, have supper 吃晚饭, supper club 晚餐俱乐部, Sunday supper 周日晚餐
[联] dinner 晚餐, meal 一餐, evening 傍晚, breakfast 早餐, lunch 午餐, home 家, family 家庭, light 便餐, eat 吃, kitchen 厨房
[记] 可数/不可数。部分美式地区 supper 指家常晚饭，dinner 更正式

**appetizer**
英/美 /ˈæpɪtaɪzə(r)/
n. 开胃菜
[义] A small dish served before the main course to stimulate the appetite. 主菜前的小碟，用以开胃（appetizer platter 开胃菜拼盘，share appetizers 分享开胃菜，order appetizers 点开胃菜）。可数。英式 starter。
[例] ① We shared appetizers before the main course arrived. 主菜上来前我们先分食开胃菜。 ② The menu lists soups and salads under appetizers. 菜单把汤和沙拉列在开胃菜项下。
[搭] appetizer platter 开胃菜拼盘, share appetizers 分享开胃菜, order appetizers 点开胃菜, light appetizer 轻开胃菜, appetizer menu 开胃菜菜单
[联] starter 开胃菜（英式）, course 一道菜, menu 菜单, restaurant 餐厅, snack 小食, share 分享, main dish 主菜, soup 汤, salad 沙拉, hungry 饥饿的
[记] 可数：an appetizer。美式 appetizer；英式 starter

**course**
英/美 /kɔːs/
n. 一道菜
[义] One of the separate parts of a meal served in order, such as starter, main, or dessert. 按顺序上桌的餐食部分，如开胃、主菜或甜点（main course 主菜，three-course meal 三道菜套餐，course menu 分餐菜单）。可数。
[例] ① The main course was grilled salmon with vegetables. 主菜是烤三文鱼配蔬菜。 ② They booked a five-course tasting menu for the anniversary. 他们为纪念日订了五道式品鉴菜单。
[搭] main course 主菜, three-course meal 三道菜套餐, first course 第一道菜, dessert course 甜点道, set course 固定套餐
[联] meal 一餐, dish 菜肴, appetizer 开胃菜, dessert 甜点, menu 菜单, serve 上菜, restaurant 餐厅, order 点餐, dinner 晚餐, portion 一份
[记] 可数：a course。course 多义：课程/路线/进程。of course 与「一道菜」无关

**feed**
英/美 /fiːd/
v. 喂养；进食
n. 饲料；一餐（方言）
[义] To give food to a person or animal; to eat (informal, especially of babies or animals). 给人或动物提供食物；进食（口语，尤指婴儿或动物）（feed the baby 喂宝宝，feed on 以……为食，breastfeed 母乳喂养）。不规则动词 fed / fed / feeding。
[例] ① Remember to feed the cat before you leave. 走之前记得喂猫。 ② The restaurant feeds hundreds of customers every lunch hour. 这家餐厅午餐时段要接待数百食客。
[搭] feed the baby 喂宝宝, feed on 以……为食, feed the family 养家, feed hungry 喂饱, feed well 喂好
[联] food 食物, eat 吃, meal 一餐, hunger 饥饿, animal 动物, baby 婴儿, nourish 滋养, cook 烹饪, grow 生长, farmer 农民
[记] 不规则：feed / fed / fed。feedback 指「反馈」，与喂养义不同

**nourish**
英/美 /ˈnʌrɪʃ/
v. 滋养；提供营养
[义] To provide food or substances that people, animals, or plants need to grow and stay healthy. 提供人、动物或植物生长与健康所需的食物或物质（well-nourished 营养良好，nourish the body 滋养身体，nourish with 以……滋养）。规则动词。
[形] nourished / nourished / nourishing
[例] ① A balanced diet nourishes both body and mind. 均衡饮食滋养身心。 ② Soup and broth can nourish you when you're ill. 生病时喝汤能补养身体。
[源] 源自拉丁语 *nutrire*（滋养），与 nutrition, nutrient 同族。
[族] nutrition 营养, nutrient 营养素, nourishing 有营养的
[搭] well-nourished 营养良好, nourish the body 滋养身体, nourishing food 有营养的食物, nourish with 以……滋养, poorly nourished 营养不良
[联] food 食物, nutrition 营养, healthy 健康的, feed 喂养, grow 生长, vitamin 维生素, protein 蛋白质, diet 饮食, body 身体, malnutrition 营养不良
[记] 规则变化。比 feed 更书面，强调「提供营养」

**vegan**
英/美 /ˈviːɡən/
n. 纯素食者
adj. 纯素的
[义] A person who eats no animal products at all, including dairy and eggs; food made without animal products. 完全不吃动物制品（含蛋奶）的人；不含动物制品的食物（vegan diet 纯素饮食，go vegan 改吃纯素，vegan cheese 纯素奶酪）。可数。
[例] ① The café labels all vegan options on the menu. 咖啡馆在菜单上标出所有纯素选项。 ② She went vegan for environmental and health reasons. 她出于环境和健康原因开始纯素饮食。
[搭] vegan diet 纯素饮食, go vegan 改吃纯素, vegan cheese 纯素奶酪, vegan restaurant 纯素餐厅, plant-based vegan 植物基纯素
[联] vegetarian 素食者, diet 饮食, plant-based 植物基的, dairy 乳制品, egg 鸡蛋, meat 肉, animal 动物, organic 有机的, protein 蛋白质, ethics 伦理
[记] 可数/形容词。比 vegetarian 更严格：无蛋、无奶

**gluten**
英/美 /ˈɡluːtn/
n. 麸质；面筋
[义] A protein in wheat and some other grains that gives dough elasticity; some people avoid it for medical reasons. 小麦等谷物中的蛋白质，使面团有弹性；部分人因健康原因需避免（gluten-free 无麸质，gluten intolerance 麸质不耐，contains gluten 含麸质）。不可数。
[例] ① The label says this bread contains gluten. 标签说这面包含麸质。 ② Gluten-free pasta is easy to find in most supermarkets now. 如今大多数超市都容易买到无麸质意面。
[搭] gluten-free 无麸质, gluten intolerance 麸质不耐, contains gluten 含麸质, avoid gluten 避免麸质, gluten allergy 麸质过敏
[联] wheat 小麦, bread 面包, flour 面粉, pasta 意面, allergy 过敏, diet 饮食, celiac 乳糜泻, bake 烘烤, protein 蛋白质, label 标签
[记] 不可数。gluten-free（GF）已是超市常见分类

**cafeteria**
英/美 /ˌkæfəˈtɪəriə/
n. 自助餐厅；食堂
[义] A restaurant where you choose food from a counter and pay at the till, common in schools and workplaces. 从柜台自选食物再在收银台付款的餐厅，常见于学校与单位（school cafeteria 学校食堂，cafeteria tray 食堂餐盘，cafeteria food 食堂餐）。可数。
[例] ① We met for coffee in the office cafeteria. 我们在单位食堂喝咖啡见面。 ② The school cafeteria serves hot lunch every weekday. 学校食堂每个工作日供应热午餐。
[搭] school cafeteria 学校食堂, cafeteria tray 食堂餐盘, cafeteria food 食堂餐, office cafeteria 公司食堂, cafeteria line 食堂排队
[联] restaurant 餐厅, lunch 午餐, canteen 食堂（英式）, food 食物, tray 餐盘, school 学校, workplace 工作场所, queue 排队, coffee 咖啡, buffet 自助餐
[记] 可数：a cafeteria。美式 cafeteria；英式更常说 canteen

**bakery**
英/美 /ˈbeɪkəri/
n. 面包店；烘焙坊
[义] A shop where bread, cakes, and pastries are made and sold. 制作并出售面包、蛋糕与点心的店铺（local bakery 本地面包店，bakery fresh 面包店新鲜出炉，bakery counter 面包柜台）。可数。
[例] ① She bought a loaf of sourdough from the bakery on the corner. 她在街角面包店买了一条酸种面包。 ② The smell from the bakery drew customers in every morning. 面包店香味每天早上把顾客招进来。
[搭] local bakery 本地面包店, bakery fresh 面包店新鲜出炉, bakery counter 面包柜台, artisan bakery 手工面包店, bakery goods 烘焙食品
[联] bread 面包, cake 蛋糕, pastry 点心, bake 烘烤, flour 面粉, shop 商店, croissant 可颂, fresh 新鲜的, oven 烤箱, morning 早晨
[记] 可数：a bakery。supermarket bakery 指超市里的烘焙区

**fork**
英/美 /fɔːk/
n. 餐叉
v. 叉起
[义] A tool with a handle and pointed prongs, used for picking up food. 有柄、带尖齿，用以叉取食物的餐具（knife and fork 刀叉，salad fork 沙拉叉，forkful 一叉的量）。可数/规则动词。
[例] ① Use a fork to twirl the spaghetti. 用叉子卷意面。 ② Could you pass me a clean fork, please? 请递我一把干净叉子好吗？
[搭] knife and fork 刀叉, salad fork 沙拉叉, forkful 一叉的量, plastic fork 塑料叉, fork the food 叉取食物
[联] knife 刀, spoon 勺, chopsticks 筷子, plate 盘子, eat 吃, table 桌子, cutlery 餐具, meal 一餐, hand 手, serve 上菜
[记] 可数：a fork。fork 多义：岔路/分叉

**spoon**
英/美 /spuːn/
n. 勺子；汤匙
v. 用勺舀
[义] A tool with a small shallow bowl and a handle, used for eating or serving food. 小浅碗加柄的器具，用于进食或盛食（tablespoon 汤匙，teaspoon 茶匙，wooden spoon 木勺）。可数/规则动词。
[例] ① Stir the soup with a spoon before you taste it. 尝汤前先用勺搅一下。 ② Babies often learn to eat with a spoon before chopsticks. 婴儿常先学用勺再吃筷子。
[搭] tablespoon 汤匙, teaspoon 茶匙, wooden spoon 木勺, soup spoon 汤勺, plastic spoon 塑料勺
[联] fork 餐叉, knife 刀, chopsticks 筷子, bowl 碗, eat 吃, stir 搅拌, soup 汤, measure 计量, cutlery 餐具, serve 上菜
[记] 可数：a spoon。spoon-feed 喻「过度包办」。spoon 动词指「勺取/拥吻」

**knife**
英/美 /naɪf/
n. 刀；餐刀
v. 用刀切
[义] A tool with a blade and handle, used for cutting food. 有刃有柄的工具，用于切食物（bread knife 面包刀，steak knife 牛排刀，sharp knife 快刀）。可数/规则动词 knifes / knifed / knifing。
[例] ① Use a sharp knife to slice the tomatoes thinly. 用快刀把番茄切薄片。 ② Keep knives out of reach of young children. 刀要放在幼儿够不到的地方。
[搭] bread knife 面包刀, steak knife 牛排刀, sharp knife 快刀, butter knife 黄油刀, kitchen knife 厨刀
[联] fork 餐叉, spoon 勺, chop 切, slice 切片, cutlery 餐具, sharp 锋利的, cook 烹饪, plate 盘子, meat 肉, dangerous 危险的
[记] 复数 knives /naɪvz/。under the knife 指「动手术」，与餐刀义不同

**chopsticks**
英/美 /ˈtʃɒpstɪks/
n. 筷子
[义] A pair of thin sticks used especially in East Asia to pick up food. 一对细棍，尤在东亚用以夹取食物（use chopsticks 用筷子，disposable chopsticks 一次性筷子，chopstick rest 筷架）。复数。
[例] ① She taught her friend how to hold chopsticks properly. 她教朋友正确拿筷子。 ② The restaurant provides both chopsticks and forks. 餐厅提供筷子和叉子。
[搭] use chopsticks 用筷子, disposable chopsticks 一次性筷子, chopstick rest 筷架, wooden chopsticks 木筷, pair of chopsticks 一双筷子
[联] fork 餐叉, spoon 勺, rice 米饭, noodle 面条, Chinese 中国的, Japanese 日本的, skill 技巧, pick up 夹起, Asian 亚洲的, etiquette 礼仪
[记] 复数形式；不说 × a chopstick。pair of chopsticks 指「一双」

**plate**
英/美 /pleɪt/
n. 盘子；餐盘
v. 装盘
[义] A flat dish from which food is eaten or served. 盛放或进食用的扁平碟（dinner plate 餐盘，clean plate 干净盘子，full plate 满盘）。可数/规则动词。
[例] ① She piled vegetables onto her plate. 她把蔬菜堆满盘子。 ② The waiter cleared the empty plates from the table. 服务员收走桌上空盘。
[搭] dinner plate 餐盘, clean plate 干净盘子, empty plate 空盘, side plate 小碟, plate of food 一盘食物
[联] bowl 碗, dish 菜肴, fork 餐叉, serve 上菜, meal 一餐, table 桌子, portion 一份, restaurant 餐厅, wash 洗, ceramic 陶瓷的
[记] 可数：a plate。on a plate 喻「轻而易举」。license plate 指「车牌」，不同义

**salmon**
英/美 /ˈsæmən/
n. 三文鱼；鲑鱼
[义] A large pink-fleshed fish eaten as food, often grilled, smoked, or served raw. 大型肉质呈粉红色的鱼，常烤、熏或生食（smoked salmon 熏三文鱼，grilled salmon 烤三文鱼，salmon fillet 三文鱼排）。可数/不可数。
[例] ① The main course was grilled salmon with lemon butter. 主菜是烤三文鱼配柠檬黄油。 ② Smoked salmon on bagels is a classic brunch dish. 贝果配熏三文鱼是经典早午餐。
[搭] smoked salmon 熏三文鱼, grilled salmon 烤三文鱼, salmon fillet 三文鱼排, wild salmon 野生三文鱼, baked salmon 烤三文鱼
[联] fish 鱼, seafood 海鲜, grill 烧烤, smoke 熏, pink 粉红色的, omega-3 欧米伽-3, course 一道菜, lemon 柠檬, healthy 健康的, sushi 寿司
[记] 可数/不可数。复数 salmon 不变。farm-raised 与 wild salmon 风味与价格差异大

**duck**
英/美 /dʌk/
n. 鸭肉；鸭
[义] Meat from a duck, or the bird itself when cooked as food. 鸭子的肉，或作为食材的鸭（roast duck 烤鸭，duck breast 鸭胸，Peking duck 北京烤鸭）。可数/不可数。
[例] ① The restaurant is famous for its crispy roast duck. 这家餐厅以脆皮烤鸭闻名。 ② Duck pairs well with plum sauce or orange glaze. 鸭肉配梅酱或橙釉很搭。
[搭] roast duck 烤鸭, duck breast 鸭胸, Peking duck 北京烤鸭, duck sauce 鸭酱, crispy duck 脆皮鸭
[联] meat 肉, poultry 家禽, roast 烤, chicken 鸡肉, Chinese 中国的, feast 盛宴, fat 脂肪, crispy 脆的, orange 橙子, plum 梅子
[记] 可数/不可数。duck 多义：鸭子/躲避/扎入水中。与 chicken 相比 duck 更肥、风味更浓

**ramen**
英/美 /ˈrɑːmən/
n. 拉面；日式汤面
[义] A Japanese noodle soup dish with broth, often topped with egg, pork, and vegetables. 日式汤面，常有汤、鸡蛋、猪肉与蔬菜（instant ramen 方便面，ramen shop 拉面店，bowl of ramen 一碗拉面）。不可数/可数。
[例] ① We warmed up with bowls of hot ramen after the rain. 雨后我们喝热拉面暖身。 ② Instant ramen is cheap but often high in salt. 方便面便宜但常含盐量高。
[搭] instant ramen 方便面, ramen shop 拉面店, bowl of ramen 一碗拉面, pork ramen 豚骨拉面, spicy ramen 辣拉面
[联] noodle 面条, soup 汤, Japanese 日本的, broth 高汤, egg 鸡蛋, pork 猪肉, chopsticks 筷子, cheap 便宜的, salty 咸的, comfort food 慰藉食物
[记] 不可数/可数。源自日语；instant ramen 是全球学生党 staple

**taco**
英/美 /ˈtækəʊ/
n. 塔可；墨西哥卷饼
[义] A Mexican dish of a folded tortilla filled with meat, beans, cheese, and salsa. 墨西哥玉米饼折叠，内填肉、豆、奶酪与莎莎酱（fish taco 鱼肉塔可，taco shell 塔可壳，taco night 塔可之夜）。可数。
[例] ① We set up a taco bar with different fillings for guests. 我们摆塔可自助台，馅料多种任选。 ② Hard-shell tacos can break when you bite into them. 硬壳塔可一咬容易碎。
[搭] fish taco 鱼肉塔可, taco shell 塔可壳, taco bar 塔可自助台, beef taco 牛肉塔可, taco night 塔可之夜
[联] Mexican 墨西哥的, tortilla 玉米饼, salsa 莎莎酱, cheese 奶酪, bean 豆子, spicy 辛辣的, street food 街头小吃, fold 折叠, crunch 脆响, lime 青柠
[记] 可数：a taco。taco 近二十年进入全球快餐菜单

**donut**
英/美 /ˈdəʊnʌt/
n. 甜甜圈（美式）
[义] A small sweet ring-shaped cake, fried and often glazed or filled. 小环形甜蛋糕，油炸，常淋糖霜或填馅（glazed donut 糖霜甜甜圈，donut shop 甜甜圈店，jelly donut 果酱甜甜圈）。可数。亦拼 doughnut。
[例] ① She bought a dozen donuts for the office meeting. 她买了一打甜甜圈带去开会。 ② Fresh donuts are best eaten on the day they are made. 新鲜甜甜圈最好当天吃完。

[记] 可数：a donut。donut / doughnut 两拼均可。coffee and donuts 是美式经典组合

n. 玛芬；松饼

[例] ① She baked blueberry muffins for the kids' lunch boxes. 她烤了蓝莓玛芬放进孩子午餐盒。 ② English muffins are split and toasted before serving. 英式玛芬要切开烤热再上桌。

[例] ① Top the warm waffles with butter and maple syrup. 热华夫饼上抹黄油、淋枫糖浆。 ② Belgian waffles are thicker and deeper-pocketed. 比利时华夫更厚、格更深。

n. 柠檬水

[例] ① We met at a café near the museum for lunch. 我们在博物馆旁一家咖啡馆吃午饭。 ② Paris is famous for its café culture and sidewalk seating. 巴黎以咖啡馆文化与路边座闻名。

n. 肉铺；屠夫

n. 一小口

[形] sipped / sipped / sipping

[例] ① The recipe uses less dairy for a lighter sauce. 这食谱少用乳制品，酱汁更轻。 ② If you're lactose intolerant, choose dairy-free options. 乳糖不耐就选无乳制品选项。

**oyster**
英/美 /ˈɔɪstə(r)/
n. 牡蛎；生蚝
[义] A shellfish with a rough shell, eaten raw or cooked, often on the half shell. 壳粗糙的贝类，可生吃或熟吃，常半壳上桌（raw oysters 生蚝刺身，oyster bar 生蚝吧，fried oysters 炸生蚝）。可数。
[例] ① We tried raw oysters with lemon at the seaside bar. 我们在海边酒吧试了淋柠檬的生蚝。 ② The world is your oyster 是一句常用比喻。 ③ The menu warns that raw oysters carry food safety risks. 菜单提醒生蚝有食品安全风险。
[搭] raw oysters 生蚝刺身, oyster bar 生蚝吧, fried oysters 炸生蚝, oyster sauce 蚝油, fresh oysters 新鲜生蚝
[联] seafood 海鲜, shell 壳, raw 生的, lemon 柠檬, bar 吧台, sauce 酱汁, expensive 昂贵的, ocean 海洋, pearl 珍珠, risky 有风险的
[记] 可数：an oyster / oysters。the world is your oyster 指「前途无量」。oyster sauce 是「蚝油」，与生蚝本身不同

**parsley**
英/美 /ˈpɑːsli/
n. 欧芹
[义] A herb with curly or flat green leaves, used to garnish and flavour food. 叶卷曲或扁平的绿色香草，用于点缀与增味（fresh parsley 新鲜欧芹，chopped parsley 欧芹碎，parsley garnish 欧芹点缀）。不可数。

[联] herb 香草, garnish 点缀, green 绿色的, chop 切, soup 汤, fish 鱼, Italian 意大利的, leaf 叶子, fresh 新鲜的, plate 盘子
[记] 不可数。作 garnish 极常见。与 cilantro（香菜）不同，parsley 更偏「西式点缀」

**basil**

n. 罗勒

[联] herb 香草, Italian 意大利的, tomato 番茄, pesto 青酱, pizza 披萨, pasta 意面, aromatic 芳香的, leaf 叶子, Thai 泰国的, green 绿色的
[记] 不可数。Thai basil 与 sweet basil 风味不同。basil 是 pesto 核心原料

**digest**

n. 文摘；摘要

[形] digested / digested / digesting
[例] ① Rich food can be hard to digest late at night. 油腻食物夜里难消化。 ② Give yourself time to digest what you've read. 给自己时间消化读到的内容。

**gulp**

v. 大口喝；吞咽
n. 一大口

[形] gulped / gulped / gulping
[例] ① He gulped down his coffee and ran for the bus. 他大口灌完咖啡赶公交。 ② Take small sips rather than gulping your drink. 小口吃喝，别大口灌。

**fibre**

n. 膳食纤维
[义] Parts of plant food that the body does not digest, important for healthy digestion. 植物食物中身体不消化的部分，有助健康消化（dietary fibre 膳食纤维，high-fibre 高纤维，fibre-rich 富含纤维）。不可数。美式拼写 fiber。
[例] ① Whole grains and vegetables are good sources of fibre. 全谷物和蔬菜是纤维好来源。 ② A high-fibre breakfast helps you feel full longer. 高纤维早餐更扛饿。
[搭] dietary fibre 膳食纤维, high-fibre 高纤维, fibre-rich 富含纤维, source of fibre 纤维来源, add fibre 增加纤维
[联] digest 消化, vegetable 蔬菜, whole grain 全谷物, healthy 健康的, diet 饮食, fruit 水果, bean 豆子, gut 肠道, nutrition 营养, bowel 肠道
[记] 不可数。英式 fibre；美式 fiber。与「光纤 fibre optics」同形，语境消歧

**pub**
英/美 /pʌb/
n. 酒吧；小酒馆（英式）
[义] A place that serves beer and often simple meals, especially in Britain. 供应啤酒且常供简餐的场所，尤英式（local pub 当地酒馆，pub lunch 酒馆午餐，pub food 酒吧餐）。可数。
[例] ① We met friends for a pub lunch on Sunday. 周日我们在酒吧吃午饭见朋友。 ② The pub serves fish and chips and pie and mash. 这家酒馆供炸鱼薯条和派配土豆泥。
[搭] local pub 当地酒馆, pub lunch 酒馆午餐, pub food 酒吧餐, pub garden 酒馆花园, traditional pub 传统酒馆
[联] beer 啤酒, restaurant 餐厅, British 英国的, lunch 午餐, bar 吧台, meal 一餐, casual 休闲, steak 牛排, chips 薯条, social 社交
[记] 可数：a pub。public house 缩写。英式 pub culture 与美式 bar 文化有差异

**diner**
英/美 /ˈdaɪnə(r)/
n. 小餐馆（美式）
[义] A small cheap restaurant, often open late, serving American comfort food. 小型廉价餐馆，常营业至深夜，供美式家常 comfort food（roadside diner 路边小馆，diner breakfast 小馆早餐，classic diner 经典小馆）。可数。
[例] ① They stopped at a roadside diner for pancakes and coffee. 他们在路边小馆停了，吃薄饼喝咖啡。 ② Diners are icons of mid-century American road culture. 小餐馆是中世纪美国公路文化图标。
[搭] roadside diner 路边小馆, diner breakfast 小馆早餐, classic diner 经典小馆, local diner 当地小馆, all-night diner 通宵小馆
[联] restaurant 餐厅, breakfast 早餐, coffee 咖啡, pancake 薄饼, burger 汉堡, American 美国的, cheap 便宜的, late 深夜, road 公路, milkshake 奶昔
[记] 可数：a diner。亦指「就餐者」，同形异义。diner 与 fine dining 相对

**garnish**
英/美 /ˈɡɑːnɪʃ/
v. 点缀；装饰
n. 装饰菜
[义] To add a small amount of food to a dish to make it look or taste better. 在菜上加少量食物以增色或增味（garnish with parsley 撒欧芹点缀，lemon garnish 柠檬装饰）。规则动词/可数。
[形] garnished / garnished / garnishing
[例] ① Garnish the soup with a swirl of cream. 汤上淋一圈奶油点缀。 ② A lemon wedge is a common garnish for fish. 柠檬角是鱼类常见装饰。
[搭] garnish with parsley 撒欧芹点缀, lemon garnish 柠檬装饰, garnish a dish 点缀菜肴, fresh garnish 新鲜点缀, simple garnish 简单点缀
[联] parsley 欧芹, plate 盘子, serve 上菜, decorate 装饰, lemon 柠檬, herb 香草, presentation 摆盘, chef 厨师, soup 汤, fish 鱼
[记] 可数/动词。garnish 法律义「扣工资还债」，与烹饪无关

**melt**
英/美 /melt/
v. 融化；熔化
n. 融化
[义] To become liquid through heat; to make cheese or butter soft by heating. 受热变液体；加热使奶酪或黄油变软（melt butter 化黄油，melt cheese 化奶酪，melt in the mouth 入口即化）。规则动词/可数。
[形] melted / melted / melting
[例] ① Melt the butter in a pan before adding the eggs. 平底锅里先化黄油再加鸡蛋。 ② Good chocolate should melt smoothly on your tongue. 好巧克力应在舌上顺滑融化。
[搭] melt butter 化黄油, melt cheese 化奶酪, melt in the mouth 入口即化, melt chocolate 化巧克力, slowly melt 慢慢融化
[联] butter 黄油, cheese 奶酪, heat 加热, chocolate 巧克力, warm 温热, liquid 液体, cook 烹饪, pan 锅, soft 软的, grill 烧烤
[记] 规则变化。meltdown 原指「熔毁」，喻「崩溃」。烤芝士三明治靠融化奶酪成菜

**spread**
英/美 /spred/
n. 涂抹酱；涂开
v. 涂抹；摊开
[义] A soft food put on bread; to cover bread or a surface with such food. 涂面包的软质食物；把这类食物涂开（cheese spread 涂抹奶酪酱，spread butter 抹黄油，chocolate spread 巧克力酱）。可数/规则动词。
[形] spread / spread / spreading
[例] ① She spread jam thinly on the toast. 她在吐司上薄薄抹果酱。 ② Nut spreads are popular on breakfast tables. 坚果酱在早餐桌上很受欢迎。
[搭] cheese spread 涂抹奶酪酱, spread butter 抹黄油, chocolate spread 巧克力酱, spread on bread 抹在面包上, thick spread 厚涂酱
[联] butter 黄油, jam 果酱, bread 面包, toast 吐司, peanut butter 花生酱, sandwich 三明治, knife 刀, thin 薄的, breakfast 早餐, layer 一层
[记] 不规则动词：spread / spread / spread。spread 多义：传播/蔓延/价差

**squid**
英/美 /skwɪd/
n. 鱿鱼；乌贼
[义] A sea animal with a soft body and ten arms, eaten as seafood. 软体、十腕的海生动物，作海鲜食用（fried squid 炸鱿鱼，grilled squid 烤鱿鱼，squid ink 鱿鱼墨）。可数/不可数。
[例] ① We shared a plate of crispy fried squid at the tapas bar. 我们在小食酒吧分了一盘脆炸鱿鱼。 ② Squid cooks quickly, so don't overcook it. 鱿鱼熟得快，别煮过头。
[搭] fried squid 炸鱿鱼, grilled squid 烤鱿鱼, squid ink 鱿鱼墨, fresh squid 新鲜鱿鱼, calamari 意式炸鱿鱼
[联] seafood 海鲜, octopus 章鱼, fry 油炸, grill 烧烤, ocean 海洋, tender 嫩的, ink 墨汁, restaurant 餐厅, crispy 脆的, Mediterranean 地中海的
[记] 可数/不可数。复数 squid / squids。calamari 多指餐厅里的炸鱿鱼圈

**cherry**
英/美 /ˈtʃeri/
n. 樱桃
[义] A small round red or black fruit with a stone; also the tree it grows on. 小而圆的红或黑核果；亦指樱桃树（fresh cherries 新鲜樱桃，cherry pie 樱桃派，maraschino cherry 酒渍樱桃）。可数/不可数。
[例] ① She topped the cake with fresh cherries. 她在蛋糕上放了新鲜樱桃。 ② Cherry season is short, so we freeze some every year. 樱桃季短，我们每年都冻一些。
[搭] fresh cherries 新鲜樱桃, cherry pie 樱桃派, maraschino cherry 酒渍樱桃, cherry blossom 樱花, black cherry 黑樱桃
[联] fruit 水果, sweet 甜的, red 红色的, stone 核, summer 夏天, pie 派, dessert 甜点, tree 树, jam 果酱, ripe 成熟的

**pear**
英/美 /peə(r)/
n. 梨
[义] A sweet fruit that is narrow at the top and wider at the bottom. 上窄下宽的甜水果（ripe pear 熟梨，pear juice 梨汁，pear tart 梨挞）。可数/不可数。
[例] ① A ripe pear should give slightly near the stem. 熟梨在蒂部按下去应略软。 ② She sliced pears into the salad for sweetness. 她把梨片放进沙拉增甜。

**raspberry**
英/美 /ˈrɑːzbəri/
n. 树莓；覆盆子
[义] A small soft red or black berry made of many tiny parts. 由许多小粒组成的软红或黑浆果（fresh raspberries 新鲜树莓，raspberry jam 树莓酱，raspberry sauce 树莓酱）。可数/不可数。
[例] ① Raspberries bruise easily, so handle them gently. 树莓易压伤，要轻拿。 ② Raspberry sauce pairs well with chocolate dessert. 树莓酱配巧克力甜点很搭。

[联] berry 浆果, fruit 水果, sweet 甜的, tart 酸, jam 果酱, dessert 甜点, red 红色的, summer 夏天, delicate 易损, antioxidant 抗氧化
[记] 可数/不可数。复数 raspberries。英式 raspberry 亦指「吐舌嘲讽声」，与水果无关

**cumin**
英/美 /ˈkjuːmɪn/
n. 孜然；小茴香
[义] A spice with a warm, earthy taste, used in curry, Mexican, and Middle Eastern food. 带暖香、土味的香料，用于咖喱、墨西哥与中东菜（ground cumin 孜然粉，cumin seeds 孜然粒，cumin powder 孜然粉）。不可数。
[例] ① Toast cumin seeds briefly to release their aroma. 孜然粒略烘出香气。 ② A pinch of cumin lifts the flavour of bean stew. 一小撮孜然能提炖豆的风味。
[搭] ground cumin 孜然粉, cumin seeds 孜然粒, cumin powder 孜然粉, toasted cumin 烘烤孜然, add cumin 加孜然
[联] spice 香料, curry 咖喱, Mexican 墨西哥的, seed 种子, earthy 土香, bean 豆子, aroma 香气, Middle Eastern 中东的, powder 粉末, stew 炖菜
[记] 不可数。与 caraway（葛缕子）不同，cumin 更偏咖喱/墨西哥风味。拼写勿与 cummin 旧式混

**nutmeg**
英/美 /ˈnʌtmeɡ/
n. 肉豆蔻
[义] A spice made from the seed of a tropical tree, used in baking and savoury dishes. 热带树种子磨成的香料，用于烘焙与咸口菜（ground nutmeg 肉豆蔻粉，nutmeg grater 肉豆蔻磨，pinch of nutmeg 一小撮肉豆蔻）。不可数。
[例] ① Grate a little nutmeg over the béchamel sauce. 在白酱上磨一点肉豆蔻。 ② Nutmeg is common in pumpkin pie and eggnog. 肉豆蔻常见于南瓜派与蛋酒。

[联] spice 香料, cinnamon 肉桂, bake 烘烤, dessert 甜点, grate 磨碎, warm 暖香, pumpkin 南瓜, sauce 酱汁, holiday 节日, aromatic 芳香的

**quench**

v. 解渴；熄灭
[义] To satisfy thirst by drinking; to put out fire or strong desire (figurative). 通过饮水解渴；喻指「熄灭/满足强烈欲望」。规则动词。
[形] quenched / quenched / quenching
[例] ① A cold drink quenched his thirst after the hike. 徒步后一杯冷饮解了他的渴。 ② Nothing quenches thirst like plain water on a hot day. 大热天没什么比白水更解渴。

[例] ① Store leftovers in the refrigerator within two hours. 剩菜两小时内进冰箱。 ② The refrigerator is full after the weekly grocery run. 每周采购后冰箱塞满了。

**oven**

n. 烤箱

n. 肉汤；清汤

[例] ① Sip hot broth when you have a cold. 感冒时喝热清汤。 ② The recipe starts with homemade chicken broth. 这菜谱从自制鸡汤底开始。

**stock**

n. 高汤；库存
[义] Liquid made by boiling bones, meat, or vegetables, used as a base for soup and sauce. 煮骨、肉或蔬菜得到的液体，作汤与酱底（chicken stock 鸡汤底，vegetable stock 蔬菜高汤，beef stock 牛肉高汤）。不可数/可数。
[例] ① Good risotto begins with warm chicken stock. 好的意式烩饭从温热的鸡汤底开始。 ② Freeze stock in cubes for quick weeknight cooking. 把高汤冻成块，平日做饭方便。

**oatmeal**

**porridge**
英/美 /ˈpɒrɪdʒ/
n. 粥；燕麦粥（英式）
[义] A hot dish made by boiling oats or other grain in milk or water. 用奶或水煮燕麦或其他谷物制成的热食（bowl of porridge 一碗燕麦粥，porridge oats 燕麦片，salty porridge 咸燕麦粥）。不可数。
[例] ① Porridge with honey keeps you full until lunch. 蜂蜜燕麦粥能扛到午饭。 ② In Scotland, porridge is a traditional breakfast staple. 在苏格兰，燕麦粥是传统早餐主食。
[搭] bowl of porridge 一碗燕麦粥, porridge oats 燕麦片, salty porridge 咸燕麦粥, hot porridge 热燕麦粥, stir porridge 搅拌燕麦粥
[联] oatmeal 燕麦, breakfast 早餐, oat 燕麦, milk 牛奶, honey 蜂蜜, warm 暖的, cereal 麦片, Scottish 苏格兰的, healthy 健康的, stir 搅拌
[记] 不可数。英式 porridge 几乎专指燕麦粥；中文「粥」更宽，还包括 rice porridge（稀饭）

**grate**
英/美 /ɡreɪt/
v. 磨碎；擦丝
n. 擦丝器
[义] To rub food against a grater to cut it into small thin pieces. 把食物在磨碎器上擦成细条或粉末（grate cheese 磨奶酪，grate lemon zest 擦柠檬皮，finely grated 磨细的）。规则动词/可数。
[形] grated / grated / grating
[例] ① Grate the Parmesan over the pasta at the table. 上桌后在意面上磨帕玛森。 ② Freshly grated ginger tastes brighter than jarred paste. 现磨姜比罐装酱更鲜。

[联] cheese 奶酪, lemon 柠檬, nutmeg 肉豆蔻, ginger 姜, kitchen 厨房, tool 工具, fine 细的, pasta 意面, zest 皮屑, shred 切丝
[记] 规则变化。grater 是工具名。grated carrot 即胡萝卜丝；pre-grated cheese 方便但易干

**mince**
英/美 /mɪns/
v. 绞碎；剁碎
n. 绞肉；肉末
[义] To cut food into very small pieces, especially meat; also the product (British mince). 把食物切极碎，尤指肉；英式亦指绞肉（mince garlic 剁蒜，minced beef 牛肉末，mince pie 圣诞果馅派）。规则动词/不可数。
[形] minced / minced / mincing
[例] ① Mince the garlic finely before adding it to the pan. 下锅前把蒜剁细。 ② In the UK, mince usually means ground beef for bolognese. 在英国，mince 常指做肉酱面的牛肉末。

[联] chop 切, garlic 大蒜, beef 牛肉, meat 肉, grind 磨碎, kitchen 厨房, cook 烹饪, pie 派, onion 洋葱, fine 细的
[记] 规则变化。美式 ground beef；英式 minced beef。mince pie 是圣诞果馅派，馅非肉末

**ladle**
英/美 /ˈleɪdl/
n. 汤勺；长柄勺
v. 舀
[义] A large deep spoon with a long handle, used for serving soup or stew. 长柄大深勺，用来盛汤或炖菜（soup ladle 汤勺，ladle soup 舀汤，serving ladle 上菜汤勺）。可数/规则动词。

[记] 可数：a ladle。动词 ladle out 指「舀出分配」。与 small spoon 相对，强调「大、深、长柄」

**cutting board**
英/美 /ˈkʌtɪŋ bɔːd/
n. 砧板
[义] A flat board on which food is cut with a knife. 用刀切菜用的砧板（wooden cutting board 木砧板，plastic cutting board 塑料砧板，chop on a cutting board 在砧板上切）。可数。
[例] ① Wash the cutting board after handling raw chicken. 处理生鸡肉后要洗砧板。 ② Use separate boards for meat and vegetables. 生肉与蔬菜分开砧板更卫生。

[记] 可数：a cutting board。英式 chopping board 同义。生熟分开是食品安全基本习惯

**allergy**
英/美 /ˈælədʒi/
n. 过敏
[义] A bad reaction to certain foods or substances, such as nuts or gluten. 对特定食物或物质的不良反应，如坚果或麸质（food allergy 食物过敏，nut allergy 坚果过敏，allergy-friendly 过敏友好）。可数/不可数。
[例] ① The menu marks dishes that contain common allergens. 菜单标注含常见过敏原的菜。 ② She has a severe peanut allergy, so we avoid shared fryers. 她花生过敏严重，我们避开共用炸锅。

[联] allergic 过敏的, nut 坚果, gluten 麸质, dairy 乳制品, reaction 反应, menu 菜单, safe 安全的, label 标签, severe 严重的, avoid 避免
[记] 可数/不可数。allergic 形容词；allergen 指「过敏原」。餐厅问 "Any allergies?" 很常见

**food poisoning**
英/美 /fuːd ˈpɔɪzənɪŋ/
n. 食物中毒
[义] Illness caused by eating food that contains harmful bacteria or toxins. 因食用含有害细菌或毒素的食物而生病（get food poisoning 食物中毒，case of food poisoning 食物中毒案例，avoid food poisoning 避免食物中毒）。不可数。
[例] ① Several guests got food poisoning from the seafood buffet. 几位客人因海鲜自助餐食物中毒。 ② Reheat leftovers properly to reduce the risk of food poisoning. 剩菜充分加热可降低食物中毒风险。

[联] sick 生病的, bacteria 细菌, spoil 变质, raw 生的, hygiene 卫生, restaurant 餐厅, stomach 胃, vomit 呕吐, safe 安全的, leftover 剩菜
[记] 不可数。summer picnic 与室温放置是常见诱因。food safety 是 broader 话题

[例] ① Massaged kale salad holds up well in lunch boxes. 按摩过的羽衣甘蓝沙拉便当里不易塌。 ② Kale chips are a trendy alternative to potato crisps. 羽衣甘蓝脆片是土豆片的时髦替代。

**clove**
英/美 /kləʊv/
n. 丁香（香料）
[义] A dried flower bud used as a spice, especially in baking and mulled drinks. 干燥花蕾作香料，尤用于烘焙与热红酒（whole cloves 整丁香，ground cloves 丁香粉，garlic clove 蒜瓣〔同形异义〕）。可数/不可数。

**guacamole**

**dough**

n. 面团
[义] A mixture of flour, water, and other ingredients used to make bread and pastry. 面粉、水及其他材料混合，用于做面包与糕点（bread dough 面包面团，pizza dough 披萨面团，knead the dough 揉面）。不可数。

**yeast**

n. 酵母
[义] A fungus used to make bread rise and to ferment beer. 使面包发酵膨胀、亦用于酿酒的真菌（active yeast 活性酵母，dry yeast 干酵母，yeast dough 酵母面团）。不可数。

**knead**
英/美 /niːd/
v. 揉（面）
[义] To press and fold dough repeatedly with your hands to develop gluten. 用手反复按压折叠面团以形成面筋。规则动词。
[形] kneaded / kneaded / kneading
[例] ① Knead the dough for ten minutes until smooth. 揉面十分钟至光滑。 ② You can knead bread dough in a stand mixer. 立式搅拌机也可揉面。

[记] 规则变化。与 need（需要）同音，拼写不同。充分 knead 决定面包组织

**poach**
英/美 /pəʊtʃ/
v. 水煮
[义] To cook food gently in liquid just below boiling point. 在略低于沸点的液体里温和烹煮（poach an egg 水煮蛋，poach fish 水煮鱼，poached chicken 水煮鸡）。规则动词。
[形] poached / poached / poaching
[例] ① Poach the eggs in simmering water with a splash of vinegar. 微沸水中加几滴醋水煮鸡蛋。 ② Poached salmon stays moist and delicate. 水煮三文鱼保持湿润细嫩。
[搭] poach an egg 水煮蛋, poach fish 水煮鱼, poached chicken 水煮鸡, poach gently 小火水煮, poached pear 水煮梨
[联] egg 鸡蛋, fish 鱼, simmer 小火炖, water 水, gentle 温和的, moist 湿润, vinegar 醋, breakfast 早餐, delicate 细嫩, liquid 液体
[记] 规则变化。poach 亦指「偷猎/挖角」，与烹饪无关。poached egg 是 brunch 经典

**sauté**

v. 嫩煎；快炒
n. 嫩煎菜
[义] To fry food quickly in a small amount of hot fat, often in a pan. 少量热油脂里快速煎炒，常用平底锅（sauté onions 炒洋葱，sauté mushrooms 炒蘑菇，sauté in butter 黄油嫩煎）。规则动词/可数。
[形] sautéed / sautéed / sautéing
[例] ① Sauté the onions until soft and golden. 洋葱嫩煎至软金黄。 ② Sautéed greens make a quick side dish. 快炒青菜是快手配菜。
[搭] sauté onions 炒洋葱, sauté mushrooms 炒蘑菇, sauté in butter 黄油嫩煎, sauté garlic 炒大蒜, lightly sauté 轻煎
[联] fry 煎, pan 锅, butter 黄油, onion 洋葱, quick 快的, heat 加热, garlic 大蒜, mushroom 蘑菇, oil 油, stir 翻炒
[记] 来自法语「跳跃」。比 deep fry 用油少、火快。菜单常写 sautéed vegetables

**blanch**

v. 焯水
[义] To put food briefly in boiling water, then often into cold water, to soften or preserve colour. 食物短暂入沸水，常再入冷水，以软化或保色。规则动词。
[形] blanched / blanched / blanching
[例] ① Blanch the green beans for two minutes, then plunge them into ice water. 四季豆焯水两分钟再浸入冰水。 ② Blanching tomatoes makes the skins easy to peel. 番茄焯水后更好去皮。
[搭] blanch vegetables 蔬菜焯水, blanch briefly 短暂焯水, blanch and shock 焯水后过冷, blanch tomatoes 番茄焯水, blanch almonds 杏仁焯水
[联] boil 煮, water 水, vegetable 蔬菜, ice 冰, peel 削皮, colour 保色, soft 软化, brief 短暂, green bean 四季豆, prepare 备菜
[记] 规则变化。blanch 亦指「（因惊）脸发白」，与烹饪无关。shock in ice water 是专业术语

[搭] metal colander 金属漏盆, drain in a colander 在漏盆沥干, pasta colander 意面漏盆, plastic colander 塑料漏盆, kitchen colander 厨房漏盆

n. 枫糖浆
[义] A sweet thick liquid from maple trees, poured on pancakes and waffles. 枫树采制的甜稠液体，淋在薄饼与华夫饼上（pure maple syrup 纯枫糖浆，maple syrup on pancakes 薄饼枫糖浆，Canadian maple syrup 加拿大枫糖浆）。不可数。
[例] ① Pancakes without maple syrup feel incomplete to many Canadians. 许多加拿大人觉得薄饼没枫糖浆就不完整。 ② Use real maple syrup, not flavoured corn syrup. 用真枫糖浆，别用调味玉米糖浆。
[搭] pure maple syrup 纯枫糖浆, maple syrup on pancakes 薄饼枫糖浆, Canadian maple syrup 加拿大枫糖浆, drizzle maple syrup 淋枫糖浆, warm maple syrup 温枫糖浆
[联] pancake 薄饼, waffle 华夫饼, sweet 甜的, Canadian 加拿大的, breakfast 早餐, pour 淋, tree 树, sugar 糖, drizzle 淋, syrup 糖浆

**carbohydrate**
英/美 /ˌkɑːbəʊˈhaɪdreɪt/
n. 碳水化合物
[义] A nutrient in foods such as bread, rice, and sugar that provides energy. 面包、米饭、糖等食物中的营养素，供能（complex carbohydrate 复合碳水，low-carb 低碳水，carbohydrate intake 碳水摄入）。可数/不可数。
[例] ① Athletes often watch their carbohydrate intake before a race. 运动员赛前常关注碳水摄入。 ② Not all carbohydrates are equal; whole grains are healthier. 碳水并非都一样，全谷物更健康。
[搭] complex carbohydrate 复合碳水, low-carb 低碳水, carbohydrate intake 碳水摄入, simple carbohydrate 简单碳水, cut carbs 减少碳水
[联] protein 蛋白质, fat 脂肪, energy 能量, bread 面包, rice 米饭, sugar 糖, diet 饮食, grain 谷物, nutrition 营养, calorie 卡路里
[记] 可数/可数集合。口语 carbs。low-carb diet 近二十年极流行

**sodium**
英/美 /ˈsəʊdiəm/
n. 钠
[义] A mineral found in salt, important in small amounts but harmful in excess. 盐中含有的矿物质，少量必要、过量有害（sodium content 钠含量，low-sodium 低钠，sodium intake 钠摄入）。不可数。
[例] ① Check the sodium content on canned soup labels. 看罐头汤标签上的钠含量。 ② A low-sodium diet helps some people manage blood pressure. 低钠饮食有助部分人控血压。
[搭] sodium content 钠含量, low-sodium 低钠, sodium intake 钠摄入, reduce sodium 减钠, high sodium 高钠
[联] salt 盐, label 标签, health 健康, blood pressure 血压, canned 罐装的, diet 饮食, excess 过量, mineral 矿物质, soup 汤, processed 加工食品
[记] 不可数。表上常写 Na。processed food 往往 sodium 偏高
